### [1] [Disordered hyperuniform modulated phases and the cosmic web from one free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11988)
*Fausto Martelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无可调耦合的自由能泛函（含相场晶体有序项与牛顿尾项），其两种动力学极限分别生成迷宫型III类无序超均匀态与宇宙网结构，且两种结构中长波涨落的抑制均继承自初始条件而非由动力学产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀性跨越25个数量级，从软凝聚态物质到宇宙物质分布。作者希望用一个统一的自由能泛函框架，在无需可调耦合参数的条件下，同时刻画并联系这两类截然不同的超均匀结构，揭示其共同起源。

Method: 构造一个无可调耦合的自由能泛函，包含相场晶体(PFC)有序项与牛顿(Newtonian)尾项；分别在其两种动力学极限下求解：(1) 守恒过阻尼动力学；(2) 膨胀背景下的惯性动力学。并分析初始条件与动力学在长波涨落抑制中的作用，以及有序调制的平均场反作用。

Result: 守恒过阻尼动力学下形成双连续迷宫结构（III类无序超均匀态），见于受限流体、嵌段共聚物、活性物质与植被格局；惯性动力学在膨胀背景下生成宇宙网，其潮汐骨架与纯引力结果各形态类误差<0.03。两种极限下长波涨落抑制均继承自初始条件：冻结态大尺度指数等于原初指数；引力将红外谱按线性增长因子重标定，在增长因子1363范围内精度优于1%。

Conclusion: 两种结构在动力学上连续相连，其"距离"可测量：当有序调制饱和后，平均场反作用给介质留下正比于λ₀²的剩余压力，以平滑因子抑制最大尺度增长，并在冷物质极限λ₀→0时消失。该工作表明无序超均匀性可跨尺度统一描述，且长波抑制源于初始条件。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniformity spans twenty-five orders of magnitude, from the microscopic world of soft and condensed matter to the distribution of matter in the universe. We introduce a free-energy functional with no adjustable coupling, a phase-field-crystal ordering term and a Newtonian tail, and show that its two kinetic limits generate two of these structures. Under conserved overdamped dynamics it arrests in a bicontinuous labyrinth of the kind found in confined fluids, block copolymers, active matter and vegetation patterns, which is a class III disordered hyperuniform state; under inertial dynamics in an expanding background it generates a cosmic web whose tidal skeleton matches that of gravity alone to within $0.03$ in every morphological class. In both limits the suppressed long-wavelength fluctuations are inherited from the initial condition rather than made by the dynamics: the large-scale exponent of the arrested state equals the primordial one, and gravity rescales the infrared spectrum by the linear growth factor to better than one per cent over a factor $1363$ of growth. The two sectors are continuously connected, and their distance is measurable: once the ordering modulation saturates, its mean-field back-reaction leaves the medium with a residual pressure proportional to $λ_0^2$ that suppresses the growth of the largest scales by a smooth factor and vanishes in the cold-matter limit $λ_0\to0$.

</details>


### [2] [Models and Measures of Statistical Physics and Sociophysics for Fracture Mechanics and Earthquake Dynamics: An Introduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12109)
*Sudip Sarkar,Soumyajyoti Biswas,Bikas K. Chakrabarti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是一篇入门性综述，旨在填补凝聚态物理与统计物理研究生教材中关于材料断裂模型与地震动力学模型及其性质的空白，向更广泛的读者介绍这些领域的基本模型、历史脉络与当前前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料断裂与地震破坏自古以来备受关注，达·芬奇约1500年发现名义相同铁丝的拉伸强度随长度急剧下降，揭示了无序材料断裂性质具有极端（非自平均）统计特征。尽管从Griffith裂纹成核理论、Peirce最弱链纤维束模型到Burridge-Knopoff地震模型已有重要进展，但凝聚态物理与统计物理的研究生教材却未纳入这些基本模型及其性质。近年来计算机科学家与社会科学家的加入进一步推动了该领域发展。

Method: 综述方式：梳理断裂力学与地震动力学建模的历史发展脉络（自达·芬奇观察到Griffith、Peirce、Burridge-Knopoff等模型），介绍相关统计物理模型及其性质，并引入跨学科（计算机科学、社会科学）中用于前兆失稳雪崩统计的新型不等式（不平等）测度。

Result: 指出无序材料弹性在热力学极限下仍可由自平均统计描述，而其断裂性质却呈现非自平均的极端统计；断裂力学与地震动力学虽已有精确建模与刻画，但未被纳入标准研究生教材；当前研究前沿在于结合社会不平等测度探索大崩塌或破坏的前兆不平等指标。

Conclusion: 本综述面向更广泛读者，旨在系统引入材料断裂与地震动力学的基本模型及其性质，并将其引向该领域的当前研究前沿。

Abstract: The breaking or fracture of materials and damages or devastations due to earthquakes have caught our attention since the earliest stage of human civilization. In fact, at around 1500, Leonardo Da Vinci made the pioneering observation that, unlike elastic constants (formalized later by Hooke in 1678) of the materials, the tensile (breaking) strengths of nominally identical iron wires decrease drastically with their length, indicating the vanishing breaking strength of materials in the large size limit, suggesting the impossibility of constructing arbitrarily large structures. This was the first observation that unlike the elasticity of disordered materials, which remains defined in the thermodynamic limit (self averaging statistics), the breaking properties of the disordered materials have extreme (non self averaging) statistics. In spite of major and some precise developments in statistical physical modelling and characterizing the fracture mechanics (starting with Allan Griffith's crack nucleation theory in 1921 and the weakest link failure Fibre Bundle Model of Frederick Thomas Peirce in 1926) and of earthquake dynamics (starting with Robert Burridge and Leon Knopoff's continuum dynamical model in 1967 and later other discrete or lattice train models or fractal overlap models of stick slip earthquakes), no graduate level textbook in condensed matter physics or in statistical physics introduces the basic models and their properties. And now computer scientists and social scientists (coming with their social inequality measures applied to the avalanche statistics in precursor failures) are joining their efforts to explore the new precursory inequality measures for big avalanches or failures. This introductory review is designed to fill this gap with a view to introducing these to this wider audience and to take them to the current frontiers in these fields.

</details>


### [3] [Certifying Hidden Dissipation from Observed Current Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12598)
*Ahmed Roman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过观测到的跃迁电流的均值与涨落，可在不知道速率、也看不到隐藏跃迁的情况下，认证驱动酶或马达的总耗散；当被观测跃迁张成网络所有循环时，观测电流协方差可恢复完整的密度收缩电流几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单分子实验只能分辨驱动酶/马达的部分跃迁，但人们希望知道包括隐藏步骤在内的总自由能耗散；现有电流涨落不确定关系只能给出耗散下界，无法说明部分观测何时能确定隐藏贡献。

Method: 将被观测跃迁上的电流均值与涨落作为认证量；利用涨落由动力学活性（traffic，即电流的对称伙伴）决定这一机制，对经验电流的大偏差代价在密度涨落方向做收缩，把观测电流协方差与一个traffic加权的度量等同起来，从而把部分观测问题转化为在未观测循环上的最小能量补全问题，并推导循环可观测性条件。

Result: 当被观测跃迁张成网络循环时，观测电流协方差恢复完整的密度收缩二次电流几何，包含从未被看见的跃迁的贡献；最小能量补全问题的解即为被认证的隐藏耗散；所推导的循环可观测性条件给出了部分观测确定隐藏贡献的判据，弥补了已有不确定关系的不足。

Conclusion: 部分观测下隐藏耗散可由观测电流的均值与涨落被认证，无需跃迁速率与隐藏跃迁信息；但结论针对长时间均值与涨落，有限时间估计需另行误差控制。

Abstract: A single-molecule experiment on a driven enzyme or motor resolves only a few of the transitions the machine makes, yet one would like to know how much free energy it dissipates in total, including on the steps that stay hidden. The mean and the fluctuations of the currents on the watched transitions are shown to certify this hidden dissipation, with no knowledge of the transition rates and no access to the hidden transitions: when the watched transitions span the cycles of the network, the observed-current covariance recovers the full density-contracted quadratic current geometry, including the contribution of transitions that are never seen. The mechanism is that fluctuations are set by the dynamical activity, or traffic, the symmetric partner of the current. Contracting the large-deviation cost of empirical currents over density fluctuations identifies the observed-current covariance with a traffic-weighted metric, and turns partial observation into a minimum-energy completion problem over the unseen cycles, whose solution is the certified hidden cost. Existing current-fluctuation uncertainty relations bound dissipation from chosen currents but do not say when partial observation fixes the hidden contribution; the cycle-observability condition derived here does. The statements concern long-time means and fluctuations; finite-time estimates require separate error control.

</details>


### [4] [Slow activity decay in excitable models with discontinuous phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12862)
*Paulo H. Lorenzoni,Géza Ódor,Silvio C. Ferreira,Róbert Juhász*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在存在阈值激活条件（需多于一个活跃邻居）的阈值接触过程中，两相共存区且初始活性足够低时会出现比指数更慢的全局密度衰减，源于自发形成的缓慢消失、互不连通的活性团簇；该现象在规则格点和有限维随机格点（如Voronoi-Delaunay网络）上普遍存在，但在小世界网络（随机规则网络）上被抑制；当序参量呈幂律衰减时，唯象理论指出衰减指数需超过1/2才能使衰减态对无界成核稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为相互作用可激发自由度系统建立一个简单模型，并探究在无淬火无序情况下是否也能出现偏离临界却具有无标度特征的慢衰减动力学，从而提供除Griffiths效应之外的另一种机制。

Method: 采用阈值接触过程模型（激活事件需多于一个活跃邻居），结合数值模拟与唯象理论，在多种格点和网络上（规则格点、Voronoi-Delaunay网络、随机规则网络）研究全局密度的衰减行为，并通过幂律衰减假设分析衰减态对无界成核的稳定性条件。

Result: 在两相共存区且初始活性足够低时，出现比指数更慢的全局密度衰减，由自发形成的缓慢消失且互不连通的活性团簇引起；该慢衰减现象在规则格点和有限维随机格点（如Voronoi-Delaunay网络）上普遍出现，但被小世界性质抑制（随机规则网络模拟证实）；在二维规则格点等情形下序参量呈幂律衰减时，唯象理论表明只有当衰减指数超过1/2时衰减态才对无界成核稳定。

Conclusion: 该慢衰减现象根源于阈值条件，提供了一种在无无序情况下也可发生的偏离临界但无标度动力学的新机制，区别于淬火随机系统著名的Griffiths效应。

Abstract: As a simple model of interacting systems of excitable degrees of freedom, we consider a threshold contact process on various lattices and networks, in which a successful activation event requires the presence of more than one active neighbors. We show by combining numerical simulations and a phenomenological theory that, in the two-phase coexistence region and for a sufficiently low initial activity, a slower-than-exponential temporal decay of the global density emerges, caused by the spontaneous formation of slowly vanishing and non-communicating clusters of activity. This slow-decay phenomenon is found to appear generally for regular lattices and also for finite-dimensional random lattices such as the Voronoi-Delaunay network. However, the slow-decay is impeded by small-world property, as demonstrated by simulations on random-regular networks. In the case of a power-law decay of the order parameter, which is valid among others on two-dimensional regular lattices, our phenomenological theory points out that the decaying state is stable against unbounded nucleation only if the decay exponent exceeds $1/2$. Besides the well-known Griffiths effects of quenched random systems, this slow-decay phenomenon rooted in the threshold condition provides an alternative mechanism of off-critical but scale-free dynamics, occurring also in the absence of disorder.

</details>

### [5] [Fundamental Dynamical Units for Physics-Informed Structural Inference from Perturbation Time-Series in Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11934)
*Nima Nouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以基本动力学单元（FDUs）为核心的结构化推断框架，将符号三节点交互模式作为可组合基元，结合物理信息神经常微分方程，实现网络动力系统中交互结构的可解释恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络动力系统中，识别带符号的交互结构是核心机制问题，但面临三大耦合障碍：交互架构的组合爆炸、有限干预下的因果归因歧义，以及状态依赖动力学对结构推断的干扰。作者认为这些障碍本质上是结构性的，因此需要结构性的解决方案。

Method: 采用还原论思路，引入基本动力学单元（FDUs）——带符号的三节点交互模式作为可组合基元，将交互假设空间转化为有限、可构造且可处理的表示；基于FDUs证明局部交互结构决定了解耦直接与中继影响所需的扰动条件，从而把干预设计变为FDU表示的结构性推论；并将FDU正则化的结构推断嵌入物理信息神经常微分方程中，以控制方程约束将结构假设转化为可验证的动力学预测，实现交互结构与扰动轨迹的联合恢复。

Result: 在具有已知真值的合成基准上验证了该框架，表明其能够支持结构承诺，并通过FDU基元、基序规定的干预设计和物理信息学习三方面体现。

Conclusion: 该工作将结构承诺作为网络动力系统中机制可解释推断的原则性基础，为交互结构识别提供了有限、可构造且物理约束的建模范式。

Abstract: In networked dynamical systems, the parameter of primary mechanistic interest is signed interaction structure. Recovering this structure from perturbation time-series data is a fundamental identification problem, compounded by three coupled obstacles: the combinatorial complexity of interaction architectures, ambiguity of causal attribution under limited interventions, and state-dependent dynamics that confound structural inference. Each obstacle is structural in origin and calls for a structural solution. We address these challenges by adopting a reductionist approach, introducing Fundamental Dynamical Units (FDUs): signed three-node interaction patterns as composable primitives that convert the interaction hypothesis space into a finite, constructive, and tractable representation. We show that local interaction structure determines the perturbation conditions required to disentangle direct from relayed influence, making intervention design a structural consequence of the FDU representation. We embed FDU-regularized structural inference within a physics-informed neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) whose governing-equation constraint transforms structural hypotheses into verifiable dynamical predictions, enabling joint recovery of interaction structure and perturbation-resolved trajectories. Validated on synthetic benchmarks with known ground truth, the framework supports structural commitment, expressed through FDU primitives, motif-prescribed intervention design, and physics-informed learning, as a principled basis for mechanistically interpretable inference in networked dynamical systems.

</details>


### [6] [Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction: Embedding PDE Consistency into Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11935)
*Michael Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息保形预测PI-CP，利用PDE残差构建非一致性分数，实现分布无关且空间自适应的不确定性估计，并揭示FNO平移等变性对Dirichlet边界条件问题的近似壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子（如FNO）在求解PDE时精度高，但缺乏严格的不确定性量化。同时，FNO的平移等变性在Dirichlet边界条件下可能构成近似障碍。

Method: 将PDE残差嵌入split conformal prediction的非一致性分数中，构建PI-CP框架；并从理论上分析FNO平移等变性的近似壁垒，提出坐标通道作为解决方案。

Result: PI-CP在六类物理场景（热传导2D/3D、结构力学2D/3D、Darcy流、Navier-Stokes）中实现89-91%的覆盖率，而MC Dropout和Deep Ensembles不稳定（82-100%）；坐标通道带来最高63倍误差降低；FNO比CNN和DeepONet好10-12倍。

Conclusion: PI-CP提供了具有可证明覆盖保证的分布无关、空间自适应预测区间，坐标通道可突破FNO在Dirichlet边界下的近似壁垒，验证了物理信息保形预测的有效性。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) achieve remarkable accuracy in approximating solutions to partial differential equations (PDEs). However, providing rigorous uncertainty estimates remains an open challenge. We propose Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction (PI-CP), a framework that embeds PDE residuals into the nonconformity score of split conformal prediction, producing prediction intervals that are (i) distribution-free with provable coverage guarantees, and (ii) spatially adaptive when the PDE residual correlates with prediction error -- tighter where physics is well-satisfied, wider where it is violated. Additionally, we prove that FNO's translation equivariance creates a fundamental approximation barrier for PDEs with Dirichlet boundary conditions, and show that coordinate channels resolve this with up to 63x error reduction. We validate PI-CP across six physics scenarios -- heat conduction (2D/3D), structural mechanics (2D/3D), Darcy flow, and Navier-Stokes -- demonstrating consistent 89-91% coverage for all four Conformal methods, while MC Dropout and Deep Ensembles are unstable (82-100%). FNO outperforms CNN and DeepONet by 10-12x.

</details>


### [7] [Fed-Equilibrium Framework for Topological Pareto Control in Robust and Fair Clinical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11937)
*Ting Xu,Henry Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fed-Equilibrium框架，通过两阶段梯度控制级联（余弦相似度过滤+拓扑帕累托拐点调制），在联邦学习多中心临床网络中缓解"知识主导"，使占比不足3%的少数节点达到与数据丰富中心相当的收敛效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在多中心临床网络部署时面临"知识主导"问题：高数据量中心自然压制少数社区节点，将其独特的临床模式隐式视为异常值。现有几何防御虽提供安全基线，却无法解决效率—公平性困境。

Method: Fed-Equilibrium采用顺序式架构协同的两阶段梯度控制级联：阶段I（几何质量保证）通过余弦相似度漏斗强制方向一致性，过滤恶意噪声，构建稳定流形；阶段II（拓扑帕累托控制）通过识别最优帕累托拐点，对已验证的贡献进行主动调制。验证采用加拿大CNODES与美国SyntheticMass注册库的双国模拟。

Result: 实验表明系统在抵御对抗性发散的同时兼顾了代表性不足的信号。值得注意的是，数据量占比不足3%的少数美国分支节点达到了与数据丰富的加拿大中心相当的深度收敛。

Conclusion: Fed-Equilibrium有效对抗"知识主导"，建立了真正的"知识公地"——全局泛化能力不再以牺牲本地临床代表性为代价。

Abstract: The deployment of Federated Learning (FL) in multi-center clinical networks faces the challenge of "knowledge dominance," where high-volume hubs naturally overwhelm minority community nodes, implicitly treating the distinct clinical patterns of smaller cohorts as outliers. Existing geometric defenses provide a security baseline but leave this efficiency-fairness dilemma unresolved. To bridge this gap, we propose Fed-Equilibrium, a framework that advances the paradigm from simple defense to topological equilibrium. Unlike traditional aggregators, Fed-Equilibrium implements a sequential architectural synergy. It utilizes a two-stage gradient control cascade: Stage I (geometric quality assurance) enforces directional consistency via a cosine similarity funnel to filter malicious noise, creating a stabilized manifold; Stage II (topological Pareto control) then actively modulates verified contributions by identifying the optimal Pareto knee point. We validated this framework on a bi-national simulation integrating Canadian (CNODES) and U.S. (SyntheticMass) registries. Experimental results demonstrate that the system simultaneously secures the network against adversarial divergence while accommodating underrepresented signals. Notably, the minority U.S. spoke (representing less than 3% of data volume) achieved deep convergence comparable to the data-rich Canadian hub. This confirms that Fed-Equilibrium effectively counters "knowledge dominance," establishing a true "knowledge commons" where global generalizability does not come at the cost of local clinical representation.

</details>


### [8] [Efficient AI Model Deployment Using Quantization Analysis Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11954)
*Dwith Chenna,Kanishka Macherla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantization Analysis Tool，一个基于 ONNX 的实用量化分析系统，通过逐层敏感度分析和权重/激活分布可视化，指导精度选择，在保持高精度的同时减小模型体积与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型日益部署于资源受限的边缘与低功耗设备，亟需在保持高精度的前提下降低模型体积与计算成本的优化手段。量化是关键方向，但如何选择精度、识别敏感层缺乏实用工具支持。

Method: 构建于 ONNX 框架之上以保证广泛互操作性，提供逐层敏感度分析、权重与激活分布可视化，并给出精度选择建议；通过识别对降精度鲁棒或敏感的层，辅助开发者在模型体积、延迟与精度之间进行权衡。

Result: 在多种神经网络架构上的实验评估表明，该工具有效提升了量化后的精度，改善了实际部署场景中的效率，并为开发者提供了关于量化对模型及精度影响的有价值洞察。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了工具的能力与实际应用价值，强调其在通过稳健的量化分析实现高效 AI 模型部署中的作用。

Abstract: As deep learning models are increasingly deployed on resource constrained devices, the demand for efficient model optimization techniques continues to grow. Effective deployment of AI models on edge and low power platforms requires optimization methods that reduce model size and computational cost while maintaining high accuracy. This paper presents Quantization Analysis Tool, a practical system designed to streamline quantization workflows and support performance efficient model deployment. Built on the ONNX framework for broad interoperability, the tool provides detailed layer-wise sensitivity analysis, visualization of weight and activation distributions, and insights to guide precision selection. By identifying layers that are resilient or sensitive to reduced precision, the tool enables developers to make informed trade-offs between model size, latency, and accuracy. Experimental evaluations across multiple neural network architectures demonstrate that the tool effectively improves the quantized accuracy, leading to improved efficiency in real-world deployment scenarios. The tool also provides developers valuable insights into the effects on quantization on the model and its accuracy. This work highlights the tools capabilities, practical applications, and its role in enabling efficient AI model deployment through robust quantization analysis

</details>


### [9] [DCRA: Diffusion-Conditioned Representation Alignment for Robust Time-Series Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11997)
*Wenrui Xu,Anas Enanaa,Keshab K. Parhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DCRA训练框架，将扩散前向过程用作结构化扰动调度器，通过特征级一致性目标对齐不同噪声水平下的表征，在CHB-MIT癫痫检测任务上提升了多噪声条件下的鲁棒性与低假阳性率下的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序信号（尤其是EEG、ECG等临床信号）在噪声和分布偏移下的鲁棒表征学习仍是难题。现有增强与一致性方法依赖独立采样的扰动，缺乏结构化的扰动轨迹，难以实现连续可控的表征演化。

Method: DCRA将扩散前向过程重新用作结构化扰动调度器，构建跨噪声水平连续可控的扰动轨迹；引入特征级一致性目标，在保持类别判别结构的同时对齐不同噪声水平下的表征，促成潜在空间中平滑且语义连贯的特征轨迹。框架与编码器无关，可接入状态空间模型与Transformer架构。

Result: 在CHB-MIT EEG数据集的癫痫检测实验中，DCRA在多种噪声条件下持续提升性能，并在低假阳性率下取得更高敏感度；分析表明其表征比基线模型和纯扩散模型更均衡、更具结构。

Conclusion: 结构化扰动与表征对齐相结合，对鲁棒时序学习具有显著益处。

Abstract: Learning robust representations for time-series signals under noise and distribution shifts remains challenging, especially in clinical applications such as electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. We propose Diffusion-Conditioned Representation Alignment (DCRA), a training framework that repurposes the forward diffusion process as a structured corruption scheduler for representation learning. Different from conventional augmentation and consistency-based methods that rely on independently sampled perturbations, DCRA introduces a structured corruption trajectory via the diffusion forward process, which enables continuous and controlled representation evolution across noise levels. We introduce a feature-level consistency objective that aligns representations across noise levels while preserving class-discriminative structure. This mechanism promotes structure-preserving consistency, which enables smooth and semantically coherent feature trajectories in latent space. The proposed framework is encoder-agnostic and can be integrated with state space models and Transformer architectures. The seizure detection experiments on the CHB-MIT EEG dataset show that DCRA consistently improves performance under multiple noise conditions and achieves higher sensitivity at low false-positive rates. Analysis reveals that DCRA produces more balanced and structured representations compared to baseline and diffusion-only models. These findings highlight the benefit of combining structured corruption with representation alignment for robust time-series learning.

</details>


### [10] [Look Before You Leap: Pre-Action Verification for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11957)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"动作生效前的廉价确定性检查"作为智能体监督手段,并在shell命令与代码编辑两种模态上验证其能有效将静默失败转为可恢复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的错误动作常不报错而是静默产生看似合理但错误的副作用,现有监督机制缺乏对此类静默失败的直接度量与拦截手段。

Method: 在同一框架下研究两类动作模态:1)对shell命令构建静态验证器(含语法、二进制、flag检查),基于9930条命令与482个工具;2)对代码编辑建立640处编辑、224个文件的基准,隔离apply步骤,区分内容锚定格式与位置锚定格式;并采用"不确定即拒绝"策略及anchor-and-verify应用器。

Result: shell静态验证器以10.0%假阳性率捕获95.8%无效命令,语法与二进制检查oracle精确(零假阳性,捕获半数错误),flag检查受help文本覆盖限制且贡献全部假阳性;代码编辑中内容锚定格式干净失败,位置锚定格式静默失败(一行偏移致99.1%文件损坏,函数名编辑12.7%命中错误函数);selective grounding达0.958召回、7.0%假阳性,anchor-and-verify在8320次试验中仅1次静默误应用(0.01%)。

Conclusion: 在执行前通过构造固定动作的正确效果,可让验证器选择弃权而非猜测,从而直接度量静默失败;确定性预检查是有效且尚未充分利用的智能体监督形式。作者开源两个基准、验证器与防护组件。

Abstract: An LLM agent acts on the world by emitting actions: shell commands to run, edits to apply. A wrong action does not always fail loudly; it can fail silently, producing a plausible but incorrect effect that raises no error. We argue that a cheap deterministic check, run before an action takes effect, is an effective and underused form of agent oversight, and we study it across two action modalities in one framework. The idea is to fix an action's correct effect by construction, before any executor runs, so that silent failure is measured directly and the verifier may abstain rather than guess. For shell commands, a static verifier over 9930 commands and 482 tools catches 95.8% of invalid commands at a 10.0% false-positive rate. Its syntax and binary checks are oracle-exact, giving zero false positives while catching half of all errors; the flag check is bounded only by help-text coverage and accounts for every false positive. For code edits, a benchmark of 640 edits over 224 files isolating the apply step exposes a sharp split. Content-anchored formats such as search/replace and diff fail cleanly, whereas location-anchored formats fail silently: line numbers corrupt 99.1% of files under a one-line shift, and function-name edits hit the wrong function 12.7% of the time. In both settings a refuse-when-unsure policy turns silent failures into recoverable ones at a tunable cost in applicability: selective grounding reaches 0.958 recall at 7.0% false positives, and an anchor-and-verify applier records one silent misapplication in 8320 trials (0.01%). We release both benchmarks, the verifiers, and the guards.

</details>


### [11] [Decoding Mixture Perception through Computational Modeling of Component Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11958)
*Fei Wang,Xiaoya Xie,Junfei Liu,Huihao Wang,Yixiao Wang,Yintao Wang,Yi Li,Hao Dong,Xing Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仿生深度学习框架，通过构建分子-受体神经响应曲线并进行多受体与多分子曲线融合，实现混合物气味感知的高精度识别，准确率达92.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅觉在人类进化与文明中具有不可替代的作用，至今仍是危险识别、情绪体验和记忆形成的关键通道。但自然界物质多为多分子混合物，其组成复杂，加之浓度依赖的饱和效应与受体特异性激活阈值，使得混合物嗅觉特征识别面临巨大挑战。

Method: 1) 稳健构建分子-受体相互作用的神经响应曲线；2) 设计融合策略，将注意力加权的多受体曲线与浓度依赖的多分子曲线相结合，模拟混合物组分的竞争性激活与协同整合；3) 通过比较响应曲线模式的一致性，将分子关联的语义丰富空间知识迁移至混合物感知特征识别；4) 建立从化学混合、神经编码到感知形成的完整计算路径。

Result: 综合评估显示模型具有显著优越性，识别准确率达到92.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作为长期存在的混合物感知难题提供了可推广的解决方案，并可集成至具身认知系统，增强智能体在复杂场景下的感知与交互能力。

Abstract: Olfaction played an indispensable role throughout human evolution and civilization. Even in the contemporary era of advanced technology, olfaction remains a critical channel for person to conduct danger discrimination, emotional experience, and memory formation. However, most substances in nature exist as multi-molecule mixtures. The complexity of mixture compositions, as well as concentration dependent saturation effects and receptor specific activation thresholds, pose substantial challenges in identifying olfactory characteristics. In this study, we proposed a novel bio inspired deep learning framework for accurate odor perception recognition of mixtures. We robustly constructed neural response curves for molecule-receptor interactions, and developed a fusion strategy that integrates attention-weighted multi-receptor curves with concentration-dependent multi-molecule curves, replicating the competitive activation and synergistic integration of mixture components. Furthermore, by comparing the consistency of response curve patterns, the model can transfer knowledge from the semantically rich space of molecular associations to guide recognition of mixture perception characteristics. Therefore, we established a complete computational pathway from chemical blending, neural encoding, to perceptual formation. Finally, we conducted comprehensive evaluation, and results demonstrated exceptional superiority, achieving an accuracy of 92.2%. Consequently, our work provides a generalizable solution to the long standing mixture perception challenge. More importantly, it can be integrated into embodied cognitive systems to enhance the agents perceptual and interactive capabilities in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [12] [Space as an Interventional Invariant: Cross-Modal Predictive Geometry for Stratified Cities and Em-Spaced Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11959)
*Tao Yang,Xuhui Lin,Kunyao Li,Haijiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"空间"定义为干预不变量——在可容许动作下保持局部相容性与未来观测条件律的最小关系结构，提出跨模态预测几何框架，并证明潜在空间可识别至干预群中心化子，进而用层论表示扩展到分层城市系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学、物理、空间认知、城市科学与具身智能等领域对空间结构的处理存在两类局限：一是把空间当作共享的几何容器，二是把空间视为彼此割裂的表征集合。二者均难以解释异质的感觉与城市过程如何在度量或表征不一致时共同揭示同一空间结构。

Method: 提出跨模态预测几何：整合局部状态空间、模态特定的观测映射、动作群胚与规范的预测状态商；给出识别干预性（而非仅观测性）结构的显式因果条件；并利用层值表示扩展至分层城市系统，使几何、物理、移动性、社会与经济各层共存而不被压缩为单一度量。在噪声条件下开展合成实验，评估等变性、预测充分性、和乐性、限制映射恢复、跨尺度一致性与情境饱和。

Result: 在联合点分离、等变性与干预忠实性条件下，潜在空间可识别至干预群的中心化子，从而将表征歧义归约为残余坐标自由度；合成实验对等变性、预测充分性、和乐性、限制映射恢复、跨尺度一致性及情境饱和进行了检验。

Conclusion: 该框架为空间认知、城市科学、具身AI与"em-spaced intelligence"提供了统一且可证伪的基础。

Abstract: Space is a foundational concept across mathematics, physics, spatial cognition, urban science, and embodied intelligence, yet these fields often treat spatial structure either as a shared geometric container or as a collection of disconnected representations. Such approaches struggle to explain how heterogeneous sensory and urban processes can jointly reveal a common spatial structure, particularly when different modalities do not share the same metric or representation. This paper addresses this gap by defining space as an interventional invariant: the minimal relational structure that preserves local compatibility and the conditional laws of future observations under admissible actions. We develop a cross-modal predictive geometry that integrates local state spaces, modality-specific observation maps, an action groupoid, and a canonical predictive-state quotient, with explicit causal conditions for identifying interventional rather than merely observational structure. The key theoretical result shows that, under joint point separation, equivariance, and interventional faithfulness, the latent space is identifiable up to the centraliser of the intervention group, thereby reducing representational ambiguity to residual coordinate freedom. The framework is further extended to stratified urban systems using sheaf-valued representations, allowing geometric, physical, mobility, social, and economic layers to coexist without being reduced to a single metric. Synthetic experiments under noise evaluate equivariance, predictive sufficiency, holonomy, restriction-map recovery, cross-scale consistency, and context saturation. The resulting framework provides a unified and falsifiable foundation for spatial cognition, urban science, embodied AI, and em-spaced intelligence.

</details>


### [13] [Convergence of Stochastic Gradient Methods under Heavy-Tailed Noise and Hölder Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12785)
*Misbah Uz Zaman,Anirbit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在目标函数为 $(L,s)$-Hölder 连续梯度且梯度噪声仅满足有界 $\\alpha$ 阶矩的条件下，系统分析了 SGD、$\\delta$-GClip 与 G-Clip 的收敛性，统一推广了经典非凸随机优化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机梯度方法收敛性依赖 Lipschitz 光滑与有限方差梯度噪声两大假设，而实际中目标常为 Hölder 光滑、噪声呈重尾分布，二者均被违反。因此需在联合放松这两个假设下重新建立非凸随机优化的收敛保证。

Method: 研究非凸随机优化，目标函数具 $(L,s)$-Hölder 连续梯度（$s\\in(0,1]$），梯度噪声仅满足 $\\alpha$ 阶有界矩（$\\alpha\\in(1,2]$）。分别分析标准 SGD、$\\delta$-regularized 梯度裁剪（$\\delta$-GClip）与标准梯度裁剪（G-Clip）三种算法的收敛速率。

Result: 当 $\\alpha\\ge1+s$ 时，标准 SGD 收敛速率 $O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$；$\\delta$-GClip 达 $O(T^{-2s(\\alpha-1)/[(1+s)(2\\alpha-1)]})$。G-Clip 在 $\\alpha\\ge1+s$ 时达到上述速率，在极重尾 $\\alpha<1+s$ 时为 $O(T^{-2s(\\alpha-1)/[(\\alpha-1)+s(2\\alpha-1)]})$，为该区域首个基于随机梯度方法的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 该工作同时放松光滑性与噪声矩假设，给出 SGD、$\\delta$-GClip 与 G-Clip 的统一收敛刻画，并首次覆盖极重尾噪声区域，为理解梯度裁剪在重尾与 Hölder 光滑场景下的作用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Classical convergence guarantees for stochastic gradient methods typically assume Lipschitz-smooth objectives and finite-variance gradient noise, both frequently violated in practice. In contrast, we study nonconvex stochastic optimization under the joint relaxation of these assumptions: objectives with $(L,s)$-Hölder continuous gradients, $s\in(0,1]$, and gradient noise satisfying only a bounded $α$-th moment condition for $α\in(1,2]$. We establish three convergence results. Firstly, that standard SGD converges at rate $O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$ whenever $α\ge1+s$, extending the classical nonconvex SGD rate to heavy-tailed noise and Hölder smoothness simultaneously. Secondly, we analyze $δ$-regularized gradient clipping ($δ$-GClip), a provable trainer of wide and deep nets, and establish a stationarity rate of $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})$ under the same condition. Thirdly, we analyze standard gradient clipping (G-Clip) and show that it recovers the above rate for $α\ge1+s$ while in the very heavy-tailed regime $α<1+s$, it has a convergence rate $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})$ --- the first convergence guarantee in this regime for any stochastic gradient based method.

</details>


### [14] [On-Device Language Models for Privacy-Preserving Stress Prediction: A Multimodal Evaluation on Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11961)
*Ibukunoluwa Soyebo,Alyssa Donawa,Rodrigo Aguilar Barrios,Brice Patchou,Corey E. Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了在移动端资源受限条件下,端侧语言模型(ODLM)用于多模态压力预测的可行性,采用零样本提示,并同时测量预测精度、延迟与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力是心理健康的重要决定因素,也是移动健康干预的关键靶点。端侧语言模型可提供保护隐私的推理且不依赖云端,但其在移动资源约束下用于健康预测的可行性尚缺乏研究。

Method: 使用端侧语言模型进行多模态压力预测,采用零样本提示(zero-shot prompting),在评估预测精度的同时测量延迟与吞吐量,并比较客观传感器特征与主观自我报告的表现。

Result: 平均而言,客观传感器特征略优于主观自我报告;轻量级(参数量小于2B)模型可实现低延迟且资源使用可预测。

Conclusion: 研究结果既揭示了端侧语言模型用于移动心理健康的潜力,也指出了其实际应用中的约束条件。

Abstract: Stress is a pervasive determinant of mental health and a key target for mobile health interventions. On-device language models (ODLMs) offer privacy-preserving inference without cloud dependency, yet their feasibility for health prediction under mobile resource constraints remains underexplored. We evaluate ODLMs for multi-modal stress prediction using zero-shot prompting, measuring predictive accuracy alongside latency and throughput. Our results show that objective sensor features marginally outperform subjective self-reports on average, and that lightweight sub-2B models achieve low latency with predictable resource usage. Our findings highlight both the promise and the practical constraints of ODLMs for mobile mental health.

</details>


### [15] [Dimension-Corrected Hitting Times for Heavy-Tailed Spectral Emergence in Neural Optimizer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12994)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将神经网络权重矩阵重尾谱密度的出现建模为右删失命中时间问题，提出并验证了维度校正的谱间隙定律 τ_HT ≈ CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ，并给出条件性理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾经验谱密度被广泛用作隐式自正则化的诊断指标，但重尾出现所需的步数复杂度（onset time）尚缺乏清晰理解；现有研究多关注稳态谱形状，忽视出现时间的动力学与维度依赖。

Method: 将谱重尾形成形式化为右删失命中时间问题，观测窗口内未达诊断的 run 视为删失数据而非丢弃；在全批量 teacher-student 动力学中进行受控实验（330 个完成 run），用有限命中回归拟合维度校正谱间隙定律，并用右删失对数正态加速失效时间（AFT）模型比较维度校正模型与仅间隙模型；理论上分析线性网络早期损失动力学、Adam 递推与投影奇异基扩散机制。

Result: 有限命中回归支持 (τ_HT≈CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ)，R²=0.683，γ=0.626，ρ=0.772（330 个完成 run）；右删失 AFT 模型偏好维度校正模型，AIC 从 706.62 降至 628.70。理论上证明：线性网络精确早期损失动力学不决定因子谱尾；Adam 递推本身不蕴含谱重分布；投影奇异基扩散蕴含谱尾势收缩，从而给出维度校正的命中时间界。实证上，投影核剖面支持充分扩散机制，Adam 与 AdamW 在测试网格下一致，GD 与 signGD 在相同区间内未达 onset，预训练 Qwen2.5-0.5B 与 Pythia-70M 权重相对匹配高斯零假设呈现非高斯谱尾结构。

Conclusion: 本文给出一个可复现的谱命中时间定律及严格的条件下理论，强调了维度校正在解释重尾出现时间中的作用；但并非断言 Adam 必然从第一性原理生成重尾，其机制解释具有条件性。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed empirical spectral densities of neural-network weight matrices are widely used as diagnostics of implicit self-regularization, but the step complexity of heavy-tail emergence remains poorly understood. We formulate spectral heavy-tail formation as a right-censored hitting-time problem: a run that does not reach a heavy-tail diagnostic within the observation horizon is treated as censored rather than discarded. In controlled full-batch teacher--student dynamics, we find that the first-step spike--bulk gap alone does not explain onset time. Instead, finite-onset regression supports a dimension-corrected spectral-gap law, (τ_{\mathrm{HT}}\approx CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ), with (R^2=0.683), (γ=0.626), and (ρ=0.772) across 330 completed runs. Right-censored lognormal accelerated-failure-time models further favor the dimension-corrected model over a gap-only model, improving AIC from 706.62 to 628.70. Theoretically, we prove that exact early loss dynamics in linear networks do not determine factor spectral tails, that Adam recurrences alone do not imply spectral redistribution, and that projected singular-basis spreading implies contraction of a spectral-tail potential and hence a dimension-corrected hitting-time bound. Empirically, projected-kernel profiles support the sufficient spreading mechanism, Adam and AdamW agree under tested grids, GD and signGD do not reach onset in the same regimes, and real pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B and Pythia-70M transformer weights show non-Gaussian spectral-tail structure relative to matched Gaussian nulls. The result is a reproducible spectral hitting-time law with rigorous conditional theory, not a claim that Adam necessarily generates heavy tails from first principles.

</details>


### [16] [FINESSE: An Agent-Based Simulator and Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Financial Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11993)
*Tyler Farnan,Benjamin Eng,Adam Abate,Xirui Hou,Rizal Fathony,Nam H. Nguyen,Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FINESSE——一个基于智能体的金融事件序列仿真环境，用于生成多流耦合的合成结构化金融数据，并发布FINESSE-Bench基准（含四类任务）及相应基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务的机器学习研究受限于缺乏具有代表性的开源数据集：现有资源通常只聚焦单一模态或单一任务，无法反映金融问题本身具有的结构化、多模态和动态特性。

Method: 构建FINESSE（Financial Event Sequence Simulation Environment）基于智能体的仿真框架，生成由多条相互依赖事件流组成的合成结构化数据集；每条流对应一种金融行为（交易、支付、账户状态变更、政策干预），具有独立动作空间、schema与变量类型，并通过智能体的潜在演化状态相互耦合，从而模拟时间上丰富的交互；基于该仿真器生成FINESSE-Bench基准数据集。

Result: FINESSE-Bench支持四个代表性任务：余额预测、交易欺诈检测、漏缴/逾期还款预测、下一事件预测；采用时间序列预测、事件序列建模、时序图、时序点过程等方法报告了基线结果。

Conclusion: 开源发布FINESSE框架（含仿真器与数据集），以加速金融服务中结构化、多模态事件序列建模挑战的研究。

Abstract: Machine learning research in financial services is limited by the scarcity of representative open-source datasets. Existing resources are often narrowly focused on a single modality or task and fail to reflect the structured, multimodal, and dynamic nature inherent to many problems in financial services.
  In this paper, we introduce FINESSE, a Financial Event Sequence Simulation Environment, an agent-based simulation framework for generating synthetic, structured datasets composed of multiple interdependent event streams. Each stream corresponds to a distinct financial behavior such as transactions, payments, account status changes, and policy interventions, each with unique action spaces, schemas and variable types. These streams are coupled through agents' latent evolving states, enabling the simulation of temporally rich interactions.
  We also introduce FINESSE-Bench, a benchmark dataset generated by the simulator, supporting four representative tasks: balance forecasting, transaction fraud detection, missed payment prediction, and next event prediction. We report baseline results using methods from time series forecasting, event sequence modeling, temporal graphs, and temporal point processes. We release the FINESSE framework, including the simulator and dataset to accelerate research on structured, multimodal event sequence modeling challenges in financial services.

</details>


### [17] [A Full Adam Theorem for Spectral Heavy-Tail Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12996)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在闭合的高斯 Stein-Hermite teacher-student 状态演化模型中，完整证明了 Adam 谱重尾起始（spectral heavy-tail onset）定理，并证明更强的任意梯度 Adam 定理不可能成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 Adam 优化器的谱重尾起始行为提供完整、端到端的理论刻画，弥补从 Adam 动量与分母到谱命中律之间缺乏形式化推导的空缺，并澄清其理论边界。

Method: 从真实全批量 Adam 递推出发，用 Stein-Hermite 微积分求总体梯度，证明有限宽度协方差集中；将多步 Adam 动量转化为精确的非中心高斯符号核；用基均匀化定理控制对角分母；由 Hermite 边缘转移定理导出正则变化的投影更新响应，并通过精确 Gram 更新与近似目标 KL 收缩（配对上下命中界）完成推导。

Result: 最终命中律为 τ_ε=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ} d^ρ log(Ψ_0/ε))，其中 Δ_1 为第一 spike-bulk 谱隙。同时证明更强的任意梯度 Adam 定理不可能，且精确两步线性网络损失动力学无法辨识因子谱或重尾命中时间。

Conclusion: 在闭合状态演化模型内，从 Adam 动量与分母到谱命中律的每一步均已形式化，给出完整的 Adam 谱重尾起始定理，并确立了该结论的紧致性与不可能性边界。

Abstract: We prove a full Adam theorem for spectral heavy-tail onset in a closed Gaussian Stein-Hermite teacher-student state-evolution model. The theorem begins with the actual full-batch Adam recurrences, derives the population gradient by Stein-Hermite calculus, proves finite-width covariance concentration, converts multi-step Adam momentum into an exact non-centered Gaussian sign kernel, controls the diagonal Adam denominator by a basis-homogenization theorem, derives a regularly varying projected update response from a Hermite edge-transfer theorem, pushes the response through the exact Gram update, and proves approximate-target KL contraction with matching upper and lower hitting bounds. The final law is (τ_\varepsilon=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ\log(Ψ_0/\varepsilon))), where (Δ_1) is the first spike-bulk spectral gap. The result is full in the following precise sense: every step from Adam's momentum and denominator to the spectral hitting law is formalized inside the closed state-evolution model. We also prove that a stronger arbitrary-gradient Adam theorem is impossible, and that exact two-step linear-network loss dynamics do not identify factor spectra or heavy-tail hitting times.

</details>


### [18] [Explainable Prediction from Mobile Sensing Data through LLM-guided Concept Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11995)
*Yuning Wang,Iman Azimi,Amir M. Rahmani,Pasi Liljeberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概念集成Transformer（CIT），用LLM生成基线感知的概念异常目标与置信权重，实现小队列移动感知数据的可解释预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小队列健康感知研究中，任务特异性结果监督相对异构感知数据十分有限，准确预测困难；同时模型输出需反映有意义的行为与生理模式，而非仅有预测分数，即需要兼顾准确性与可解释性。

Method: 构建概念集成Transformer（CIT），并结合LLM引导的概念监督：利用预训练大语言模型在无需人工概念标注的情况下，生成基线感知的概念异常目标及其置信权重，将其集成到Transformer预测框架中。

Result: 在两个纵向数据集上，CIT在AFFECT上取得最高F1（0.756），在PHQ-9数据集上并列最高（0.765）。学到的概念分数揭示可解释的行为与生理模式；在AFFECT中，睡眠量与睡眠质量在高、低负性情感组间差异最明显。

Conclusion: LLM引导的概念集成可支持小队列移动感知研究中兼具准确性与可解释性的预测。

Abstract: Mobile sensing enables longitudinal monitoring of behavioral and physiological patterns in everyday settings. However, accurate prediction remains challenging in small-cohort health-sensing studies, where task-specific outcome supervision is limited relative to heterogeneous sensing data. Interpretability is also important, as model outputs should reflect meaningful behavioral and physiological patterns rather than predictive scores alone. We develop a Concept-Integrated Transformer (CIT) with LLM-guided concept supervision for explainable prediction from mobile sensing data. CIT uses a pretrained large language model to generate baseline-aware concept abnormality targets with confidence weights without manual concept annotation. Across two longitudinal datasets, CIT achieves the highest F1 score on AFFECT (0.756) and ties for the highest on a PHQ-9 dataset (0.765). The learned concept scores also reveal interpretable behavioral and physiological patterns; in AFFECT, sleep quantity and quality show the clearest difference between high and low negative affect groups. These findings support LLM-guided concept integration for accurate and interpretable prediction in small-cohort mobile sensing studies.

</details>


### [19] [Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13039)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Pedro Ribeiro,Pedro Saleiro,Pedro Bizarro,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TrED（面向演化域的迁移学习）形式化框架，将域泛化、域适应、多域学习等经典设定统一为数据可用性轨迹上的不同阶段，而非彼此孤立的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习研究被分割为多个孤立子领域（域泛化、域适应、多域学习等），各子领域对目标域数据可用性（数据量与标注量）作出固定假设。但现实部署中数据可用性随时间演化，样本与标签被渐进收集，经典设定只是部署系统需完整经历的轨迹中的一个快照。因此需要一个把整条轨迹本身当作研究对象的统一形式化框架。

Method: 提出TrED（Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains）形式化，由三要素构成：(1) 由环境固定的数据可用性过程；(2) 方法可自由选择的学习协议；(3) 对整条模型轨迹而非单一模型打分的评价准则。在此形式化下，经典设定被还原为学习器可能经过的不同regime（阶段），而非被拼接的独立问题。随后系统梳理迁移学习文献，识别有潜力作为求解组件的机制。

Result: 文献调研发现：大多数方法只针对单一regime定制，缺乏跨阶段的通用性；即便现有最强候选方法也未能优化整条轨迹（whole trajectory）。即TrED目前尚无有效解。

Conclusion: TrED是一个well-posed（良定义）且尚未解决的问题，被视为未来研究的重要方向，需要能贯穿整个演化轨迹统一优化的迁移学习方法。

Abstract: Transfer learning explores how to leverage knowledge from various tasks or domains (sources) to enhance predictive performance in related tasks or domains (targets). Typically, transfer learning research is segmented into several isolated sub-areas (such as domain generalisation, domain adaptation, or multi-domain learning), each making distinct assumptions about target data availability, namely how much data and how many labels are available at training time. However, in many real-world applications, data availability is not fixed but evolves over time, as instances and labels are progressively collected from a new domain. Each of the classical settings then describes only a snapshot of a trajectory that a deployed system must traverse in full. We formalise this trajectory as a transfer learning problem in its own right, Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains (TrED), specified by a data availability process fixed by the environment, a learning protocol that the method is free to choose, and an evaluation criterion that scores the whole trajectory of models rather than a single one. Within this formalism, the classical settings are recovered as regimes that a learner may pass through, rather than as separate problems that TrED concatenates. We then examine the transfer learning literature to identify mechanisms that are promising building blocks for a solution, and find that most methods are tailored to a single regime and that even the strongest existing candidates do not yet optimise the whole trajectory. We argue that TrED is a well-posed and unsolved problem, and an important direction for future research.

</details>


### [20] [Benign Loss Landscapes Can Coexist with Worst-Case Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13057)
*Zach Furman,Stephan Wäldchen,Yangda Bei,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究树张量网络（TTN）的损失景观，证明其能嵌入任意read-once布尔公式、包含多项式规模但梯度下降无法多项式时间学习的难学目标，同时其损失景观对任意可实现在目标条件良性（最小范数局部极小即全局极小），学习困难源于秩亏导致的高阶退化鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络能表达多项式时间可评估但在多项式时间内无法被梯度下降学到的worst-case目标，然而在真实任务上却学得很好，这说明真实目标具有某种非泛化结构。现有代理模型无法提出该问题：深度线性网络缺少难学目标，核方法和无穷宽度极限无法高效评估此类目标。因此需要一个新的模型类来同时具备难学目标与可高效评估的景观分析能力。

Method: 采用树张量网络（TTN）作为模型类，它泛化深度线性网络与Tucker分解。作者证明TTN可嵌入任意read-once布尔公式，并分析其损失景观的良性条件；通过奇偶函数（parity function）案例研究，将景观几何与计算困难性联系起来，识别高阶梯化鞍点及其由秩亏引起的机制。

Result: 证明TTN可嵌入任意read-once布尔公式，因而包含与神经网络相同机制下无法被梯度下降多项式时间学习的多项式规模目标；同时证明对每个可实现目标其损失景观是条件良性（conditionally benign）的：每个最小范数局部极小都是全局极小。故坏局部极小并非TTN中典型问题与worst-case问题的区分因素；学习困难来自高阶退化鞍点，并由秩亏导致。奇偶函数案例进一步说明了TTN将景观几何与计算困难性相关联的潜力。

Conclusion: TTN提供了一个能同时包含难学目标并具备可分析损失景观的代理模型。其结果表明，学习困难性并不源于坏局部极小，而源于由秩亏引起的高阶退化鞍点，从而为用景观几何刻画计算困难性提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are expressive enough to contain worst-case targets that can be evaluated in polynomial time but cannot be learned in polynomial time by gradient descent. For practical tasks they nonetheless learn well, raising the question of what non-generic structure of real-world targets enables this. Existing surrogate models cannot pose this question because they either lack hard-to-learn targets entirely (deep linear networks) or cannot evaluate such targets efficiently (kernel methods, infinite-width limits). We study tree tensor networks (TTNs), a model class that generalizes deep linear networks and Tucker decompositions. We show they embed arbitrary read-once Boolean formulas, and thus contain polynomial-size targets that cannot be learned by gradient descent in polynomial time under the same mechanism as neural networks. Despite this, we prove that their loss landscapes are conditionally benign for every realizable target: every local minimum that is minimum-norm is global. Thus, surprisingly, bad local minima are not what distinguishes between typical and worst-case problems in TTNs. Instead, learning difficulty in TTNs can arise from high-order degenerate saddle points, which we show are caused by rank-deficiency. This is explored through a case study of the parity function, illustrating the potential for TTNs to relate landscape geometry to computational hardness.

</details>


### [21] [Fixed State, Long Reach: What a Constant-Size Cache Buys Block Diffusion at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11998)
*Vaibhav Singh,Pierre-André Noël,Torsten Scholak,Eugene Belilovsky,Oleksiy Ostapenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模上验证：将块级扩散模型与状态空间混合器（Mamba/混合架构）结合，可在保持质量的同时实现 O(1) 的块缓存，显著优于基于注意力的 O(L) 缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型并行解码，但其双向去噪器无法直接使用自回归推理中的 KV 缓存。块扩散通过逐块解码恢复缓存能力，然而现有块缓存依赖注意力机制：内存为 O(L)，且作为免训练改造仅是模型计算的近似。因此需要既能支持高效块缓存、又能使缓存精确的新方案。

Method: 提出配方：使用序列混合器（sequence mixer）将已完成的块摘要为可复用状态以支持块缓存，并设计相应的块因果（block-causal）训练目标使缓存精确。在此配方下预训练三个 3B 块扩散去噪器（注意力、Mamba、混合），在单一前沿目标下训练 300B token，全部通过统一的缓存接口解码。

Result: 仅状态空间缓存的规模与序列长度无关（O(1)）：内存在任意上下文长度下恒定，注意力缓存仍为 O(L)。在 256k token 时，注意力缓存增至 82GB、29 ms/step，而 Mamba 缓存延迟低 4.3 倍、内存少 11 倍、单流吞吐高 2.6 倍；由于内存恒定，可随批大小扩展，聚合吞吐达 14 倍，而注意力在单流之外无法运行。线性状态偏置还让 Mamba 与混合骨干在其训练长度 8–16 倍范围内保持检索能力，注意力检索在 2 倍处即崩溃，且未测到质量损失。

Conclusion: 以状态空间序列混合器替代注意力、配合块因果训练目标，可实现精确且 O(1) 的块缓存，在大规模下同时提升延迟、内存、吞吐与长上下文检索能力，且无质量代价，是块扩散模型高效推理的更优路径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models decode tokens in parallel, but their bidirectional denoiser rules out the naive key--value (KV) cache behind fast autoregressive inference. Block diffusion restores caching by decoding block-by-block, and the block caches deployed on it so far are tied to attention: O(L)in memory and, if used as training-free retrofits, only an approximation of the model's computation. Both constraints can be overcome: sequence mixers that summarize finalized blocks into a reusable state support block caching, and the corresponding block-causal training objective makes the cache exact. We study this recipe at scale, pretraining three 3B block-diffusion denoisers (attention, mamba, and hybrid) on 300B tokens under one single-frontier objective and decoding all three through a single cached interface. Only the state-space cache is O(1) in sequence length: its memory and per-step latency stay constant at any context length, while an attention cache remains O(L). At 256k tokens (where attention has grown to 82GB and 29 ms/step), the Mamba cache delivers 4.3x lower latency, 11x less memory, and 2.6x higher single-stream throughput; and because that footprint is constant it scales with batch as well, reaching 14x the aggregate throughput, where attention cannot run beyond a single stream. The same linear-state bias lets the Mamba and hybrid backbones keep retrieving out to 8-16x their training length, whereas attention's retrieval collapses at 2x, at no measured quality cost.

</details>


### [22] [Can We Trust LLM Judges: A Study of Capability-Dependent Biases and Multi-Judge Ensemble for Bias Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12002)
*Gemma Zhang,Prachi Badarayani,Asmi Kumar,Sadid Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦绝对评分式LLM-as-a-judge，揭示准确率与评判偏差的关系，并提出无需标签的校准加权多数投票（WMV）集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体LLM评判者存在系统性偏差，削弱可靠性；既有研究多聚焦成对比较场景，而绝对评分更贴近真实应用，且"高准确率是否等于公平评价"这一问题尚未被充分检验。

Method: 在4个基准、6个模型（36对judge-examinee）上开展实证分析；提出校准加权多数投票（WMV），依据在线估计的各评判者假阳性/假阴性率进行加权聚合，并引入仅基于评判者间一致性模式的"分歧驱动"误差率估计器，无需真值标签或任务元数据；在任务分布漂移的仿真实验中验证。

Result: 模型任务准确率强预测其评判准确率（多数模型Pearson r≥0.90），并反向预测其方向性偏差（r≤-0.83）；但更胜任的被评模型普遍获得更宽松的评判（r≥0.83）。无标签WMV在分布漂移仿真中平均与拥有完美误差率知识的oracle相差0.5个百分点以内，优于单一评判者与无权多数投票。

Conclusion: 原则性的多评判者校准可在无需标注数据的条件下同时提升准确性并纠正系统性宽松偏差，为随模型能力提升而扩展的可靠自动评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used as automated judges for model training and evaluation, yet individual judges exhibit systematic biases that undermine reliability. Much of prior work has studied biases in pairwise LLM-as-a-judge settings; in this paper, we focus on absolute scoring tasks, which mirror more realistic use cases. Across four benchmarks and six models (36 judge-examinee pairs), we show that a model's task accuracy strongly predicts its judging accuracy (Pearson $r \geq 0.90$ on most models) and inversely predicts its directional bias ($r \leq -0.83$), but that accuracy alone does not ensure fair evaluation: more capable examinee models consistently receive more lenient judgments from all judges ($r \geq 0.83$). To address this, we propose calibrated weighted majority voting (WMV), an ensemble evaluation method that aggregates multiple LLM judges weighted by online estimates of their false-positive and false-negative rates. We introduce a disagreement-based estimator that derives these error rates purely from inter-judge agreement patterns, requiring no ground-truth labels or task metadata. In a simulated experiment with shifting task distributions, our label-free WMV tracks an oracle with perfect error-rate knowledge to within 0.5 percentage points on average, outperforming both individual judges and unweighted majority voting. These results demonstrate that principled multi-judge calibration can simultaneously improve accuracy and correct for systematic leniency without requiring labeled data, offering a scalable path to reliable automated evaluation as model capabilities increase.

</details>


### [23] [QTrans: A Quantum Transformer for Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12011)
*Ren-Xin Zhao,Xinjie Huang,Yahong Liu,Maoyu Ye,Jinjing Shi,Shi Wang,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QTrans，一种基于参数化量子电路的量子-经典混合模型，用于小规模二分类情感分析，通过量子测量间高斯距离构造注意力系数，并在MR、CR、MPQA数据集上取得了优于经典基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小规模二分类情感分类场景中，否定、对比转折和跨词依赖等因素导致情感线索呈非线性耦合，传统轻量级模型难以充分捕捉词元间的上下文关系。

Method: 使用参数化量子电路构造查询（query）、键（key）和值（value）特征，并基于量子测量之间的高斯距离推导注意力系数；进一步结合量子前馈神经网络、残差连接和层归一化，构建端到端可训练的量子-经典混合情感分类框架。

Result: 在MR、CR和MPQA数据集上，QTrans的测试准确率分别为72.13%、69.51%和63.45%，相较各数据集上表现最优的经典基线分别提升2.88、3.17和3.79个百分点。

Conclusion: QTrans拓展了参数化量子电路在轻量级情感分析中的应用，并为后续研究量子多头自注意力建模文本关系奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: In small-scale binary sentiment classification scenarios, factors such as negation, contrastive shifts, and cross-word dependencies lead to the non-linear coupling of sentiment cues, making it difficult for conventional lightweight models to fully capture the contextual relationships between tokens. To address this issue, we propose a model named QTrans, which uses parameterized quantum circuits to construct query, key, and value features and derives attention coefficients from Gaussian distances between quantum measurements. By further integrating a quantum feed-forward neural network, residual connections, and layer normalization, the model establishes an end-to-end trainable quantum-classical hybrid framework for sentiment classification. Experimental results on the MR, CR, and MPQA datasets show that QTrans achieves test accuracies of 72.13\%, 69.51\%, and 63.45\%, respectively, representing improvements of 2.88, 3.17, and 3.79 percentage points over the best-performing classical baselines for each dataset. Overall, QTrans expands the application of parameterized quantum circuits in lightweight sentiment analysis and lays an experimental foundation for further research into quantum multi-head self-attention for modeling textual relationships.

</details>

*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"认证安全筛选"（certified safety curation）框架：在仅有片段比较与偶发回合预算反馈的弱监督下，用"先筛选后克隆"的流水线构建可证明安全的离线RL/模仿学习训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全离线强化学习假设每次转移都有代价函数；本文探究当安全只能通过比较短片段、并偶尔询问某回合是否超预算来判定时，仍能实现何种保证。

Method: 三步流水线：(1) 用仅基于状态的价值函数（由片段比较训练）为整条轨迹打分；(2) 用 Learn-then-Test 校准筛选阈值，从而对所选集合的不安全比例给出分布无关的 (α, δ) 认证界；(3) 对筛选结果做行为克隆。设计由 oracle 对照验证：即便价值函数精确，逐转移重加权也会失败，因此价值函数用于筛选整条轨迹。

Result: 在 15 个 DSRL 任务中的 11 个上策略满足代价预算，仅比克隆真实安全子集（需每条轨迹标签）少一个；未认证变体达到 12 个。将最强全标签方法在认证筛选集上重训后，其安全性得到恢复，而该方法自身任何代价目标设置都无法做到。拒绝（refusal）可预测：证书概率在池所达到的纯度上有闭式表达，由校准样本估计，打分器仅通过该纯度进入。

Conclusion: 在弱监督（片段比较＋偶发预算反馈）下，可对离线RL/模仿学习训练集的组成给出分布无关的安全认证，且筛选整条轨迹优于逐转移重加权。

Abstract: Safe offline reinforcement learning assumes a cost function on every transition. We ask what remains possible when safety can be judged only by comparing short clips and occasionally asking whether an episode exceeded its budget. Certified safety curation answers with a filter-then-clone pipeline: a state-only value trained from segment comparisons scores whole trajectories, Learn-then-Test calibration certifies a selection threshold under a distribution-free $(α, δ)$ bound on the unsafe fraction of the selection, and behavior cloning follows. We are not aware of prior work certifying the composition of a training set for offline RL or imitation. Oracle controls justify the design: reweighting individual transitions fails even with an exact value, so the value selects whole trajectories. The policies satisfy the cost budget on eleven of fifteen DSRL tasks, one short of cloning the ground-truth safe subset, which needs a label on every trajectory; the uncertified variant reaches twelve. Retrained on the certified selection, the strongest full-label method becomes safe where no setting of its own cost target rescues it. Refusal is predictable: the certificate's probability has a closed form in the purity the pool attains, which the calibration sample estimates and the scorer enters only through.

</details>


### [25] [Inverting Self-Triggered Control: Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Sparse Denial-of-Service Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12016)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Tristan Schuler,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自触发强化学习控制（RL-STC）的"最稀疏安全控制调度"问题反转，训练对抗性RL智能体学习最稀疏的干扰/DoS调度以使闭环失稳，并给出植物属性下界与证书级最优性推论，在三个平台上验证学习到的对抗策略优于贪心与周期性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有DoS调度计数预算分析主要针对周期控制与LTI控制器；自触发控制（STC）在Lyapunov契约下如何被最稀疏干扰击溃尚不清楚。本文通过反转RL-STC的"最稀疏安全调度"问题，研究对抗者能否以最少干扰次数使STC闭环崩溃，从而把DoS调度分析推广到STC控制器。

Method: 定义对抗性RL智能体学习最稀疏的干扰/DoS调度，采用镜像防守方安全证书的Lyapunov增性可容许谓词；针对满足Lyapunov契约的STC，证明迫使崩溃所需最小干扰次数的植物属性下界，并作为推论得到count-budget DoS调度中连续分组最优性的证书级类比。实验在Pendulum、CartPole、Quadrotor2D上对每个植物训练四类固定防守方（一个LQR与三个RL-STC）。

Result: 学习到的对抗者是在所有植物上以100%崩溃所有防守方的唯一对抗者；贪心在Quadrotor2D LQR上42%回合失败，周期性在Pendulum LQR上97%回合失败。在每次失败所需干扰时间上，其优于基线最多2.8倍，并在Quadrotor2D LQR上绝对优势最大。鲁棒性消融显示，超过初始状态量级的高斯观测噪声与仅位置观测均保持100%失败率，且在干扰时间/失败指标上仍严格领先两个基线。

Conclusion: 将DoS调度计数预算分析从周期与LTI控制器扩展到STC控制器，证明了最小干扰次数的下界与最优性推论，并表明学习到的稀疏对抗调度在破坏性与鲁棒性上全面优于贪心与周期性基线。

Abstract: Self-triggered reinforcement learning control (RL-STC) learns the sparsest control schedule that preserves Lyapunov-decreasing stability under a Run-Time Assurance (RTA) override. We invert this: an adversarial RL agent learns the sparsest jamming or Denial-of-Service (DoS) schedule that destabilizes the closed loop, with a Lyapunov-increase admissibility predicate mirroring the defender's safety certificate. We prove a plant-property lower bound on the minimum jam count required for an immediate hold-last medium-access-control adversary to force a crash against a self-triggered controller (STC) satisfying a Lyapunov contract, and recover a certificate-level analog of the consecutive-grouping optimality of prior count-budget DoS scheduling as a corollary. This extends the DoS-scheduling count-budget analysis from periodic and linear-time-invariant to STC controllers. Empirically, we train against four fixed defenders per plant (one Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) and three RL-STC) on Pendulum, CartPole, and Quadrotor2D. The learned adversary is the only adversary that crashes every defender on every plant at $100\%$: greedy misses Quadrotor2D LQR on $42\%$ of episodes and periodic misses Pendulum LQR on $97\%$. On jam-time-per-failure it beats baselines by up to $2.8\times$, and shows its widest absolute margin on Quadrotor2D LQR. Robustness ablations show that Gaussian observation noise exceeding the initial-state magnitude and position-only observation both preserve $100\%$ failure rate and keep the learned adversary strictly ahead of both baselines on jam-time-per-failure.

</details>
