*Gyeolhee Lee,Moosun Kim,Taewook Kwon,Jaehun Kim,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真度铁路转向架响应修正方法，将多体仿真作为低保真、滚振台测量作为高保真，结合实验锚定保真分配与物理信息差异学习，在385 km/h留出工况下实现高精度响应预测（平均R²=0.8197）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路工程需在无法穷尽测试的工况下预测车辆响应。与代表性测量一致虽提供关键证据，但仅在有限工况下校准不能保证其他工况的准确性，因此需要一种能借助少量高保真实验数据修正大范围仿真结果的方法。

Method: 提出多保真度转向架响应修正框架：以多体仿真历史为低保真信息、滚振台测量为高保真证据；采用实验锚定的保真度分配与物理信息差异学习，处理多通道转向架响应历史。用时间延迟神经网络（TDNN）表征工况相关的仿真趋势，并以开发集拟合的幅值对齐定义低保真基线；残差修正网络建模基线未解释的可复现响应分量并叠加到基线。引入有效动平衡方程约束所学习的差异，表征仿真与实际系统在惯量、阻尼、刚度与外激励上的差异。训练目标联合该约束、残差匹配、时间平滑及选择性施加的位移-加速度一致性项。

Result: 在评估的重构算例中，修正后响应的平均决定系数为0.8197，平均归一化均方根误差（NRMSE）为4.6055%，平均归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）为1.9297%。

Conclusion: 结果为在留出的385 km/h工况下实现准确响应预测提供了初步证据，表明该方法可将少量滚振台测量有效外推至未测试工况。

Abstract: Railway engineers need simulation models that predict vehicle responses across operating scenarios that cannot be tested exhaustively. Agreement with representative measurements provides essential evidence, but calibration at a limited set of conditions does not guarantee accuracy elsewhere. We present a multifidelity railway-bogie response-correction method that treats multibody simulation histories as low-fidelity information and roller-rig measurements as high-fidelity evidence. This method combines an experiment-anchored fidelity assignment with physics-informed discrepancy learning for multichannel bogie-response histories. A time-delay neural network (TDNN) represents the condition-dependent simulation trend, and development-fitted amplitude alignment defines the low-fidelity baseline. A residual-correction network then models the reproducible response component not explained by this baseline and adds it to the baseline. An effective dynamic-balance equation constrains the learned discrepancy by representing differences in inertia, damping, stiffness, and external forcing between the simulated and physical systems. The training objective combines this constraint with residual matching, temporal smoothness, and selectively applied displacement-acceleration consistency terms. For the evaluated reconstruction case, the corrected response gives a mean coefficient of determination of 0.8197, a mean normalized root-mean-square error (NRMSE) of 4.6055 %, and a mean normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) of 1.9297 %. These results provide initial evidence of accurate response prediction at the held-out 385 km/h condition.

</details>


### [27] [Reinforcement Learning for Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12020)
*John Zhuoyang Ye,Aarav Pabla,Jens Palsberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用强化学习结合重要性采样来自动搜索量子纠错中syndrome提取的实现方案，在所有规模上优于现有方法，逻辑错误率较AlphaSyndrome和PropHunt平均降低25.9%和71.7%，在距离15的表面码上降低达97.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错的关键子任务是提取syndrome以指示错误，而提取方式随syndrome规模指数增长，且不同实现的容错性（以逻辑错误率衡量）差异巨大，因此存在一个自然的搜索问题：寻找低逻辑错误率的实现。既有工作虽能解决该问题，但要么牺牲解的质量，要么牺牲可扩展性。

Method: 采用强化学习与重要性采样相结合的方法，用于自动搜索syndrome提取的实现方案，以在保证解质量和可扩展性的同时降低逻辑错误率。

Result: 与当前最先进的自动调度工具AlphaSyndrome和PropHunt相比，本文工具的平均逻辑错误率分别降低25.9%和71.7%，在距离15的表面码上降幅达到97.8%。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合重要性采样可在所有规模上超越此前工作，显著降低逻辑错误率，兼顾解质量与可扩展性。

Abstract: A key subtask of quantum error correction is to extract a syndrome that, if nontrivial, signals an error. The number of possible ways to extract a syndrome grows exponentially with the syndrome size, and these implementations vary greatly in fault tolerance, as measured by their logical error rates. This creates a natural search problem: find an implementation with a low logical error rate. Previous work solves this problem but sacrifices either solution quality or scalability. In this paper, we use reinforcement learning and importance sampling to outperform previous work at all scales. Compared with the state of the art automatic scheduling tools AlphaSyndrome and PropHunt, our tool reduces the logical error rate by 25.9\% and 71.7\% on average, respectively, culminating with a reduction of 97.8\% for a surface code with distance 15.

</details>


### [28] [Scalable Discrete-to-Continuous Channel Simulation for Compression and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12067)
*Joseph Rowan,Buu Phan,Ashish J. Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离散到连续信道的信道仿真方案，使用固定数量的随机样本，运行时间与信道和输入无关，并通过极化和多级编码实现O(n log n)复杂度，应用于变速率压缩和差分隐私分布式均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用信道仿真算法存在计算成本高、随机停止时间，甚至在最坏情况下需要生成无限多个共享随机样本的问题。作者希望设计一种使用固定数量随机样本、运行时间与信道及输入无关的信道仿真方案。

Method: 针对离散到连续信道，提出精确与近似仿真方案：从每个潜在目标分布生成一个样本（或固定数量的样本），对样本施加潜在置换（latent permutation），再通过指数竞赛（exponential race）进行样本选择。该方案在生成样本数量与压缩率之间提供灵活权衡。利用极化和多级编码将方法扩展到长块长度，时间复杂度为O(n log n)。

Result: 该方案使用固定数量的随机样本，运行时独立于信道和输入，并可在样本数与压缩率间灵活权衡；结合极化与多级编码后，能以O(n log n)时间处理长块长度，从而降低每符号开销。在随机VQ-VAE变速率压缩和通过高斯机制精确仿真的通信高效差分隐私分布式均值估计中展示了应用效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、参数可控的信道仿真框架，克服了传统方法随机停止时间和无限样本的问题，并成功应用于变速率压缩与差分隐私分布式均值估计等实际场景。

Abstract: Channel simulation has recently emerged as a useful component in machine learning systems where samples from a prescribed probability distribution are to be compressed. Yet, general channel simulation algorithms often suffer from high computational costs, random stopping times or, in the worst case, can require generating an infinite number of shared random samples. We introduce a scheme for both exact and approximate simulation of discrete-to-continuous channels which conversely uses a fixed number of random samples, and therefore has a runtime independent of the channel and the input. Unlike existing channel simulation schemes which generate a sequence of independent samples from a proposal distribution, our approach generates one sample, or alternatively a fixed number of samples, from each potential target distribution. We then apply a latent permutation to the samples before performing sample selection using an exponential race. Our scheme provides a flexible tradeoff between the number of generated samples and the compression rate. Using polar and multilevel coding, we scale our approach to handle long blocklengths in $O(n \log n)$ time in order to benefit from reduced per-symbol overhead. We conclude by demonstrating applications to variable-rate compression with stochastic VQ-VAEs and communication-efficient differentially private distributed mean estimation via exact simulation of the Gaussian mechanism.

</details>


### [29] [Score-based Outlier Generation via Controlling the Radon-Nikodym Derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12113)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Tristan Milne,Kry Yik-Chau Lui,Stephanie Hazlewood,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对数似然分布的测度论离群点定义，推导似然重加权对扩散得分函数的影响，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群构造指数插值控制器，无需重训练即可实现对低似然样本的可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离群点对算法压力测试和系统行为理解很重要，但现有生成方法虽将离群点描述为低似然事件，却很少显式控制似然。作者希望获得一种能保证以可指定幅度赋予低似然事件更高概率质量的可控离群样本生成方法。

Method: 首先定义基于对数似然值分布的测度论离群点概念；然后推导似然重加权如何修改扩散得分，得到由似然分布Radon-Nikodym导数导出的控制项对得分函数进行缩放；利用扩散模型背后的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群，启发式构造指数插值控制器来近似真实控制项，整个过程无需重训练扩散模型。

Result: 实验表明该方法能可控地生成低似然样本，同时生成结果与数据几何保持一致。

Conclusion: 通过测度论离群点定义与似然重加权对扩散逆时动力学的可控修改，可在不重训练的前提下实现低似然样本的可控生成，并保持数据几何一致性。

Abstract: Outliers are important for stress-testing algorithms and understanding system behaviour under rare conditions. Despite being commonly described as low-likelihood events, existing generative approaches rarely control likelihood explicitly. In this work, we introduce a measure-theoretic notion of outliers based on the distribution of log-likelihood values, which is guaranteed to assign higher probability mass to low-likelihood events with a specifiable magnitude. Building on this formulation, we derive how likelihood reweighting modifies the diffusion score and use this relation to motivate a controlled modification of the reverse-time dynamics. In particular, likelihood reweighting implies a scaling of the score function with a control term derived from the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the likelihood distributions. Correspondingly, the updated score function can be obtained with no retraining of the diffusion model. We exploit the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck semigroup underlying diffusion models to motivate an exponentially interpolated controller which approximates the true control. Experiments demonstrate controlled generation of low-likelihood samples while remaining consistent with the data geometry.

</details>


### [30] [Almost Sure Convergence Analysis of Stochastic Gradient Methods with Clipping and Additive Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12119)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了带梯度裁剪与加性高斯噪声的随机梯度下降（SGD-CN）在标准衰减步长下几乎必然收敛，并将结论推广到动量类变体，给出凸与非凸情形下的路径级收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度裁剪会引入随机梯度偏差，加性噪声会额外增加方差，二者共同作用使单条优化轨迹的长期行为难以刻画。现有理论多关注期望意义下的收敛，缺乏对路径级（几乎必然）行为的严格分析，因此需要更强的理论基础来理解裁剪随机梯度法的稳定性。

Method: 在目标函数光滑、随机梯度噪声一致有界的假设下，结合标准衰减步长条件，对SGD-CN进行几乎必然收敛性分析；并通过设计能量函数（Lyapunov/势函数）构造，将分析框架扩展到随机重球法与Nesterov加速梯度等动量变体。

Result: 证明SGD-CN在凸与非凸情形下均几乎必然收敛；动量类变体（随机重球、Nesterov加速）经合适的能量构造后同样获得几乎必然收敛保证。

Conclusion: 尽管裁剪与扰动带来偏差和方差，算法仍保持路径级稳定性；该工作为裁剪随机梯度方法的路径行为提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with gradient clipping and additive noise has become a standard technique for training machine learning models, particularly in applications requiring robustness or privacy guarantees. However, clipping introduces a bias in stochastic gradients, while additive noise introduces additional variance, making the long-run behaviour of individual optimization trajectories difficult to characterize. In this work, we prove that SGD with clipping and additive Gaussian noise (SGD-CN) converges almost surely (a.s.) under smoothness and uniformly bounded stochastic-gradient noise assumptions, provided the step sizes satisfy some standard decaying conditions. Our analysis extends to momentum variants such as the stochastic heavy ball and Nesterov's accelerated gradient, where we show that careful energy constructions yield similar guarantees. These results provide stronger theoretical foundations for understanding the pathwise behaviour of clipped stochastic gradient methods and suggest that, despite the bias and noise introduced by clipping and perturbation, the algorithm remains stable in both convex and nonconvex regimes.

</details>


### [31] [GUIDE: Generative Utility Inference and Decision Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12137)
*Anagha Tiwari,Alexander G. Gray,Nick Feamster,Brian Jabarian,Alex Imas,Alex Kale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUIDE，一种由 LLM 驱动的偏好 elicitation 架构，结合贝叶斯自适应采样与符号表示学习来高效推断用户多维偏好，并在投资组合优化任务中取得更优的冷启动与更低推荐后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量人类用户偏好是 AI 对齐的核心挑战。现有 elicitation 方法难以高效发现多维偏好，也难以将推断结果准确扎根于领域知识。

Method: GUIDE 通过对话推断用户偏好，融合两项机制：(1) 基于参数化偏好状态的贝叶斯自适应采样用于问题选择，并通过可扩展的变换类型系统泛化到多样 elicitation 问题；(2) 符号表示学习，利用基于规则的学习在初始化阶段捕捉世界知识，并基于决策备选数据设置偏好维度先验。架构还提供可观测性与可操控性以支持部署与过程分析。

Result: 在投资组合优化的 in silico 实验中，与已有工作、纯 LLM 基线及 GUIDE 消融版本相比，GUIDE 在不同用户画像上均改进了冷启动表现，并在早期 elicitation 交互中持续降低推荐后悔。

Conclusion: GUIDE 表明将自适应采样与符号知识初始化相结合，可提升多维偏好推断的效率与领域接地性，为 AI 对齐中的偏好测量提供有效架构。

Abstract: Measuring the preferences of human users remains a fundamental challenge of AI alignment. Existing elicitation approaches struggle to efficiently discover multidimensional preferences or accurately ground these inferences in domain knowledge. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, an LLM-driven elicitation architecture that infers user preferences through conversations by combining Bayesian adaptive sampling for question selection and symbolic representation learning to initialize domain-specific preference models. GUIDE generalizes adaptive sampling to diverse elicitation questions through an extensible type system of transforms on a parameterized preference state. GUIDE produces domain-specific preference representations through an initialization process using symbolic rule-based learning to capture world knowledge and set priors over preference dimensions grounded in data about decision alternatives. The architecture provides observability and steerability to facilitate deployment and analyze elicitation processes. In silico experiments on investment portfolio optimization demonstrate that GUIDE improves cold-start and minimizes recommendation regret consistently within early elicitation interactions across user personas compared to prior work, LLM-only baselines, and ablated GUIDE versions.

</details>


### [32] [Rank-Efficient LoRA via Joint Tangent-Space Optimization under Isotropic Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12123)
*Zihan Zhu,Zhehang Du,Xuyang Chen,Tim Tsz-Kit Lau,Jiayuan Wu,X. Y. Han,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ISO-LoRA优化器，通过谱下降耦合LoRA因子更新，提升有效秩与秩利用率，在0.1B–7B语言模型适配中优于因子独立优化器，在中等至较大秩时增益最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中通常用LoRA秩控制参数预算与表达能力，但该观点不完整：名义秩只决定表达能力上限，优化器决定该能力被实际使用的程度。GPT-2案例显示强秩依赖的优化器效应——相同名义秩下AdamW产生谱集中、低有效秩的逐步更新，而Muon方向更丰富且更稳定地从增大秩中获益。因此需要面向"秩利用率"的优化器设计。

Method: 提出ISO-LoRA：在权重空间中对诱导的切向扰动进行谱下降，从而耦合LoRA两个因子的更新；促使更新能量在奇异方向上更均匀分布，提高秩利用率，同时保持与LoRA参数化兼容。并以风格化的尖峰梯度模型下的单步分析给出理论保证，说明其有效秩可高于标准逐因子优化器。

Result: 在0.1B–7B参数语言模型适配任务上验证：ISO-LoRA提升了有效秩与下游性能，在中等至大LoRA秩时增益最强。

Conclusion: 秩利用率是LoRA优化的关键因素，优化器设计是通向更强参数高效适配的重要路径。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is an effective approach for adapting large pretrained models by learning low-rank weight updates. In practice, the LoRA rank is used to control an adapter's parameter budget and representational capacity. We show that this view is incomplete: while the nominal rank determines the representational capacity, the optimizer shapes how much of that capacity is used in the induced weight-space updates. In a case study of GPT-2 adaptation with LoRA, we observe a strong rank-dependent optimizer effect. Despite using the same nominal rank, AdamW often produces per-step updates with concentrated singular spectra and low effective rank, whereas Muon uses a richer set of directions and benefits more consistently from increasing LoRA rank. These observations motivate ISO-LoRA, an optimizer that couples the LoRA factor updates through spectral descent on the induced tangent perturbation in weight space. ISO-LoRA promotes updates that distribute energy more evenly across singular directions, improving rank utilization while preserving compatibility with the LoRA parameterization. We complement this design with theoretical guarantees showing that ISO-LoRA can achieve higher effective rank than standard factor-wise optimizers through a one-step analysis under a stylized spiked-gradient model. We validate this design on language-model adaptation across 0.1B-7B-parameter models, where ISO-LoRA improves effective rank and downstream performance, with the strongest gains at moderate-to-large LoRA ranks. Our results highlight rank utilization as a key factor in LoRA optimization and suggest that optimizer design offers an important path toward stronger parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [33] [Certifying Concept Unlearning in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12163)
*Mansi,Luca Marzari,Francesco Leofante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向T2I扩散模型概念遗忘的认证框架，量化残余概念泄漏并给出高置信上界，弥补攻击成功率评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I概念遗忘评估主要依赖自动对抗提示搜索得到的攻击成功率，仅覆盖有限查询，无法量化更广提示空间中的残余泄漏，易高估遗忘效果、低估安全风险。

Method: 结合统计认证与概念相关嵌入方向上的最坏情况分析，在用户指定置信水平下推导泄漏概率的显式上界。

Result: 在NSFW内容、艺术风格和名人身份三类概念、六种先进遗忘方法上评估，认证泄漏上界比标准攻击成功率平均高16.2%，揭示现有协议遗漏的显著残余风险。

Conclusion: 基于攻击的经验评估会显著低估残余泄漏，认证应作为T2I扩散模型概念遗忘可靠审计的必要补充。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of concept unlearning in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models primarily rely on attack success rates obtained through automated adversarial prompt search. However, these metrics provide only empirical evidence over a finite set of queries and leave residual leakage over the broader prompt space largely unquantified. This limitation can lead to overestimating unlearning effectiveness and underestimating safety risks. To address this gap, we introduce a novel certification framework for T2I concept unlearning that provides high-confidence guarantees with bounded error on residual concept leakage. Our approach combines statistical certification with worst-case analysis along concept-relevant embedding directions to derive explicit upper bounds on leakage probability under user-specified confidence levels. We evaluate our framework across three major concept categories namely NSFW content, artistic styles, and celebrity identities, and six state-of-the-art unlearning methods. Certified leakage bounds consistently exceed standard attack success rates by 16.2%, uncovering substantial residual risks missed by existing evaluation protocols. Crucially, our results demonstrate that empirical attack-based evaluations can significantly underestimate residual leakage and establish certification as a necessary complement for reliable auditing of concept unlearning in T2I diffusion models.

</details>


### [34] [Estimating Pedestrian Volumes from GIS-Derived Built-Environment Features: A Machine Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12173)
*Bahareh Golchin,Banafsheh Rekabdar,Sirisha Kothuri,Joseph Broach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建机器学习流水线，利用开放GIS数据预测波特兰101个城市交叉口的2小时晚高峰行人流量，最优模型较负二项GLM基线显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构需要在全路网范围内估算行人流量以优化安全投资，但人工计数成本高且仅覆盖少数交叉口，因此需要可扩展的预测方法。

Method: 以实践中常用的负二项GLM为基线，引入特征选择、计数感知梯度提升和重复交叉验证；输入为建成环境、土地利用和街道网络等开放GIS特征；通过RMSE、MAPE、SMAPE在四种交叉验证策略下的综合排名选择配置。最优配置为基于直方图的梯度提升（Poisson损失）结合L1 Lasso特征选择。

Result: 最优模型将交叉验证RMSE较GLM基线降低12%（89.8降至78.7），留出集RMSE降低19%（108.0降至87.9）。

Conclusion: 计数感知的梯度提升结合特征选择能有效提升城市交叉口行人流量预测精度，代码已在GitHub发布，便于实践应用与复现。

Abstract: Transportation agencies need pedestrian volume estimates across entire road networks to prioritize safety investments, yet manual counts are expensive and cover only a small share of intersections. We present a machine learning pipeline that predicts 2-hour PM peak pedestrian volume at 101 urban intersections in Portland, Oregon, from built-environment, land-use, and street-network features drawn from open GIS data. Starting from the Negative Binomial GLM used in practice, we add feature selection, count-aware gradient boosting, and repeated cross-validation, selecting one configuration by a combined rank over RMSE, MAPE, and SMAPE across four cross-validation strategies. The winner, a histogram-based gradient boosting model with Poisson loss and L1 Lasso feature selection, reduces cross-validated RMSE by 12% over the GLM baseline (89.8 to 78.7) and holdout RMSE by 19% (108.0 to 87.9). Code is released on GitHub.

</details>


### [35] [Explanations-Driven Active Feature Acquisition for Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12179)
*Vinura Galwaduge,Jagath Samarabandu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解释驱动的特征获取方法EDFA，将算法追索与主动特征获取统一，在保持预测精度同时显著减少特征获取成本并提供可信追索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法假设模型可获得个体全部特征，但现实中特征获取成本高、决策常基于部分信息。主动特征获取（AFA）虽处理成本约束下的预测，却是"解释无关"的：以往只在获取额外特征后才给出解释，而未用解释来驱动特征获取。

Method: 利用马尔可夫毯理论统一反事实、半事实与替代事实（alterfactual）解释，刻画随着特征被获取可用追索如何增长；据此提出EDFA方法，按单位成本的解释价值选择特征；并扩展框架，为部分信息下给出的追索提供无分布有效性保证，给出认证所需校准数据量的下界。

Result: 在7个公开数据集与基于神经网络的预测模型上，EDFA相比最先进AFA基线获取的特征显著更少，同时保持可比精度，并给出更具决策相关性和可操作性的追索。

Conclusion: 将追索与特征获取联合建模是可行且有效的：解释可作为驱动特征获取的信号，实现低成本、可信且可操作的算法追索；框架附带理论保证与数据需求下界。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse methods typically assume that a predictive model has access to all features of an individual. In practice, decisions are often made with partial information, because features are costly to acquire. Active feature acquisition addresses cost-constrained prediction, but existing methods are explanation-agnostic: prior work provides explanations only after acquiring additional features, rather than using explanations to drive acquisition. This work flips that and treats algorithmic recourse and feature acquisition jointly. We use Markov Blanket theory to unify counterfactual, semifactual, and alterfactual explanations and to characterize how available recourse grows as features are acquired. Building on this framework, we propose an Explanation-Driven Feature Acquisition (EDFA) method that selects features by explanatory value per unit cost. The framework is further extended with distribution-free validity guarantees for recourse issued from partial information, which signal trustworthy, lower-cost recourse, along with a lower bound on the calibration data required to certify them. Experiments on 7 publicly available datasets with neural network-based predictive models show that EDFA acquires substantially fewer features than state-of-the-art AFA baselines while maintaining comparable accuracy and yielding more decision-relevant, actionable recourse. The implementation is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [36] [Quantile-based Loss Filtering for Outlier-Robust Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13040)
*Jamie Haddock,Anna Ma,Elizaveta Rebrova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并分析了基于损失过滤的通用框架 Quantile-\(k\)-Loss SGD (Q\(k\)L-SGD)，用于处理部分分量函数被损坏的有限和优化问题，并给出了线性收敛与小子样概率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限和优化中部分分量函数的梯度可能高度不可靠，现有 min-\(k\)-loss 等方法在鲁棒性上存在局限，例如 min-\(k\) 常因反复选择接近求解的分量而停滞，因此需要更通用的损失过滤框架。

Method: 提出 Q\(k\)L-SGD：每次迭代采样 \(k\) 个分量损失，并从经验下 \(q\)-分位数中均匀选择一个索引进行更新。理论分析包含两部分：在标准凸性假设下证明线性收敛，要求样本量随损坏数量及子集强凸阈值缩放；对于无法或不宜采用大样本的情形，给出覆盖任意样本量 \(k\) 的小子样概率分析，其收敛行为取决于选中离群点的概率与所选良好步的曲率。

Result: 在多项式回归、正则化逻辑回归和正则化 hinge 损失上的实验表明，中间分位数通常优于标准 SGD 和 min-\(k\)-loss SGD；min-\(k\) 常因反复选择接近求解的分量而停滞，而中间分位数保持鲁棒性并产生更有信息量的更新。

Conclusion: Q\(k\)L-SGD 为带损坏分量的有限和优化提供了通用且可分析的损失过滤框架，兼顾线性收敛保证与小子样场景下的概率刻画，并在多个任务上验证了中间分位数的优势。

Abstract: We study loss-based filtering for finite-sum optimization with a subset of corrupted component functions whose gradients may be highly unreliable. Motivated by minimum-loss-based SGD (min-$k$-loss) and quantile-based methods for corrupted linear systems, we propose and analyze a general loss-filtering framework -- Quantile-\(k\)-Loss SGD (Q\(k\)L-SGD) -- that samples \(k\) component losses at each iteration and updates using an index chosen uniformly from the lower empirical \(q\)-quantile. We prove linear convergence of this family of methods under standard convexity assumptions, requiring the sample size to scale with the number of corruptions and a subset strong-convexity threshold. For the cases when large enough sampling is impossible or undesirable, we give a complementary small-sample probabilistic analysis that covers any sample size $k$ and the convergence behavior depends on the probability of selecting an outlier and on the curvature of the selected good step. Experiments on polynomial regression, regularized logistic regression, and regularized hinge loss show that intermediate quantiles often outperform both standard SGD and min-\(k\)-loss SGD. In particular, min-\(k\) often stalls by repeatedly selecting nearly solved components, while intermediate quantiles retain robustness and produce more informative updates.

</details>


### [37] [Predicting Collision Cross Sections with GRACE: Geometric Residual Adduct Conditioning via Early-fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12223)
*Parthasarathy Suryanarayanan,Susanta Das,Shreyans Sethi,Kenneth M. Merz,,Joseph A. Morrone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRACE模型，通过几何残差与早期融合的加合物条件化，实现高精度三维碰撞截面（CCS）预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCS是离子迁移质谱中的常用分子注释描述符，反映气相离子的尺寸、形状与电离状态，预测难度大。现有预测器多忽略显式三维结构，或将加合物身份作为后期分类特征处理，难以捕捉加合物依赖的几何效应。

Method: GRACE基于预训练分子几何编码器，融合两种归纳偏置：一是相对加合物感知物理描述符基线的残差目标；二是通过可学习加合物token与低秩注意力适配器在编码器内部实现加合物条件化（早期融合）。在9000余条实验分子-加合物CCS记录上，采用随机、骨架和加合物敏感三种划分进行评估。

Result: GRACE在三种划分上均取得最佳平均百分比误差：随机划分1.67%、骨架划分2.11%、加合物敏感划分2.36%。诊断分析表明残差学习通过去除质量-CCS主导趋势稳定训练，早期融合较后期融合提升加合物敏感预测。在四个独立外部测试集上误差一致更低，并在留出集上优于四种已报道的物理工作流。

Conclusion: 残差学习与编码器级加合物条件化是实用有效的归纳偏置，可支持快速、准确的CCS预测。

Abstract: Collision cross section (CCS), derived from ion mobility mass spectrometry, is a common descriptor for molecular annotation. Prediction is challenging for machine learning models because it reflects the size, shape, and ionization state of a gas-phase molecular ion. Most predictors either ignore explicit 3D structure or treat adduct identity as a late categorical feature, which limits their ability to capture adduct-dependent geometric effects. We present GRACE (Geometric Residual Adduct Conditioning via Early-fusion), a 3D CCS predictor that adapts a pretrained molecular geometry encoder using geometric residual adduct conditioning via early fusion. GRACE combines two inductive biases: a residual objective relative to an adduct-aware physical descriptor baseline and adduct conditioning within the encoder via a learned adduct token and low-rank attention adapters. We evaluate the model on a curated set of over 9,000 experimental molecule-adduct CCS records with random, scaffold, and adduct-sensitive splits designed to separate interpolation, scaffold generalization, and adduct-driven generalization. GRACE achieves the best mean percentage difference among the evaluated learned models on all three splits: 1.67% on the random split, 2.11% on the scaffold split, and 2.36% on the adduct-sensitive split. Diagnostic analyses suggest that residual learning stabilizes training by removing the dominant mass-CCS trend, while early fusion improves adduct-sensitive prediction relative to late fusion. Across four independent external test sets, GRACE shows consistently lower error than the other evaluated models. On a held-out set, GRACE also attains the lowest mean percent difference when compared with four previously reported physics-based workflows. These results support residual learning and encoder-level adduct conditioning as practical inductive biases for fast, accurate CCS prediction.

</details>


### [38] [SIMS: Scale-Invariant Merit-Function-Based Scalarization for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12599)
*Zebin Chen,Fei Xing,Yang Chen,Hua Liu,Andy HF Chow,Yuhua Qian,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMS（尺度不变的基于价值函数的标量化方法），通过引入变换诱导的价值函数解决多任务学习中不同任务损失量级差异导致现有标量化方法对小损失目标优化不足的问题，理论上证明对数变换是保证尺度不变性的唯一选择，实验表明其性能达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习需要权衡多个竞争目标，通常采用多目标优化（MOO）框架。现有基于价值函数的标量化方法对任务损失的相对尺度高度敏感。实际多任务场景中不同任务的损失常相差数个数量级，优化过程会偏向尺度更大的目标，而Pareto最优解本应对正数缩放保持不变。这种尺度敏感性导致优化不公平、次优。

Method: 提出SIMS（Scale-Invariant Merit-function-based Scalarization）。核心是采用变换诱导的价值函数，将MOO问题转化为对损失量级不变的单目标优化。理论上证明：满足尺度不变性要求唯一地决定该变换为对数变换；并证明该变换诱导的价值函数保持弱Pareto最优性，且存在近似误差可控的光滑替代形式。通过实验在代表性多任务基准上验证。

Result: 在多个代表性多任务基准上，SIMS一致优于现有标量化方法，取得了状态最优（state-of-the-art）性能，验证了尺度不变性设计的有效性。

Conclusion: SIMS通过对数变换诱导的价值函数，从根本上解决了多任务学习中标量化方法对损失尺度敏感的问题，理论保证尺度不变性与弱Pareto最优性，并提供误差可控的光滑近似，实证表现优于现有方法，为多任务MOO提供了一种有效且理论完备的标量化方案。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) requires navigating unavoidable trade-offs among competing objectives. This paradigm is frequently formulated as multi-objective optimization (MOO), where the scalarization is favored to reduce an MOO problem to a single objective. We empirically find that existing merit-function-based scalarization approaches are sensitive to the relative scales of different objectives in practical MTL, where task losses commonly differ by orders of magnitude. The optimization process often favors objectives with larger scales even though the underlying Pareto optimal solutions remains invariant to rescaling (i.e., multiplying an objective by a positive constant). To address this issue, we propose Scale-Invariant Merit-function-based Scalarization (SIMS) for MTL. Specifically, SIMS adopts a transformation-induced merit function to convert the MOO problem of MTL to a single objective that renders optimization invariant to the magnitudes of losses. Theoretically, we prove that the requirement for scale invariance uniquely determines this transformation to be logarithmic. We further show that this general transformation-induced merit function preserves weak Pareto optimality and admits a smooth surrogate with controllable approximation error. Extensive experiments on representative multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that SIMS consistently outperforms existing scalarization methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [39] [Patient-Reported Survey Data Improve Prediction of Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12224)
*Xiyue Jiang,Zihan Ding,Grace Han,Yinan Liu,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于 All of Us 中 267,747 名有阿片类暴露史的参与者，比较 EHR-only 与 EHR+问卷模型对首次 OUD 诊断的预测能力，发现加入问卷数据在全部 24 种模型-时间窗组合中均提升 PR-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）可能无法完整捕捉与阿片使用障碍（OUD）相关的患者自报因素，因此需要评估问卷数据是否能提供 EHR 之外的补充预测信号。

Method: 研究纳入 All of Us 中 267,747 名有阿片类暴露记录的参与者，其中 OUD 病例 15,287 例。在 6、12、24 个月回顾窗口下，分别构建 EHR-only 与 EHR+问卷模型，采用逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM、多层感知机、LSTM、GRU 和 Transformer 进行预测比较，并进行置换重要性分析。

Result: 问卷增强在全部 24 个模型-窗口组合中均提升 PR-AUC，提升幅度为 0.0087–0.0505；最佳 24 个月 LightGBM 模型由 0.6219 提升至 0.6603。问卷覆盖率随窗口延长而提高，且因 OUD 状态而异（24 个月：OUD 阳性 21.7%，OUD 阴性 60.7%）。置换分析显示，在两种评估模型中，问卷特征在 24 个月时均为第二重要的信息域。

Conclusion: 患者自报问卷数据能够提供结构化 EHR 之外的互补预测信号，但问卷可得性存在差异，可能影响模型性能与公平性，需重视问卷覆盖问题。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) may incompletely capture patient-reported factors associated with opioid use disorder (OUD). We evaluated whether survey data improve prediction of a first recorded OUD diagnosis among 267,747 All of Us participants with documented opioid exposure, including 15,287 OUD cases. Survey augmentation improved PR-AUC across all 24 model-window combinations by 0.0087-0.0505; the best 24-month LightGBM model improved from 0.6219 to 0.6603. Survey coverage increased with longer windows and differed by OUD status (24 months: 21.7% OUD-positive vs. 60.7% OUD-negative). Conclusion: 该工作为分布偏移下的信号处理提供了新视角，通过前置预测替代事后检测，为OOD问题提供了新的研究范式与评估工具。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) data poses a significant threat to machine learning models, often leading to model failure during deployment. All existing OOD detection methods are post-hoc, relying on evaluation metrics such as accuracy and AUC-ROC during inference to indirectly assess the model's response to OOD data by measuring deviations. In contrast to existing approaches, the proposed work shifts the paradigm from OOD detection to OOD prediction by proposing a pre-hoc anticipatory framework called PLSP for OOD prediction. We make several key contributions: (a) a dataset-independent metric called the CREDibility Score (CREDS) is proposed for OOD prediction; (b) credibility curves are introduced to study the maximum credibility a model can attain; and (c) credibility heat maps (and volume under surface) are introduced to characterize pre-hoc model behavior across different datasets. This work provides a novel perspective on signal processing under distributional shifts. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed metric serves as a valuable measure for improving the robustness of machine learning models toward OOD prediction.

</details>


### [41] [CRFCAN: A Complex-Valued Cross-Domain Residual Network for Joint Channel and Phase Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12244)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRFCAN——一种复数残差FFT卷积注意力网络，用于sub-THz通信中信道与相位噪声的联合端到端估计，在NMSE与BER上优于传统算法与现有深度学习模型，且具备单次固定复杂度推理和跨PN模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sub-THz通信中超宽带宽与严重相位噪声耦合，导致传统联合信道与PN估计复杂度极高、计算代价难以承受；现有深度学习方案多依赖级联网络或神经网络与传统迭代估计器的混合框架，难以真正做到端到端联合恢复。

Result: 仿真结果表明，CRFCAN在归一化均方误差（NMSE）和误比特率（BER）上显著优于传统算法及当前最先进的深度学习模型；且以单次、固定复杂度推理即可取得优越性能，并可在不微调的情况下良好泛化到未见过的PN模型。

Abstract: In sub-terahertz (sub-THz) communications, the coupling of ultra-wide bandwidth and severe phase noise (PN) impairments renders conventional joint channel and PN estimation highly complex and computationally prohibitive. To address this, we propose CRFCAN, a complex-valued residual FFT convolutional attention network designed for joint channel and PN estimation. Unlike existing deep learning schemes that rely on cascaded networks or hybrid frameworks combining neural networks with conventional iterative estimators, CRFCAN performs joint recovery in a truly end-to-end fashion through a physics-inspired cross-domain structure. Specifically, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and inverse FFT modules are embedded within residual groups to enable iterative feature interaction across the time and frequency domains, thereby capturing both frequency-selective fading and time-varying phase distortions. In addition, two dedicated residual blocks are introduced for complex feature extraction and multiplicative phase-distortion modeling, respectively. A physics-aware PN output tail with soft normalization is further employed to improve estimation stability while preserving the physical characteristics of the effective PN process. Simulation results demonstrate that CRFCAN significantly outperforms conventional algorithms and state-of-the-art deep learning models in terms of normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER). Notably, CRFCAN achieves superior performance with single-shot, fixed-complexity inference and generalizes well to unseen PN models without fine-tuning, highlighting its robustness and practicality for sub-THz receivers.

</details>

*Zavier Li*

TL;DR: 该文研究在给定信息通道下，部分训练数据如何决定正定度量（cometric）几何，并给出仿射不变黎曼几何下满列秩正定压缩的唯一显式完备化与可辨识性刻画。


<details>

Result: 压缩映射是split-Hadamard度量submetry，存在实现可见目标的仿射不变最近满几何的唯一显式完备化，并给出精确满-可见变分约化；通道变动时完备化构成正定锥的规范不变秩分层；闭式拉回配对度量通过参考失配权重分离可见度量运动与子空间旋转，给出显式半正定多方向Gram矩阵，并揭示参考值模态的精确奇异性；正定实现给出闭式先验-数据收缩；对角与块族化为有效面部证书的锥像检验；确定性与有限样本界量化可见几何及其子空间的恢复。

Conclusion: 上述结果完整刻画了所述有限维仿射不变模型下的精确约化、参考依赖完备化与结构化表达性。

Abstract: Training data constrains optimizer geometry through the covectors visible to a declared information channel. We study how such partial information determines a full positive cometric relative to a reference and which degrees of freedom remain unidentified. Our central result resolves full-column-rank positive-definite compression under affine-invariant Riemannian geometry. The compression map is a split-Hadamard metric submetry and admits an explicit unique completion that is the affine-invariant nearest full geometry realizing a visible target and yields exact full-to-visible variational reduction. When the channel moves, the completions form a gauge-invariant rank stratification of the positive-definite cone. Its closed-form pullback pair metric separates visible-metric motion from subspace rotation through a reference-mismatch weight, yields an explicit positive-semidefinite multi-direction Gram matrix, and exposes the precise singularity of reference-valued modes. The mechanism is explained by a metric theorem equating ball submetry, attained fiber distance, and lossless reduction of every monotone radial visible decision problem. A smooth split-Hadamard theorem supplies coherent information sheets, proximal commutation, and solution-wise gradient-flow lifting. The positive-definite realization also gives closed-form prior-data shrinkage. Diagonal and block optimizer families reduce to relative-interior conic image tests with valid facial certificates, while deterministic and finite-sample bounds quantify recovery of the visible geometry and its subspace. Together these results characterize exact reduction, reference-dependent completion, and structured expressivity for the stated finite-dimensional affine-invariant model.

</details>

*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在硬单状态瓶颈+固定解码器的群复合任务上，梯度下降招募的秩恰等于任务代数所需的最小忠实实表示维度 d_min（Spearman ρ=0.9747）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值记忆的秩决定其能绑定、组合、追踪的独立方向数量。作者希望从标量容量界提升为表示论意义下的秩定律，并给出秩被"因果性"招募的证据。

Result: 招募秩等于群的最小忠实实表示维度 d_min，Spearman ρ=0.9747（设计上限）；S_4 与 A_5 在预注册检验下统计等价；低于 d_min 一个秩时，余弦相似度被目标单位谱上界 √((d_min-1)/d_min)≤0.894 限制（每群均＜0.9），实测为上限的86-95%（均值91%）；在 d_min 处，五群各四个种子均越过预注册锚定门槛。

Conclusion: 在该测试台上，实测有效秩跟踪表示维度；可解/非可解且维度匹配的 S_4/A_5 比较落在预注册容差内，支持秩由任务代数需求因果决定而非受群结构类别影响。

Abstract: Matrix-valued memories make rank the natural budget of a learned representation: the number of independent directions a state spans bounds what it can bind, compose, and track. We report causal evidence, on a group-composition testbed trained under a hard single-state bottleneck with a fixed decoder that cannot launder rank, that gradient descent recruits precisely the rank the task's algebra demands. A companion paper [Larson, 2026a] establishes the analogous recruitment and causal necessity pattern on a $K$-pair associative-binding testbed, where exact recovery provably requires state rank at least $K$; this paper inherits that instrument and extends the rank law from a scalar capacity bound to a representation-theoretic one. We train toward chosen minimal faithful reference representations embedded in larger matrices. On group-composition state tracking over five finite groups spanning the solvable/non-solvable divide, the recruited rank equals the group's minimal faithful real representation dimension $d_{\min}$ (Spearman $ρ= 0.9747$, the design's tie-capped maximum), the dimension-matched solvable/non-solvable pair $S_4$/$A_5$ is statistically equivalent under a pre-registered test, and a pre-registered force-rank test separates a guaranteed similarity ceiling from empirical recovery at the target dimension: one rank below $d_{\min}$, cosine similarity is capped by the target's tied unit spectrum at $\sqrt{(d_{\min}{-}1)/d_{\min}} \le 0.894$, below the $0.9$ threshold in every group by construction, with observed cells at 86-95% (mean 91%) of that ceiling; at $d_{\min}$, not guaranteed a priori, recovery clears the pre-registered anchor-relative bar at four seeds per group in all five groups. Within this testbed, measured effective rank tracks representation dimension; the matched-dimension $S_4$/$A_5$ comparison establishes equivalence within the pre-registered tolerance.

</details>

Conclusion: 将EHR基础模型作为生成式策略并用带时间感知奖励的强化学习微调，可有效缓解下一词预测范式的推理瓶颈，提升临床预测性能、数据效率与跨任务泛化，并改善生成轨迹的质量与实用性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) foundation models trained on longitudinal patient trajectories have demonstrated strong performance across diverse clinical prediction tasks. However, their clinical reasoning capabilities remain constrained by next-token prediction on limited and incomplete EHR data. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework that treats EHR foundation models as generative policies over patient trajectories. We formulate common clinical prediction problems (e.g., hospital readmission) as event-conditioned, time-windowed reasoning tasks. We then design time-aware, rollout-sensitive rewards to account for finite rollout lengths and temporally inconclusive outcomes. We find that RL fine-tuning consistently improves over pre-trained backbones and strong baselines. Notably, it enables smaller models to surpass larger pre-trained models in data-limited regimes and induces positive transfer across tasks. Further analysis shows that RL fine-tuned models generate trajectories with stronger structural and semantic alignment to ground truth and greater downstream utility.

</details>


### [45] [Amortized Low-Rank Adaptation for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12278)
*Fernando Palafox,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CLAW：用超网络在测试时生成 LoRA 适配器来适配世界模型，兼顾低计算成本与高表达力，仅需数秒交互数据即可超越梯度适配与上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型易因环境变化而失准，需要在测试时用少量交互快速适配未知环境。现有方法存在计算成本与表达力的权衡：上下文学习（in-context learning）成本低但表达力受限，梯度适配表达力强但计算昂贵。

Method: CLAW 使用超网络在测试时生成低秩（LoRA）适配器。预训练阶段，模拟对多种环境的适配过程，联合训练超网络与基础世界模型；测试阶段冻结基础模型，仅用一小批测试时 transition 经超网络前向传播生成适配器。

Result: 在动力学、形态与奖励各异的 locomotion 与 manipulation 环境族上评测：仅用数秒测试数据，CLAW 在线适配效果优于梯度适配与上下文学习；在数据稀缺时避免过拟合；其优势来自高表达力的适配器而非上下文条件；联合预训练超网络与基础模型优于事后训练超网络。

Conclusion: CLAW 通过超网络生成 LoRA 适配器，有效缓解了测试时适配中计算成本与表达力的权衡，为世界模型的快速在线适配提供了高效且表达力强的方案。

Abstract: World models let agents plan by predicting the consequences of their actions, but changes in the environment can make them inaccurate. We study the problem of adapting a world model to an unknown test-time environment, drawn from a known environment family, using only a few episodes of interaction. Existing approaches trade off computational cost against expressivity, i.e., the range of models a method can produce. For example, in-context learning is computationally cheap but limited in expressivity, and gradient-based adaptation is expressive but computationally expensive. We present CLAW (Context-conditioned Low-rank Adaptation of World models), which addresses this tradeoff by using a hypernetwork to generate low-rank (LoRA) adapters at test time. During pretraining, we simulate adaptation to a variety of environments and jointly train the hypernetwork and base world model. At test time, we freeze the base model and use a forward pass of the hypernetwork to generate adapters from a small batch of test-time transitions. We evaluate CLAW in locomotion and manipulation environment families that vary in dynamics, embodiment, and reward. We show that, using only seconds of test-time data, CLAW outperforms gradient-based adaptation and in-context learning during online adaptation. We also show that CLAW avoids overfitting in data-scarce regimes, that its advantage comes from the expressive adapters rather than context conditioning, and that pretraining the hypernetwork jointly with the base model outperforms training it post hoc.

</details>


### [46] [Sampling via Decision-Flow: Training-Free Extraction of Improved Latent Reasoning Paths in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12317)
*Zhendong Mi,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-Sample，一种无需训练、无需数据的推理时方法，通过构建分层推理树并对终节点打分、回传效用，来挖掘基础模型中潜在的高质量推理路径，从而在无需RL微调的情况下提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：RL究竟是为LLM赋予全新能力，还是仅重塑已有知识在推理时的表达方式。基于"分布锐化假说"——RL将概率质量重新分配到基础模型中已潜藏的高奖励轨迹上——作者追问：能否绕开昂贵的RL微调，直接释放这些潜在路径？

Method: 提出Decision-Flow Sampling（DF-Sample），一个无需训练、无需数据的推理时框架：构建分层推理树，对终节点进行质量打分，并将效用回传以指导每个中间分支决策。与仅做局部逐步选择的传统采样策略不同，DF-Sample在确定路径前进行显式的全局轨迹评估，从而恢复标准解码所忽略的高质量、低概率推理链。

Result: 在GPQA上，DF-Sample达到45.6%准确率，超过power sampling（38.9%）和GRPO（39.9%），表明无需训练的方法可超越经过训练的方法。在三个模型和四个基准上，DF-Sample持续优于各基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明预训练基础模型中蕴藏着可观的潜在推理能力，且这些能力可通过训练无关的全局搜索式采样被有效激发，为"RL主要是分布锐化而非能力生成"的观点提供了支持。

Abstract: A central question in LLM reasoning is whether reinforcement learning (RL) instills genuinely new capabilities or merely reshapes how existing knowledge is expressed during inference. Building on the distribution-sharpening hypothesis, which holds that RL reallocates probability mass toward high-reward trajectories already latent in base models, we ask: can we unlock those latent paths without costly RL fine-tuning? We present Decision-Flow Sampling (DF-Sample), a training-free, data-free inference-time framework that constructs a hierarchical reasoning tree, scores terminal nodes for quality, and back-propagates utilities to inform each intermediate branching decision. Unlike conventional sampling strategies that make purely local step-wise choices, DF-Sample performs explicit global trajectory evaluation before committing to a path, recovering high-quality but low-probability reasoning chains that standard decoding overlooks. On GPQA, DF-Sample achieves 45.6% accuracy, surpassing power sampling (38.9%) and GRPO (39.9%), showing that a training-free method can outperform a trained one. Across three models and four benchmarks, DF-Sample consistently outperforms baselines, indicating substantial latent reasoning potential in pretrained base models.

</details>


### [47] [Simulating Disengaged Students to Evaluate LLM-based Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12331)
*Xianghui Meng,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAS2（Disengagement-Aware Student Simulators），一个可复现的部署前协议与学生模拟器框架，用于建模五种学习者投入状态并对AI导师进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算模型生成的模拟学生是评估人类与AI导师策略的实用手段，但现有模拟未充分考虑学习者的脱离行为（如刷系统、原地打转、离题），而导师针对不同学习状态可能需要差异化应对。因此需要一个能覆盖多元投入状态的模拟与评估框架。

Method: 基于ASSISTments09数据集，两名标注者依据匿名化交互日志摘要独立标注100个辅导会话；构建规则式标签体系，对比人工共识标签与DAS2标签的一致性；将模拟条件化于目标学习状态；对比微调Qwen2.5-7B与仅提示GPT-4o在响应时间分布和状态区分度上的表现；并评估来自Claude、Llama、Gemini、Qwen、GPT五个家族的五个AI导师在不同学习状态与交互长度下的表现。

Result: 标注者间一致率为84%（Cohen's kappa=0.78），一致案例中人工共识与DAS2规则标签匹配率为81%（kappa=0.75）。条件化模拟使游戏行为的正确率差距从0.54降至0.20，原地打转从0.51降至0.18。微调Qwen2.5-7B更贴合真实响应时间分布，仅提示GPT-4o生成的学习状态区分度更高。五个AI导师的相对排名在不同状态和交互长度下保持稳定，但绝对性能存在差异，揭示状态特异性支持差异。人工验证表明自动评估与人类判断并不完全一致。

Conclusion: DAS2为评估AI导师在部署前如何应对多样化学习者投入状态提供了可复现的框架，但自动评估尚不能完全替代人类判断。

Abstract: Simulated students generated by computational models provide a practical way to evaluate tutoring strategies and pedagogical approaches used by human and AI tutors. However, such simulations should account for disengaged behaviors, including gaming the system, wheel-spinning, and off-task behavior, because tutors may need different responses for different learner states. We present Disengagement-Aware Student Simulators (DAS2), a reproducible pre-deployment protocol that models five learner-engagement states: engaged, gaming, wheel-spinning, off-task, and mixed, and evaluates AI tutor performance across these states. Using ASSISTments09, two coders independently labeled 100 sampled tutoring sessions based on anonymized interaction-log summaries. They achieved 84% agreement (Cohen's kappa = 0.78), and among agreed cases, human consensus labels matched DAS2 rule-based labels in 81% of cases (kappa = 0.75). Conditioning simulations on intended learner states reduced the correctness-rate gap between simulated and authentic sessions from 0.54 to 0.20 for gaming and from 0.51 to 0.18 for wheel-spinning. Fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B better matched authentic response-time distributions, while prompt-only GPT-4o generated more distinguishable learner states. Evaluation of five AI tutors from the Claude, Llama, Gemini, Qwen, and GPT families showed that relative rankings remained stable across learner states and interaction lengths, while absolute performance varied, revealing state-specific differences in tutor support. Human validation further showed that automated tutor evaluation does not fully align with human judgment. DAS2 provides a pre-deployment framework for evaluating how AI tutors respond to diverse learner-engagement states before deployment.

</details>


### [48] [Theoretical Guarantees for One-Shot Magnitude Pruning and Compute-Adaptive Early Exit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12337)
*Erdem Koyuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"部分计算 vs 完整计算"的统一视角研究神经网络的计算量削减问题，涵盖静态情形下的一次性幅度剪枝与自适应情形下的早退机制，并给出具有显式速率的理论刻画与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络推理的计算开销较大，剪枝（静态）与早退（自适应）是两条主流降算力路径，但二者通常被分开研究，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来比较与刻画部分计算相对完整计算的性能损失。

Method: 1）将计算量削减统一为部分计算与完整计算的对比问题，静态对应一次性幅度剪枝，自适应对应早退；2）在渐近单神经元模型中，证明一次性幅度剪枝的集中定理并给出显式收敛速率；3）提出条件感知机（conditional perceptron）用于刻画早退，推导其超额泛化误差随"计算间隙"的幂律衰减，指数随部分计算与完整计算的对齐程度趋于1而趋于无穷；4）扩展至深度网络，刻画剪枝导致的扰动随深度的累积机制，并在神经网络高斯过程（NNGP）模型下推导冻结骨干早退的计算-精度权衡。

Result: 给出了剪枝的集中性定理与显式速率；证明早退超额泛化误差按计算间隙的幂律衰减，且该指数随对齐度趋近1而发散；在深度网络中得到剪枝扰动随深度的累积规律及冻结骨干早退的计算-精度权衡关系；数值仿真与预测的标度律一致。

Conclusion: 该工作以统一的"部分/完整计算"视角建立了剪枝与早退的理论联系，揭示了计算量削减与泛化性能之间的定量标度关系，为静态与自适应算力削减方法的设计与分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study compute reduction in neural networks through a unified partial versus full computation view, captured by one-shot magnitude pruning in the static regime and early exit in the adaptive regime. In an asymptotic single-neuron model, we prove a concentration theorem for one-shot magnitude pruning with explicit rates. We also introduce the conditional perceptron for early exit and show that its excess generalization error decays as a power of the compute gap, with an exponent that grows to infinity as the alignment between partial and full computations tends to one. We then extend the analysis to deep networks, characterizing how pruning-induced distortions accumulate with depth and deriving a corresponding compute-accuracy tradeoff for frozen-backbone early exit under a neural network Gaussian process model. Numerical simulations corroborate the predicted scaling laws.

</details>


### [49] [ParaRecover: A Process-Level Benchmark for Error Localization and Recovery in Parallel Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12345)
*Bowen Guan,Zhentao Yin,Yanming Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ParaRecover，一个面向多轮并行工具调用智能体的过程级基准，用于评估中间执行错误的定位与恢复能力，并配套SDE评分准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准主要评估最终任务成功或工具调用正确性，难以揭示智能体能否可靠诊断并从中间执行失败中恢复。该问题在多轮并行工具调用场景尤为关键，因为错误会跨依赖分支传播并引发级联失败。

Method: 构建细粒度错误分类体系，涵盖规划依赖、工具选择与参数匹配三类共14种错误类型；基于此构建含10,626个实例、分两个难度等级的基准；并提出SDE评分准则，从结构完整性、诊断推理与演化策略三个维度对智能体执行过程进行细粒度评估。

Result: 在十余个主流大语言模型上的实验表明，即便是最先进模型仍在多轮错误传播、隐式工具调用失败与精确重规划方面表现不佳；同时SDE准则可作为有效监督信号，提升智能体的反思性恢复能力。

Conclusion: ParaRecover将评估从结果导向推进到过程导向，为多轮并行工具调用智能体的错误定位与恢复研究提供了基准与方法支撑，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing agent benchmarks mainly evaluate final task success or tool-call correctness, providing limited insight into whether agents can reliably diagnose and recover from intermediate execution failures. This limitation becomes particularly critical in multi-turn parallel tool-use scenarios, where errors may propagate across dependent branches and trigger cascading failures. We introduce ParaRecover, a process-level benchmark for evaluating error localization and recovery in multi-turn parallel tool-use agents. Built upon a fine-grained taxonomy of 14 error types covering planning dependencies, tool selection, and argument matching, the benchmark comprises 10,626 instances spanning two difficulty levels. To enable finegrained, process-oriented evaluation, we further propose the SDE rubric, which measures structural integrity, diagnostic reasoning, and evolutionary strategy during agent execution.Experiments across more than ten mainstream LLMs reveal that even state-of-the-art models still struggle with multi-turn error propagation,implicit tool-use failures, and precise replanning. Moreover, we demonstrate that the SDE rubric provides effective supervision signals for improving agents' reflective recovery capabilities. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gbw206/ParaRecover.

</details>


### [50] [LatentVerse: A Framework for Understanding Shared and Modality-Specific Information in Multimodal Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12364)
*Majd Alafrange,Samuel Friedman,John Kitonyo,Sana Tonekaboni,Mahnaz Maddah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentVerse是一个表示分析资源，融合Web可视化分析平台与命令行接口，统一多种表示质量度量诊断并支持多模态嵌入的共享/模态特定成分分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在嵌入已成为现代机器学习尤其是生物医学领域的核心数据抽象，但现有表示分析工作流分散于定制脚本和孤立指标，缺乏多模态分析能力，限制了可访问性与可复现性。

Method: 提出LatentVerse，包含面向可访问、报告驱动探索的Web可视化分析平台与面向可扩展技术工作流的命令行接口；统一多种表示质量度量的诊断，并将嵌入分解为共享成分与模态特定成分以支持多模态场景。

Result: 通过受控的单模态与多模态仿真、真实生物医学嵌入的发现导向分析，以及覆盖多样用例的用户研究对LatentVerse进行了评估。

Conclusion: LatentVerse支持对潜在空间进行全面且可解释的评估，使基础模型表示在生物医学与数据科学应用中更易理解。

Abstract: Latent embeddings have become a central data abstraction in modern machine learning, especially in biomedicine, where foundation models are increasingly used to encode multimodal data like clinical text, medical images, omics, and physiological signals. However, the utility and value of these representations depends on understanding their quality, structure, and the information they encode. Existing analysis workflows for evaluating representations remain fragmented across custom scripts, isolated metrics, and most importantly lack multimodal analysis, limiting accessibility and reproducibility. We present LatentVerse, a representation analysis resource that combines a web-based visual analytics platform for accessible, report-driven exploration with a command-line interface for scalable technical workflows. LatentVerse unifies diagnostics for various representation quality metrics and extends to multimodal settings by decomposing embeddings into shared and modality-specific components. We evaluate LatentVerse through controlled unimodal and multimodal simulations, discovery-oriented analyses on real biomedical embeddings, and a user study across diverse use cases. By supporting thorough and interpretable evaluation of latent spaces, LatentVerse makes foundation model representations more understandable in biomedical and data science applications.

</details>


### [51] [Split Conformal Prediction with Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12386)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Juyeon Kim,Erkhembayar Jadamba,Seungjin Choi,Hyunjin Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对标签漂移下共形预测失效问题，提出标签漂移调整贝叶斯分数（LSA分数），基于后验预测倾斜恒等式直接校正源预测分布，在分子性质预测中给出更短且覆盖相当的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测依赖可交换性假设，而标签漂移（标签边缘分布变化、条件分布不变）会破坏该假设，使标准共形程序无法维持目标覆盖。现有方法存在两类不足：基于残差的重加权方法忽略预测不确定性，导致区间宽度均匀（非自适应）；贝叶斯共形方法虽能产生自适应区间，但在源预测分布下评估符合性，与标签漂移下的目标域不一致。因此需要一种在标签漂移下与目标预测分布对齐的符合性分数。

Method: 提出标签漂移调整贝叶斯分数（LSA分数），一种由后验预测倾斜恒等式（posterior predictive tilting identity）导出的非符合性分数。该恒等式表明目标预测分布是源预测分布的 importance-weighted 变换，据此对贝叶斯分数做直接校正，使符合性评估与目标域对齐。该方法适用于任何具有可处理对数密度的源预测分布；作者以贝叶斯岭回归实例化，此时校正项具有闭式解。在受控标签漂移下的分子性质预测任务上进行评估。

Result: LSA分数在目标域中持续给出比基于残差和基于源分布的贝叶斯分数更短的预测区间，同时目标域覆盖率保持可比。在更强的标签漂移下，由于基于伪标签的密度比估计，所有方法均出现一定覆盖损失。

Conclusion: LSA分数通过后验预测倾斜恒等式将贝叶斯共形分数校正到目标域，实现了标签漂移下更紧的区间且覆盖可比，且适用于任意对数密度可处理的源预测分布，并以贝叶斯岭回归给出闭式校正。局限在于强漂移下伪标签密度比估计会带来覆盖损失。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification under exchangeability. However, this assumption is violated by label shift, where the marginal distribution of labels changes while the conditional distribution of inputs given labels remains stable. Under such shifts, standard conformal procedures no longer maintain their intended coverage behavior. Existing approaches address this via importance weighting. They pair the reweighting with residual-based nonconformity scores that ignore predictive uncertainty. The resulting intervals have uniform width. Bayesian conformal methods produce adaptive intervals by leveraging predictive distributions. They evaluate conformity under the source predictive, which is misaligned with the target domain under label shift. We propose the \emph{Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Score} (LSA score), a nonconformity score derived from a posterior predictive tilting identity. This identity shows that the target predictive is an importance-weighted transformation of the source predictive. We use it to derive a direct correction to the Bayesian score. We evaluate the method on molecular property prediction under controlled label shift. The LSA score consistently yields shorter intervals than residual-based and source-based Bayesian scores. Coverage in the target domain remains comparable. Under stronger shift, all methods incur some coverage loss due to pseudo-label-based density-ratio estimation. The LSA score is defined for any source predictive with a tractable log-density. We instantiate it with Bayesian Ridge Regression, where the correction admits a closed form.

</details>


### [52] [RiPPLE: Cross-Space Performance Prediction from Early Training for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12418)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiPPLE 将部分训练视作少量锚点架构的标签来源：先训练锚点至早期前缀，将其学习曲线外推为代理标签，再把标签传播到无标签的架构特征上，从而以少量训练预算实现全搜索空间的架构排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS 中为排序整个搜索空间而充分训练大量候选网络代价高昂；零成本代理虽在初始化时打分，但排序质量随搜索空间变化而不稳定；已有的学习型预测器虽降低成本，却通常需要完全训练标签或针对单个候选的部分训练特征。

Method: 方法将部分训练视为少量覆盖锚点集合的标签来源，而非每个候选的特征。流程为：训练锚点至早期前缀 → 外推其学习曲线得到代理标签 → 在无标签架构特征上传播标签。特征、读出与编码规则的选择不依赖留出准确率，并可跨搜索空间复用。

Result: 在来自四个搜索空间家族的十二个基准单元以及更大的 DARTS 空间上进行评估，考察了排序质量、标签效率、架构选择，以及读出、覆盖与传播三者的作用。结果表明，RiPPLE 能以占比很小的锚点训练预算给出全空间排序，相关比较均在各自的评估与成本协议下解读。

Conclusion: 早期训练信号被保留为锚点上的标签而非逐候选特征，配合可复用且无需留出准确率的规则选择，RiPPLE 在有限训练预算下实现了高效的全搜索空间架构排序。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) evaluates candidate networks, but fully training enough architectures to rank an entire space is expensive. Zero-cost proxies score architectures at initialization, yet their ranking quality varies across search spaces. Learned predictors reduce evaluation cost but typically require fully trained labels or partial-training features for individual candidates. We introduce $\textbf{RiPPLE}$, $\underline{\textbf{R}}$anking v$\underline{\textbf{i}}$a $\underline{\textbf{P}}$refix-$\underline{\textbf{P}}$ropagated $\underline{\textbf{L}}$abel $\underline{\textbf{E}}$xtrapolation, which treats partial training as a source of labels for a small coverage set of anchors. RiPPLE trains these anchors to an early prefix, extrapolates their learning curves to surrogate labels, and propagates the labels over label-free architecture features. The early-training signal remains a label on the anchors rather than a per-candidate feature. Feature, readout, and encoding rules are selected without held-out accuracy and reused across search spaces. We evaluate the method on twelve benchmark cells from four search-space families and on the larger DARTS space. The results examine ranking quality, label efficiency, architecture selection, and the roles of readout, coverage, and propagation. RiPPLE provides a whole-space ranking from a fractional anchor-training budget, with comparisons interpreted under their respective evaluation and cost protocols.

</details>


### [53] [MInTRL: Off-policy Intervention can boost On-policy RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12419)
*Mingyu Chen,Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Xuezhou Zhang,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MInTRL通过在on-policy轨迹中引入稀疏、局部的干预来扩展探索前沿，并采用序列级优势回归目标避免重要性采样，在数学和代码基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy RL虽保持数据与当前策略接近但探索受限于策略自身可发现的轨迹；off-policy方法（如SFT）能利用外部知识但存在较大分布偏移。核心挑战在于扩大探索的同时不牺牲可学习性。

Method: 提出最小干预强化学习（MInTRL）：生成时，judge-intervention策略周期性审查当前策略输出，将错误后缀替换为简短修正并立即交还控制权；训练时采用序列级优势回归目标，无需重要性采样。

Result: 稀疏局部干预显著提升覆盖范围，超越有限预算的on-policy采样，同时保持轨迹的整体on-policy性质。在数学与代码基准上持续优于标准on-policy和off-policy基线。消融实验显示自干预及不同judge策略下仍有效，性能在中等干预强度达到峰值。

Conclusion: 最小干预是一种有效的范式，可在保持on-policy特性的同时增强探索，提升RL训练效果，且强调"最小干预"的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is typically performed on-policy, keeping training data close to the current policy but limiting learning to trajectories that the policy can discover itself. Off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning, on the other hand, can leverage external knowledge beyond the base model's capabilities, but may suffer from large distribution shift. The key challenge is thus to expand exploration without sacrificing learnability. In this work, we introduce Minimal Intervention Reinforcement Learning (MInTRL), which expands the exploration frontier through sparse, local interventions in otherwise on-policy rollouts. During generation, a judge-intervention policy periodically reviews the current policy's output, replaces erroneous suffixes with short corrections, and immediately returns control to the policy. During training, MInTRL adopts a sequence-level advantage-regression objective that eliminates the need for importance sampling. We show that sparse, local interventions can substantially improve coverage beyond finite-budget on-policy sampling while preserving the overall on-policy nature of the resulting trajectories. Across math and code benchmarks, MInTRL consistently outperforms standard on-policy and off-policy baselines. Ablations show that MInTRL remains effective with self-intervention and across different judge policies, while performance peaks at moderate intervention intensity, highlighting the importance of intervening minimally. These results establish minimal intervention as an effective paradigm for enhancing on-policy RL.

</details>


### [54] [Granularity-Adaptive Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12424)
*Taoran Liang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Yingguang Yang,Rongrong Zhang,Yingzong Min,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GACA是一种无评论家的强化学习优势估计方法，按照状态不确定性动态调整步级与回合级信号混合权重，在ALFWorld和WebShop上优于GRPO和GiGPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在长时程任务中通常只有一个稀疏的回合级奖励。GRPO等无评论家、组相对方法会把单一的轨迹级标量广播到所有步骤，无法辨别哪一步决策导致了最终结果；GiGPO虽通过锚定状态分组恢复步级信号，却用固定权重融合步级与回合级估计，对关键分支决策和常规近乎确定性的转移分配了相同的分辨率，忽视了不同状态对精细信号的需求差异。

Method: 作者提出GACA，一种无评论家的估计器，其粒度由基于不确定性的关键性代理决定。具体地，GACA用每一步自身rollout记录的负对数似然（NLL）作为关键性评分，并以随该评分增长的逐步权重混合步级与回合级优势：NLL高于平均水平时加权细粒度信号，低于平均水平时加权回合级信号。此外，论文对实现的混合给出了精确的风险分解，证明在正方向对齐下，足够小的调制优于固定混合；还给出一个条件性结果，用期望NLL界定局部动作价值变化，并通过误差投影分析刻画混合相对于标量不确定性重加权在何时带来额外收益。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准上，GACA在1.5B和7B两种模型规模下均比GRPO和GiGPO取得了更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 研究表明优势估计的粒度应是状态依赖的而非固定的。以NLL为不确定性代理逐步调制步级与回合级信号的混合比例，能够在关键决策处保留细粒度信用分配、在常规转移处依赖回合级信号，从而在长时程稀疏奖励的智能体任务中提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now the standard way to train large language model agents on long-horizon tasks, where dozens of interdependent actions precede a single sparse reward. Critic-free, group-relative methods such as GRPO suit this regime, but they broadcast one trajectory-level scalar to every step and cannot say which decision drove the outcome. GiGPO recovers a step-level signal by grouping time steps that share an anchor state, yet it merges the step- and episode-level estimates under one fixed weight, spending the same resolution on a pivotal branching decision as on a routine, near-deterministic transition. We argue that the right resolution is state-dependent, and propose GACA, a critic-free estimator whose granularity follows an uncertainty-based criticality proxy. GACA scores every step by the negative log-likelihood its own rollout already records, then blends the two advantages with a per-step weight that grows with that score, so the gradient places more weight on the fine-grained signal at above-average NLL and on the episode-level signal below it. We derive an exact risk decomposition for the implemented mixture and show that sufficiently small modulation improves on fixed mixing under positive directional alignment. A separate conditional result bounds local action-value variation using expected NLL, while an error-projection analysis characterizes when mixing adds value beyond scalar uncertainty reweighting. On ALFWorld and WebShop, GACA improves task success over GRPO and GiGPO at both 1.5B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [55] [Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation for Longitudinal Tumor-State Proxy Forecasting in Post-Treatment Glioma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12435)
*Yeonjae Jung,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将胶质瘤治疗后MRI的纵向预测建模为"观测锚定的数字孪生更新"问题：以中间观测作为患者特异状态的锚点，用SegMamba单步预测器生成更新提案，再通过观测锚定选择性同化（OASA）与校准进行融合，在120例患者来源的三元组数据上验证其优于直接预测与纯推演策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤治疗后MRI随访可提供患者特异的肿瘤状态代理估计的序列观测，用于持续更新个体化状态；但影像表现与演化轨迹的高度异质性使得未来状态预测十分困难。既有方法或完全依赖初始扫描预测，或在无锚定的情况下多步推演，容易产生漂移，缺乏对真实中间观测的有效利用。

Method: 将预测形式化为观测感知的数字孪生更新：以中间观测锚定患者特异状态。数据为203例患者、594个随访时间点；按预设的"无新治疗"标准从236个候选三元组中保留120个，并在患者层面划分为81/24/15的训练/验证/测试三元组。每个时间点用MRI病灶标签导出的连续体素级肿瘤状态代理图（取值[0,1]）表示。以基于SegMamba的单步预测器从多模态源状态张量生成更新提案；提出观测锚定选择性同化（OASA），保留已观测中间代理作为状态锚点，并通过验证集选定的分级病例级规则和体素级软门控选择性施加上更新。对比基线包括：初始扫描预测、无同化推演、最近观测持续（persistence）、直接预测、OASA、OASA+校准以及形态学膨胀。检查点、OASA规则与校准阈值仅在验证集上选择。

Result: 在15个留出测试三元组、3个随机种子上，OASA在τ=0.2时Dice与persistence相当（0.6071±0.0025 对 0.6070），在τ=0.5时Dice数值上更高（0.4269±0.0079 对 0.3981），RMSE略有上升。校准将τ=0.2的Dice提升至0.6178±0.0025，同时使假阳性支撑从11,836增至18,663，假阴性支撑从22,107降至17,536。

Conclusion: OASA通过将观测中间状态作为锚点并选择性同化模型更新，可在不损失低阈值分割精度的前提下改善较高阈值下的预测表现；但校准带来的增益主要源于近阈值支撑的重新分配（阈值效应），而非肿瘤生物学预测能力的实质性提升。代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-treatment MRI in patients with glioma provides serial observations for updating patient-specific tumor-state proxy estimates, but variable appearances and trajectories complicate forecasting. We formulate forecasting as an observation-aware digital-twin update in which an intermediate observation anchors the patient-specific state. Among 203 patients and 594 follow-up time points, a predefined no-new-treatment criterion retained 120 of 236 candidate triplets, split into 81/24/15 training/validation/test triplets at the patient level. Each time point was represented by a continuous voxel-wise tumor-state proxy map in [0,1] derived from MRI lesion labels. A SegMamba-based single-step forecaster predicted update proposals from multimodal source-state tensors. Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation (OASA) retained the observed intermediate proxy as the state anchor and selectively applied updates through a validation-selected tiered case-level rule and voxel-wise soft gate. We compared initial-scan forecasting, rollout without assimilation, latest-observation persistence, direct prediction, OASA, OASA + calibration, and morphological dilation. Checkpoints, OASA rules, and calibration thresholds were selected using validation data only. Across three seeds on 15 held-out test triplets, OASA maintained Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 comparable to persistence (0.6071 $\pm$ 0.0025 vs. 0.6070) while yielding numerically higher Dice at $τ$ = 0.5 (0.4269 $\pm$ 0.0079 vs. 0.3981), with a small RMSE increase. Calibration increased Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 to 0.6178 $\pm$ 0.0025, increased false-positive (FP) support (11,836$\rightarrow$18,663), and reduced false-negative (FN) support (22,107$\rightarrow$17,536). This reflects near-threshold support calibration rather than improved biological predictive capability. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jsudg436/longitudinal-proxy-forecasting.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond the Query: Do Retrieval Signals Improve Adaptive Multimodal RAG Routing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12437)
*Qiaomu Li,Qiuyuan Zhang,Nong Ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在文档、音频、视频三类RAG任务中，通过严格控制变量的对照实验发现：在已知查询(query)的前提下，额外的检索状态信号并未带来可靠的routing增益。核心结论是方法学层面的——检索状态特征的价值必须通过与匹配的query-only基线对照来证明，而非默认假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adaptive RAG通常利用检索时信号来决定是否执行额外检索、重排序或多模态步骤，但既有工作往往将这些检索状态的增益默认为有效，缺乏与"仅依赖查询"基线的严格对照。作者质疑：当查询本身已知时，检索状态信号是否仍能提供增量路由价值。

Method: 在文档、音频、视频三类RAG任务上进行匹配对照实验。保持可选动作(是否检索、重排、多模态)、routing模型族、训练流程、评估方式完全一致，仅对比query-only router与query+retrieval router两种设置。

Result: 在留出的最终评估中，加入所测试的检索信号相对query-only基线并未产生可靠的路由改进。部分检索信号与"后续步骤是否有效"存在关联，但这种可预测性并不能稳定转化为更优的RUN/SKIP决策。

Conclusion: 结论主要是方法学意义上的：不应在没有对照query-only控制组的情况下，将路由价值归功于检索状态特征。结果并非表明routing或检索状态普遍无用，而是强调检索信号的增量价值必须被证明，而不能被默认假设。

Abstract: Adaptive RAG often uses retrieval-time signals to decide whether another retrieval, reranking, or multimodal step should run. We ask whether these signals add routing value once the query itself is already known. Across document, audio, and video RAG, we compare matched query-only and query+retrieval routers while holding the optional actions, router family, training procedure, and evaluation fixed. On the held-out final evaluation, adding the tested retrieval signals does not produce a reliable routing improvement over the query-only baseline. Some retrieval signals are associated with whether a later step will help, but that predictability does not consistently lead to bet- ter RUN/SKIP decisions. The main lesson is therefore methodological: retrieval-state features should not be credited with routing value unless they improve over a matched query-only control. Our results do not show that routing or retrieval state is generally useless; they show that the incremental value of retrieval signals must be demonstrated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [57] [3D Digital Twin Visualization of Multiclass GRF-Based Gait Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12442)
*Nayoung Son,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个整合框架，基于双侧GRF和COP信号对健康步态与多种肌骨损伤组进行分类，结合ε-LRP可解释性与Blender三维可视化，验证准确率99.00%、测试准确率90.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动步态分析既需要准确的分类性能，也需要可解释的输出；现有方法往往只关注分类精度，缺乏解释性与直观的可视化手段，难以提升模型透明度。

Method: 使用双侧地面反作用力（GRF）与压力中心（COP）信号；信号在支撑相内归一化，并用训练集统计量标准化；采用会话级（session-level）数据划分训练分类模型；使用类特定ε-LRP对预测进行归因分析，识别左右两侧、多信号分量及不同支撑相的正负贡献；将处理后GRF信号与模型预测同步嵌入基于Blender的三维可视化，实现试次级样本检查。

Result: 验证准确率99.00%，测试准确率90.07%（会话级划分）；ε-LRP可定位双侧、多分量、多支撑相的正负贡献；三维可视化支持样本级步态试次与分类结果同步检查。

Conclusion: 该框架整合分类、可解释性与三维可视化，提升了模型透明度，可为肌骨损伤步态分析提供兼具精度与可解释性的工具；源代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated gait analysis requires accurate classification and interpretable outputs. We propose an integrated framework for classifying healthy gait and multiple musculoskeletal impairment groups using bilateral ground reaction force (GRF) and center-of-pressure (COP) signals. The signals were normalized over the stance phase and standardized using training-set statistics. The model achieved a validation accuracy of 99.00\% and a test accuracy of 90.07\% under a session-level split. Class-specific $ε$-LRP identified positive and negative contributions across both sides, multiple signal components, and different stance phases. Separately, the processed GRF signals and model predictions were synchronized within a Blender-based 3D visualization, enabling sample-level inspection of gait trials and classification results. The proposed framework integrates classification, explainability, and 3D visualization to improve model transparency. The source code is available in the following repository: https://github.com/nyoico/grf-gait-3d-visualization.git

</details>


### [58] [A Differentially Private Federated Proximal Optimization Framework for Customer Churn Prediction in Heterogeneous Federated Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12470)
*Joydeb Kumar Sana,Subrata Chakraborty,M M Manjurul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于差分隐私的联邦近端优化框架（DP-FedProx），用于电信客户流失预测，在保证隐私的同时取得接近集中式模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信客户流失预测传统上依赖集中式机器学习，需将客户数据汇聚至中央存储库，带来隐私泄露风险并可能违反数据保护法规。联邦学习虽可让多家运营商在不共享原始数据的情况下协同训练全局模型，但真实客户数据往往是非独立同分布（non-IID）的异构数据，会削弱标准联邦学习性能；同时训练后的模型还可能遭受隐私攻击。因此需要一种同时应对数据异构性与隐私泄露的方法。

Method: 提出差分隐私（DP）与联邦近端优化（FedProx）结合的 DP-FedProx 框架。在两个公开电信流失数据集上进行实验，训练并对比 FedAvg、DP-FedAvg、FedProx 与所提 DP-FedProx，并设置多种集中式与本地模型作为基线；采用七种常用评价指标进行评估；并使用 SHAP 进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 基于 FedProx 的模型一致优于基于 FedAvg 的模型。与最佳集中式模型相比，所提 DP-FedProx 在提供隐私保障的同时，仅出现小幅精度下降，取得具有竞争力的预测性能。SHAP 分析显示，DP-FedProx 优先关注收入组（revenue group）相关特征。

Conclusion: 所提 DP-FedProx 框架在预测性能与数据隐私保护之间实现了实用平衡，适用于电信客户流失预测场景。

Abstract: Customer churn is one of the major issues in the telecommunication industry. To predict customer churn, conventional centralized machine learning approaches have been widely used. This centralized approach requires customer data to be stored in a central repository, which raises privacy concerns and may violate data protection regulations. Federated learning addresses this problem by allowing multiple telecom operators to collaboratively train a global model without transferring their raw customer data. However, real-world customer data are often heterogeneous (non-IID), which may negatively affect the performance of standard federated learning. Trained models can also suffer from privacy attacks. To address those issues, we propose a Differentially Private (DP) based Federated Proximal optimization (FedProx) framework. All experiments were performed on two publicly available telecom churn datasets. We trained Federated Averaging (FedAvg), DP-FedAvg, FedProx, and the proposed DP-FedProx framework. For baseline comparison, we also used several centralized and local models. To evaluate the models, we employed seven widely used evaluation metrics. The experimental results show that the FedProx based models consistently outperform the FedAvg based models. Compared with the best centralized model, the proposed DP-FedProx framework achieves competitive prediction performance with only a small reduction in accuracy while providing privacy guarantees. To explain our model, we conducted SHAP analysis which shows that DP-FedProx method priorities revenue group features. These results indicate that the proposed DP-FedProx framework provides a practical balance between prediction performance and data privacy protection.

</details>


### [59] [Temporal Recurrence Favors Fewer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12531)
*Ivan Anokhin,Johan Obando-Ceron,Irina Rish,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环模型在序列任务中跨时间步携带隐式计算后，每个时间步内仍需多少深度，并将该问题形式化为给定计算预算下的计算分配问题。结果表明，时间循环会把最优计算分配推向更少的层数，且性能相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已表明循环可让浅层模型具备竞争力，但尚不清楚当时间循环提供了跨步骤的序列计算后，单步内还需要多少深度。作者将此问题转为计算分配问题，探讨在固定预算下深度、专家宽度与每层专家数的权衡。

Method: 在多个计算预算下，改变单步内深度、专家宽度和每层并行专家数量；对每个预算，在每步计算量近似匹配的条件下，比较最佳的循环与非循环计算分配方案；实验涉及 Sokoban 和 FineWeb 自回归语言建模。

Result: 在约匹配的每步计算量下，时间循环使最佳观测计算分配显著偏向更少的层数，同时取得相当或更好的性能。

Conclusion: 时间循环可替代部分单步内深度，序列计算的角色会改变架构的计算分配偏好，从而支持更浅的循环模型在同等每步算力下表现不劣甚至更优。

Abstract: In streaming tasks, recurrent models can carry latent computation across time, allowing each update to build on representations produced earlier. This raises a basic question: once temporal recurrence provides sequential computation across steps, how much depth is still needed within each step? Prior work has shown that recurrence can make shallow models competitive. We instead study this question as a compute-allocation problem, varying within-step depth, expert width, and the number of parallel experts per layer across several compute budgets. For each budget, we compare the best observed recurrent and non-recurrent allocations and the performance they achieve under approximately matched per-step computation. Across Sokoban and autoregressive FineWeb language modeling, we find that temporal recurrence shifts the best observed compute allocation toward substantially fewer layers, with comparable or better performance.

</details>


### [60] [$\text{GSF-}χ$: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12532)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GSF-χ图变换器，用立体单元生成反射偶相位场与手性伪标量χ驱动Chiral-RoPE，使编码器对反射敏感而非仅对旋转等变；证明其奇偶分解与对称性恒等式，在ECD等任务上取得领先并验证对称一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对映体共享原子、键与成对距离，但在手性环境中行为不同。因此分子编码器必须尊重原子重标记与真旋转不变性，同时不能对镜像反射"失明"（即需区分对映体）。现有方法往往只标记单个原子为特殊的手性中心，无法让手性信息全局作用。

Method: GSF-χ为图变换器：每个中心或轴向立体单元在所有原子上产生反射偶的相位场；手性伪标量χ设定隐空间query–key块上相对旋转的方向，构成Chiral-RoPE，使反射将其反转而非保持不变。C2投影将镜面偶的ECD峰数与峰位与镜面奇的峰符号分离。作者证明了算子的奇偶分解及注解反转、置换、单位阶恒等式（在显式规范角色条件下），并用性质测试与坐标反射审计端到端验证。

Result: GSF-χ在所有中心型ECD输出上领先，并在轴向Rotation与Symbol上分别较最强基线提升12.6%与7.9%。等预算对照表明Rotation优势来自全局带符号支撑而非参数量或边数；C2投影在完整监督下以小幅原始精度代价实现精确对映体对一致性，并在镜像监督稀缺时具备预测能力。

Conclusion: GSF-χ通过对立体单元施加全局反射偶相位场与手性RoPE，使模型在尊重置换与旋转的同时对反射敏感，理论恒等式与实验验证了其手性对称性，并在ECD、Rotation、Symbol等任务上取得优势，C2投影可提供对映体一致性。

Abstract: Enantiomers share atoms, bonds, and pairwise distances yet can behave differently in chiral environments, so molecular encoders must respect atom relabelings and proper rotations without becoming blind to reflection. We introduce GSF-$χ$, a graph transformer in which stereogenic units modulate all pairwise interactions rather than single out one atom as special. Each central or axial stereogenic unit creates a reflection-even phase field over all atoms, a handedness pseudoscalar $χ$ sets the direction of a relative rotation on latent query--key blocks, giving a \textbf{Chiral-RoPE} that reflection inverts rather than leaves fixed. A $C_2$ projection separates mirror-even ECD peak counts and positions from mirror-odd peak signs. We prove the operator's even--odd decomposition and its annotation-inversion, permutation, and unit-order identities under explicit canonical-role conditions; property tests and a coordinate-reflection audit verify the laws end to end. GSF-$χ$ leads every central-ECD output and improves axial Rotation and Symbol by $12.6\%$ and $7.9\%$ over the strongest baseline. Equal-budget controls attribute the Rotation advantage to global signed support rather than parameter or edge count; the $C_2$ projection yields exact enantiomer-pair consistency at a small raw-accuracy cost under complete supervision and becomes predictive when mirror supervision is scarce.

</details>


### [61] [Quality-Constrained Routing over a Fixed Pool of Quantized Mixture-of-Experts Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12550)
*Zhenghong Huang,Hongfan Wu,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在固定常驻量化MoE实例池的边界下研究请求级路由问题，提出FWP（脆弱性加权困惑度）指标预测请求级量化退化风险，并用窗口级线性规划生成与LP最优KKT一致的打分进行路由分配，在88条Qwen提示上实现1.284×吞吐增益，相对请求无关混合提升2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化MoE服务可在内存中常驻同一基座模型的多个预物化实例，但量化损伤随请求和比特宽度剧烈变化。实例物化与副本数量消耗内存且重配置缓慢，因此将其视为上游供给决策，本文转而研究固定常驻池内的路由问题，以在不改变供给的前提下按请求匹配最合适的量化实例。

Method: 在固定池边界内，以类级期望质量退化预算与实测实例容量为约束，最大化建模吞吐。引入FWP（Fragility-Weighted Perplexity）：在参考实例上对提示词token做prefill计算，并校准到候选实例的退化。其基础是精确的双专家亲和—脆弱性分解，以及条件多层top-k展开，显式保留偏置、交互、路由变化、可分性与高阶项。基于校准风险，用窗口级线性规划得到带符号的缩减奖励分数，在最优价格与原问题可行平局分配下与LP最优KKT一致。

Result: 在88条扩展Qwen提示上，量化全部6,144个专家块的完整W2、W3、W4实例分别产生平均ΔNLL为0.9437、0.1832、0.0513。在同一总体与τ=0.1513下，FWP分配达到1.284×的离线模型吞吐倍数，而请求无关混合为1.253×、静态W4为1.000×，FWP相对增益为2.5%。

Conclusion: 在固定常驻量化MoE实例池中，请求级风险感知路由可显著优于请求无关混合与静态单一实例方案；FWP提供了可校准、结构可解释的请求级退化预测，配合窗口级LP打分实现KKT一致的分配决策，验证了以最小额外复杂度换取吞吐提升的可行性。

Abstract: Quantized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) services can hold several pre-materialized instances of one base model, but quantization damage varies sharply across requests and bitwidths. Because instance materialization and replica counts consume memory and require slow reconfiguration, we treat them as upstream provisioning decisions and study routing within a fixed resident pool. Within this fixed-pool boundary, we route each request to maximize modeled throughput under a class-level expected quality-degradation budget and measured instance capacities. To predict this request-specific risk, we introduce FWP (Fragility-Weighted Perplexity), computed from prompt tokens on a reference-instance prefill and calibrated to candidate-instance degradation. Underlying FWP is an exact two-expert affinity--fragility decomposition and a conditional multi-layer top-$k$ expansion whose bias, interaction, route-change, separability, and higher-order terms remain explicit. Using these calibrated risks, a window-level linear program yields a signed reduced-reward score that is KKT-consistent with the LP optimum under optimal prices and primal-feasible tie allocation. On 88 extended Qwen prompts, complete W2, W3, and W4 instances quantizing all 6,144 expert blocks incur mean $Δ$NLL of $0.9437$, $0.1832$, and $0.0513$. Under the same population and $τ=0.1513$, FWP allocation reaches a $1.284\times$ offline model-based multiplier versus $1.253\times$ for request-agnostic mixing and $1.000\times$ for static W4, an incremental $2.5\%$ relative FWP gain.

</details>


### [62] [TokenMapper: A Step Toward Interoperable Speech Token Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12563)
*Tal Kozakov,Tal Rosenwein,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TokenMapper，一种方向感知框架，可在异构语音分词器间实现离散域的直接token到token翻译，无需中间波形重建，从而降低延迟并减少信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器将语音离散化为token序列，但不同模型的token空间在词表与码本结构上存在差异，导致模型间无法直接通信。现有跨模型信息传递通常需解码为波形再用第二个分词器重新编码，增加延迟并引入信息损失。

Method: TokenMapper采用方向感知（direction aware）机制，支持结构不匹配的token空间，包括单码本与多码本表征之间的映射，并在共享的有效token率下运行，实现离散域内的直接token到token翻译。

Result: 在GLM-4-Voice、MiMi与DualCodec上的实验显示跨模型性能一致：翻译WER与原生重建的差距在2.5-6.8%绝对WER以内；TokenMapper输出的人类MOS为2.29至4.39，与UTMOS呈相同方向性趋势；相较波形桥接，端到端延迟降低4.8-94.5%，每句最高节省972 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在无需中间波形重建的情况下，为跨模型语音token互操作性迈出了实用性一步。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs discretize speech into token sequences, but the resulting token spaces differ in vocabulary and codebook structure, preventing direct communication across models. This limitation affects applications such as conversational voice agents and speech to speech translation systems where multiple speech models must interact. As a result, transferring information between speech systems typically requires decoding to waveform audio and re-encoding with a second tokenizer, increasing latency and introducing potential information loss. To address these limitations, we present TokenMapper, a direction aware framework for direct token to token translation between heterogeneous speech tokenizers in the discrete domain.
