Method: 核心机制是universal reflection doubling：将每个残差profile与其反射配对，把两个binary transition matrices替换为一个固定块矩阵和一个固定swap involution；利用cell-seam identity使两个分支候选在tent fold处一致，其swap-odd分量被到fold的距离线性界定，从而用固定连续分段线性cone switch实现精确分支选择。通过circle doubling的反射商，从先前affine refinement的residual memory controller获得精确backward replay。

Result: 每个有限精化迭代都存在固定宽度、深度随迭代次数线性增长的精确ReLU实现；无需分离forcing profile与binary cell seams；可从residual memory controller精确backward replay；构造传播完整向量化profile而非分解为参考原子；并处理来自固定有限维族的stage-dependent forcing。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了向量值二元仿射精化算子的精确ReLU实现框架；当具有真正的reflection equivariance时，doubled cascade可约化为单一parity sector。

Abstract: We study vector-valued binary affine refinement operators with finitely supported matrix masks and compactly supported continuous piecewise linear input and forcing data. We prove that every finite refinement iterate admits an exact ReLU realization of fixed width and depth linear in the number of iterations. No separation of the forcing profile from the binary cell seams is required.
  The main mechanism is universal reflection doubling. Pairing each residual profile with its reflection replaces the two binary transition matrices by one fixed block matrix together with a fixed swap involution. The cell-seam identity makes the two branch candidates agree at the tent fold, while their swap-odd component is bounded linearly by the distance to the fold. This permits exact branch selection by a fixed continuous piecewise linear cone switch, without multiplication by a variable selector.
  The resulting primal recursion requires the residual orbit in reverse order. We obtain exact backward replay from the residual memory controller developed previously for affine refinement, interpreted here through the reflection quotient of circle doubling. The construction propagates the full vectorized profiles rather than decomposing the input and forcing into reference atoms. We also treat stage-dependent forcing from a fixed finite-dimensional family and show that genuine reflection equivariance reduces the doubled cascade to a single parity sector.

</details>


### [99] [Lyapunov Functions and $R$-Linear Convergence for Quadratic Barzilai-Borwein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12084)
*Shutai Yang,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文为Barzilai–Borwein方法在强凸二次问题上构造了一个显式的当前点Lyapunov函数，给出精确的衰减律，并据此证明有限维下的R-线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BB方法在强凸二次问题上具有R-线性收敛性，但目标值与梯度范数通常不单调，缺乏一个在轨迹上单调衰减的显式量，难以直接刻画收敛速率。

Method: 对warm-up步之后的二次动力学，假设初始活跃谱的两端点a<b在该步后仍存活，利用对应谱投影P_a,P_b构造端点幂次乘积形式的Lyapunov函数；该函数由Yang与Yuan的sharp-rate分析中的端点coboundary证书去归一化得到；对BB1、BB2及任意固定正权重延迟Rayleigh规则推导一步精确衰减等式；并证明端点指数在端点单项式类中是唯一minimax最优选择。

Result: 所构造的Lyapunov函数沿迭代满足一步精确关系，衰减因子为((b-a)/(b+a))^2乘以指数项exp(-2D_{a,b}(τ_k))，其中τ_k为倒数步长且D_{a,b}(τ_k)≥0；在非单调BB1轨迹上验证了该Lyapunov律；据此给出有限维R-线性收敛的证明。

Conclusion: BB方法的R-线性收敛可由一个显式当前点Lyapunov函数解释，其端点指数为唯一minimax选择，衰减律精确刻画了非单调轨迹上的收敛速率。

Abstract: The Barzilai--Borwein (BB) method is $R$-linearly convergent on strongly convex quadratics, although neither the objective value nor the gradient norm is generally monotone. We construct an explicit current-point Lyapunov function for the quadratic dynamics after the warm-up step, assuming that both endpoints of the initial active spectrum survive this step. If $a<b$ are these endpoints and $P_a,P_b$ are the corresponding spectral projectors, then \[
  \overline{f}(x)
  :=\lVert P_a\nabla f(x)\rVert^{\frac{2b}{a+b}}
  \lVert P_b\nabla f(x)\rVert^{\frac{2a}{a+b}} \] satisfies, for BB1, BB2, and every fixed positive weighted delayed Rayleigh rule, \[
  \overline{f}(x_{k+1})
  =\left(\frac{b-a}{b+a}\right)^2
  e^{-2D_{a,b}(τ_k)}\overline{f}(x_k),
  \qquad D_{a,b}(τ_k)\ge0, \] where $τ_k=1/α_k$ is the reciprocal step. The function is obtained by denormalizing the endpoint coboundary certificate in the sharp-rate analysis of Yang and Yuan. We prove that the endpoint exponents are the unique minimax choice among endpoint monomials and illustrate the Lyapunov law on a nonmonotone BB1 trajectory. Using this law, we also give a proof of $R$-linear convergence of the method in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [100] [$R$-Linear Convergence of Barzilai-Borwein Methods on Strictly Convex Quadratics via Closed-Cone Homogeneous Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12100)
*Shutai Yang,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文为BB1、BB2及固定正谱权变体在有限维严格凸二次函数上给出了R-线性收敛证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为BB1、BB2及固定正谱权变体建立严格的R-线性收敛理论，填补这些方法在严格凸二次问题上收敛速率分析的空缺。

Method: 将延迟递推改写为相容相邻梯度对闭锥上的一阶系统；其转移映射连续且正齐次一次（含有限终止）。利用紧性定理，由锥上每条轨道的逐点收敛推出一致有限步收缩，从而得到全局R-线性估计。通过实现引理，每个相容状态都启动一条BB1轨迹，结合Raydan定理得到整个锥上的逐点稳定性。最后用变换g↦W^{1/2}g将每个固定正谱权规则共轭为BB1。

Result: 证明了BB1、BB2及所有固定正谱权规则具有相同的齐次增长半径，并获得全局R-线性收敛估计与整个锥上的逐点稳定性。

Conclusion: BB1、BB2与固定正谱权变体在严格凸二次问题上共享同一齐次增长半径，因而具有统一的R-线性收敛行为。

Abstract: We give an $R$-linear convergence proof for BB1, BB2, and fixed positive spectral-weight variants on finite-dimensional strictly convex quadratics. The delayed recurrence is written as a first-order system on the closed cone of compatible consecutive-gradient pairs. Its transition is continuous and positively homogeneous of degree one, including at finite termination. A compactness theorem shows that pointwise convergence of every cone orbit implies a uniform finite-step contraction and hence a global $R$-linear estimate. By a realization lemma, every compatible state initiates a BB1 trajectory, so Raydan's theorem yields pointwise stability on the entire cone. Finally, the transformation $g\mapsto W^{1/2}g$ conjugates every fixed positive spectral-weight rule to BB1. Consequently, BB1, BB2, and all such weighted rules have the same homogeneous growth radius.

</details>


### [101] [Global and Unconditional $R$-Linear Convergence of Rank-Compressed Weighted LMSD Sweeps for Strictly Convex Quadratics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12114)
*Shutai Yang,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究用于严格凸二次优化的有限内存最速下降法（LMSD），采用精确代数秩压缩。若块起始梯度至多有 p 个活跃不同特征值，则延迟扫描有限终止；对非终止轨迹，通过完整扫描多项式的行列式与 Cauchy–Binet 公式证明全局收敛，并给出与初始状态一致的 R-线性端点估计，加权方法在谱衰减因子不变下仅使欧氏范数前因子至多增大 √κ₂(W)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 LMSD 及相关谱方法（如 harmonic-Ritz LMSD、BB 递推）的收敛性分析通常依赖于历史满秩或逐次运行的归一化条件数界，这些假设较强且限制理论适用范围。作者希望在不要求满秩历史、也不使用逐次条件数界的前提下，建立严格凸二次优化中带秩压缩 LMSD 的全局收敛与统一误差估计。

Method: 1）在每轮循环中将梯度历史限制到其列空间，并对压缩投影的所有 Ritz 值取倒数用于下一次扫描。2）对非终止轨迹，构造完整扫描多项式，利用行列式与 Cauchy–Binet 公式进行收敛分析。3）将相邻扫描端点视为闭锥上连续正齐次映射，借助紧性导出 R-线性端点估计。4）证明任意固定正谱权重 W=ω(H) 的加权方法与标准方法线性共轭。5）将估计推广到内梯度、迭代误差和目标间隙。

Result: 若块起始梯度至多有 p 个活跃不同特征值，延迟扫描可有限终止。对非终止轨迹，在不需要满秩历史或逐次归一化条件数界的情况下得到全局收敛。相邻扫描端点的连续正齐次映射在紧性下给出 R-线性端点估计，且对相容初始状态一致，常数仅依赖 H 与 p。该估计可扩展到内梯度、迭代误差及目标间隙。任意固定正谱权重 W=ω(H) 的加权方法与标准方法线性共轭，因此共享相同衰减因子，而欧氏范数前因子至多增大 √κ₂(W)，涵盖 harmonic-Ritz LMSD、固定幂权重以及延迟 BB1、BB2 递推。

Conclusion: 该工作为带精确代数秩压缩的 LMSD 提供了较一般且统一的收敛框架：有限终止与全局收敛条件更宽松，误差估计对相容初始状态一致并具有 R-线性速率；同时说明固定正谱权重方法在线性共轭意义下不改变谱衰减因子，仅以可控的欧氏前因子增加为代价，从而统一解释了包括 harmonic-Ritz LMSD 与延迟 BB1/BB2 在内的多种方法。

Abstract: We study limited memory steepest descent (LMSD) with exact algebraic rank compression for strictly convex quadratic optimization. At each cycle, the method restricts the gradient history to its column space and applies the reciprocals of all Ritz values of the compressed projection in the next sweep. If the block-start gradient has at most $p$ active distinct eigenvalues, the delayed sweep terminates finitely. For nonterminating trajectories, a determinant and Cauchy--Binet formula for the complete-sweep polynomial yields global convergence without a full-rank history or a run-wise normalized-conditioning bound. Consecutive sweep endpoints define a continuous positively homogeneous map on a closed cone of compatible states. Compactness then gives an $R$-linear endpoint estimate, uniform over compatible initial states, with constants depending only on $H$ and $p$; the estimate extends to inner gradients, iterate errors, and objective gaps. Every fixed positive spectral weight $W=ω(H)$ is linearly conjugate to the standard method. The weighted methods therefore share its decay factor, while the Euclidean-norm prefactor is increased by at most $\sqrt{κ_2(W)}$. This includes harmonic-Ritz LMSD, fixed power weights, and the delayed BB1 and BB2 recurrences.

</details>


### [102] [ADMM and Linearized ADMM for Weakly Convex Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12167)
*Shenghan Mei,Chengyu Ke,Yifei Lou,Miju Ahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对目标函数为光滑凸项与弱凸非光滑项之和的弱凸优化问题，提出基于ADMM的分离算法，并进一步线性化得到LADMM，证明其收敛到方向稳定点，实验验证了效率与解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱凸优化目标由光滑凸项与可能非光滑的弱凸项组成，直接求解困难；需要利用该结构设计可分解、子问题易解的算法，并解决光滑项更新需内层迭代求解、计算代价高的问题。

Method: 基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）构造分离框架，将两个分量的极小化解耦为可处理子问题；针对光滑项更新可能依赖内层迭代求解器的问题，对该项进行线性化，得到线性化ADMM（LADMM），其更新为廉价的单步更新。

Result: 在温和条件下，证明了ADMM与LADMM的子序列收敛性，收敛到方向稳定解；该方向稳定解与本文弱凸问题的临界点及Clarke稳定解等价。在两个低维测试函数与高维对数正则化逻辑回归模型上的数值实验表明算法计算高效，解质量与基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提ADMM与LADMM为弱凸复合优化提供了有效分离求解途径，LADMM以单步廉价更新避免内层迭代，具备理论收敛保证与良好数值表现，适用于高维非光滑弱凸问题。

Abstract: We study a class of weakly convex optimization problems in which the objective is the sum of a smooth convex term and a weakly convex term that may be nonsmooth. To exploit this structure, we develop a splitting technique based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), which decouples the minimization of the two components into tractable subproblems. Because the update associated with the smooth term may require an inner iterative solver, we further linearize this term, yielding a linearized ADMM (LADMM) scheme with an inexpensive one-step update. Under mild conditions, we establish the subsequence convergence of both ADMM and LADMM methods to directional stationary solutions, which are equivalent to critical points and Clarke stationary solutions for our weakly convex problem. Numerical experiments on two low-dimensional test functions and a high-dimensional logarithmic regularized logistic regression model demonstrate that the proposed approaches are computationally efficient and produce solutions of comparable quality to baseline methods.

</details>


### [103] [Linear Exponential Quadratic Gaussian Covariance Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12463)
*Chiran B. Cherian,Yasemin Isik,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间有限时域下的线性指数二次高斯（LEQG）协方差引导问题，将其解视为线性二次设定下高斯端点间的风险敏感薛定谔桥。作者证明最优控制器为线性状态反馈但无闭式表达，而是由满足代数方程（隐含依赖风险敏感参数）的对称矩阵参数化，并给出匹配噪声-输入通道情形下局部存在唯一性结果及数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险中性协方差引导已有闭式解，但风险敏感情形（LEQG）尚缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在将协方差引导推广到风险敏感框架，刻画风险敏感薛定谔桥的结构，并回答最优控制器是否存在、是否唯一等基础问题。

Method: 将LEQG协方差引导问题形式化为有限时域连续时间优化问题，寻求连接高斯端点的风险敏感薛定谔桥；证明最优控制器为线性状态反馈，由满足隐式代数方程的对称矩阵参数化；在噪声与输入通道匹配的情形下，以风险中性最优解为中心进行局部分析。

Result: 最优控制器虽仍为线性状态反馈，但不再具有闭式表达，而由求解代数方程的对称矩阵参数化，该结构显著推广了风险中性结果；在匹配噪声-输入通道情形下，证明了在已知风险中性最优解邻域内LEQG协方差引导问题解的存在唯一性；并给出数值示例。

Conclusion: LEQG协方差引导可统一理解为风险敏感薛定谔桥，其最优控制器结构较风险中性情形有本质推广；所建立的局部存在唯一性为风险敏感协方差引导的理论分析与数值求解提供了基础。

Abstract: We formulate and analyze the linear exponential quadratic Gaussian (LEQG) covariance steering problem in continuous time over a given deadline (finite time horizon). The solution for this problem can be seen as a risk-sensitive Schrödinger bridge between Gaussian endpoints in the linear quadratic setting. Unlike the risk-neutral case, the LEQG covariance steering controller--still a linear state feedback--can no longer be written in closed form. We show that the optimal controller is parameterized by a symmetric matrix solving an algebraic equation that encodes the implicit dependence on the risk-sensitivity parameter. We explain how the structure of this optimal controller significantly generalizes the existing results for the risk-neutral case. Building on these results, for the matched noise and input channel case, we prove the existence-uniqueness of solution for the LEQG covariance steering problem in the neighborhood of the known risk-neutral optimal solution. We give an illustrative numerical example.

</details>


### [104] [Learning-Augmented Optimization for Strategic Two-Echelon Spare Parts Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12524)
*Donato Maragno,Marco Caserta,Alberto Sinigaglia,Komlanvi Ametana,David Corredor Montenegro,Luca D'Angelo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种保守型两阶段备件库存网络设计框架，结合GNN集成代理模型、变邻域搜索与集合划分重组，在仅用精确模型报告性能的前提下，比纯精确评估基线提升30.5%综合节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段备件网络中，每个候选拓扑都需运行昂贵的库存优化模型，无法大量评估；而优化器会偏好"预测节省高"的候选，从而利用代理模型的乐观误差，导致最终网络实际表现被高估。因此需要既降低评估成本又抑制乐观偏差的设计方法。

Method: 1) 用图神经网络集成代理模型预测聚类节省，并以预测节省的下分位数作为搜索评分，抑制乐观误差；2) 代理模型在精确聚类评估结果上训练；3) 用变邻域搜索在站点聚类空间中搜索候选；4) 将搜索中发现的聚类通过集合划分重组，目标系数由代理模型给出；5) 最终网络仅由精确库存模型评估并据此报告性能。

Result: 在亚马逊北美网络246个履约中心的案例中：相比完全基于精确聚类评估的优化基线，综合节省提升30.5%，六次独立重复中服务水平约维持99.8%；在相同算力预算下，图代理引导搜索的精确节省均值高于表格型替代方案（两种评分方式下均成立）；保守评分同时提升两类代理的均值节省，并使被图代理高估的最终网络聚类占比从68%降至28%；此外，随机留出集性能不能完整刻画代理在优化过程中的质量——搜索产生的高分候选上，预测与排序精度均下降。

Conclusion: 保守的集成代理+搜索+重组框架可在保证服务水平的同时显著降低库存网络成本，且优于纯精确评估基线与表格型代理；研究同时提示：代理模型的离线（随机留出）精度不足以代表其在优化搜索中的质量，评估代理需关注搜索生成候选上的表现。

Abstract: We study the strategic design of a two-echelon spare-parts inventory network where evaluating each candidate topology requires an expensive inventory optimization model. The design partitions hundreds of sites into feasible clusters and selects a central replenishment site for each cluster to reduce costs while maintaining service levels. Because the optimizer favors candidates with high predicted savings, it can exploit optimistic surrogate errors.
  We develop a conservative framework combining a graph neural network ensemble, variable neighborhood search, and set-partitioning recombination. The surrogate is trained on exact cluster evaluations, while a lower quantile of ensemble-predicted savings guides the search to limit optimism. Clusters found during the search are recombined through set partitioning using surrogate-based objective coefficients. The resulting network is evaluated with the exact inventory model, and only this evaluation is used to report performance.
  In a case study of 246 fulfillment centers in Amazon's North American network, the framework improves combined savings by 30.5% over an optimization baseline based entirely on exact cluster evaluations, while maintaining approximately 99.8% service across six independent replications. Under equal computational budgets, graph-surrogate-guided search achieves higher mean exact savings than a tabular alternative under both scoring schemes. Conservative scoring improves mean savings for both surrogate classes and reduces the share of final-network clusters overestimated by the graph surrogate from 68% to 28%. Predictive and ranking accuracy deteriorate among search-generated candidates with high surrogate scores, indicating that random holdout performance can incompletely characterize surrogate quality during optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Discrete Potential Optimization for Absolute Value Equations: A Sign-Flip Framework with Polynomial Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12763)
*Cairong Chen,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出离散势优化（DPO）框架，将NP难的绝对值方程（AVE）$Ax-|x|=b$转化为符号向量集$\{-1,1\}^n$上的全局最大化问题，在$1$-范数条件下导出多项式时间的符号翻转算法，并将结果推广至线性互补问题（LCP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解AVE通常是NP难的，现有方法大多在连续变量空间中操作；唯一显著的例外是Rohn的sign-accord算法，但其最坏情况迭代复杂度为指数级$2^n$。为突破该瓶颈，作者转向离散的符号向量空间寻找新的优化视角与多项式时间算法。

Method: 建立离散势优化（DPO）框架：在符号向量集$\{-1,1\}^n$上定义离散势函数，在$\|A^{-1}\|_1<1/2$条件下证明AVE解恰为该势函数的全局最大化点。基于此给出统一的符号翻转算法框架（不含Rohn的sign-accord算法），包括单翻转机制（新的steepest与Gauss-Southwell规则）与全翻转更新（等价于经典GNM）。进一步通过对角缩放将条件放宽为谱半径$\rho(|A^{-1}|)$一致上界为$1/2$；并提出均匀随机化的$m$-flip方法，无需显式对角预处理。同时通过将LCP等价改写为AVE，把DPO框架推广至LCP。

Result: 在$\|A^{-1}\|_1$一致上界为$1/2$、输入为最大幅值$L$的有理数时：单翻转（steepest、Gauss-Southwell）在$\mathcal{O}(n^2\log(nL))$次迭代内终止；全翻转（GNM）仅需$\mathcal{O}(n\log(nL))$次；随机$m$-flip的期望迭代界为$\mathcal{O}(n^3\log(nL)/m)$。作为推论，GNM在$\mathcal{O}(n^2\log(nL))$次迭代内求解AVE，改进了此前在更严格条件$\rho(|A^{-1}|)<1/3$下仅有的有限终止结果。数值实验验证了GNM的实际效率与符号翻转方法的结构稳健性。

Conclusion: DPO框架将AVE的求解从指数级复杂度降至多项式级，统一并改进了符号翻转类算法；同时通过LCP到AVE的等价改写，为LCP提供具有多项式迭代复杂度的GNM与pivot型方法，兼具理论保证与实验有效性。

Abstract: Solving the AVE $Ax - |x| = b$ is generally NP-hard. Existing approaches mostly operate in continuous variable spaces, with the notable exception of Rohn's sign-accord algorithm, which incurs an exponential worst-case bound of $2^n$ iterations. To overcome this bottleneck, we develop a discrete potential optimization (DPO) framework over the sign-vector set $\{-1, 1\}^n$. Under the $1$-norm condition $\|A^{-1}\|_1 < 1/2$, we establish that the AVE solution corresponds exactly to the global maximizer of this discrete potential function. When $\|A^{-1}\|_1$ is uniformly upper bounded by $1/2$, for rational inputs with maximum magnitude $L$, we develop a unified polynomial-time framework for sign-flip algorithms, which excludes Rohn's sign-accord algorithm. Within this framework, specific single-flip mechanisms (including our new steepest and Gauss-Southwell rules) terminate in $\mathcal{O}(n^2\log(nL))$ iterations, while full-flip updates (equivalent to the classical GNM) require only $\mathcal{O}(n\log(nL))$ iterations. We further relax the assumption by requiring that the spectral radius $ρ(|A^{-1}|)$ be uniformly upper bounded by $1/2$ via rational diagonal scaling. Moreover, a uniformly randomized $m$-flip approach is proven to achieve an expected iteration bound of $\mathcal{O}\bigl(n^3 \log(nL)/m\bigr)$ without requiring explicit diagonal preconditioning. As a corollary, GNM solves the AVE in $\mathcal{O}(n^2 \log(nL))$ iterations, improving on the prior result of finite termination under the stricter condition that $ρ(|A^{-1}|)$ is less than~$1/3$. Crucially, by equivalently reformulating linear complementarity problems (LCPs) as AVEs, we extend this DPO framework to yield GNM and pivot-type methods with polynomial iteration complexity for LCPs. Numerical experiments validate the practical efficiency of GNM and the structural robustness of the proposed sign-flip approaches.

</details>


### [106] [High-Probability Convergence of SGD via Batched Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12765)
*Feng Zhu,Robert W. Heath,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 Batched SGD，将在线样本划分为多个 epoch 并用低方差梯度估计做单次更新，从而在无需受限假设与辅助序列的情况下获得最后迭代的高概率收敛保证，并推广到联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD 的均值行为（均方误差界）已被较好理解，但最后迭代的高概率保证仍具挑战：现有方法或依赖受限假设（有界域/有界梯度），或需要涉及辅助序列的复杂证明。本文旨在以简单机制同时规避这两类困难。

Method: 提出 Batched SGD：将在线样本按 epoch 分批，每个 epoch 仅执行一次更新，使用精炼的低方差梯度估计；基于该批处理机制构造简单的高概率分析。在标准光滑性与 norm-sub-Gaussian 噪声假设下进行分析，并将批处理思想自然扩展到联邦学习。

Result: 在强凸与非凸目标上均建立了近最优收敛速率；对联邦学习给出首个高概率保证，实现对数通信复杂度、随智能体数量的线性加速，以及对数据异构性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 批处理式梯度估计使最后迭代的高概率分析显著简化，同时避免受限假设与辅助序列，并在联邦学习中同时获得通信效率、线性加速与异构数据鲁棒性。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is the primary workhorse for large-scale optimization. While the average behavior of its iterates, typically characterized by mean-squared error bounds, is well-understood, obtaining high-probability guarantees for the last iterate remains challenging. Prior approaches to this problem have either imposed restrictive assumptions (such as bounded domains or gradients) or relied on complex proofs involving auxiliary sequences. In this work, we propose Batched SGD, a simple variant that partitions online samples into epochs and performs a single update per epoch using a refined, low-variance gradient estimate. Our main contribution demonstrates that this batching mechanism enables a surprisingly simple high-probability analysis that avoids both restrictive assumptions and auxiliary sequences. Under standard smoothness and norm-sub-Gaussian noise assumptions, we establish near-optimal rates for both strongly convex and non-convex objectives. Furthermore, we show that our batching idea extends naturally to federated learning (FL). We provide the first high-probability guarantees for FL, achieving logarithmic communication complexity, linear speedup in the number of agents, and resilience to data heterogeneity.

</details>


### [107] [Controlling the energy jump of multistable structures using shape optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12816)
*Arselane Hadj Slimane,Patrick E. Farrell,Alberto Paganini,Àlex Ferrer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多稳态超弹性超材料形状优化框架与算法，通过域形状优化调控snap-through能量跃变，突破经典形状优化单值解映射假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多稳态系统在相同载荷与边界条件下存在多个稳定平衡态，其形状优化有望定制先进材料与超材料的力学响应；但经典形状优化理论假设域到解为单值映射，在多稳态情形下不成立，故需扩展理论以处理非唯一解并实现能量跃变可控设计。

Method: 将域变形的缩放作为延拓参数，应用隐函数定理，给出随形状变化多解分支持续存在的充分条件；在此理论基础上构建面向指定能量跃变的实用形状优化算法。

Result: 建立可处理非唯一解的多稳态形状优化理论框架，并形成以规定稳定态间能量跃变为目标的算法，实现snap-through响应的按需系统化设计。

Conclusion: 该工作为多稳态超材料形状优化提供了理论基础与可操作算法，使snap-through能量跃变可被系统调控，支撑先进材料与超材料的力学响应定制。

Abstract: Multistable systems admit distinct stable equilibria for the same loading and boundary conditions. Shape optimization of multistable structures offers a promising route to engineer the mechanical response of advanced materials and metamaterials. In this work, we develop a formulation and algorithm for controlling the energy jump associated with snap-through behaviour in hyperelastic metamaterials by optimizing the shape of the domain. The key challenge is that classical shape optimization theory assumes a single-valued domain-to-solution map, which does not hold in the multistable setting. We address this by extending the theory to settings with non-unique solutions, treating the scaling of the domain deformation as a continuation parameter and applying the implicit function theorem to give sufficient conditions for the continued existence of multiple solution branches as the shape varies. This theoretical foundation underpins a practical algorithm that targets a prescribed energy jump between stable states, enabling the systematic design of structures whose snap-through response can be tuned on demand.

</details>


### [108] [Sampled-data optimal control of linear fractional-order systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12961)
*Pantelis Sopasakis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究Caputo线性分数阶系统(阶数α∈(0,1])在连续时间状态约束与输入约束下的最优控制问题,通过采样数据表示与Bernstein多项式构造状态约束的多面体逼近,并给出保守性显式界,通过细分采样区间使逼近任意紧致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶系统的最优控制中,状态约束需在整个采样区间而非仅采样时刻满足,导致问题呈半无限形式,难以直接求解。因此需要一种可行且可计算的方法来处理连续时间状态约束。

Method: 推导采样数据表示以刻画采样间轨迹;利用Bernstein多项式构造连续时间状态约束的多面体(polytopic)逼近;给出该逼近所引入保守性的显式界;对每个采样区间进行细分,并在每个子区间上应用Bernstein构造,使逼近精度可任意提高。

Result: 数值算例验证了所提包围(enclosure)的紧致性、方法的计算可处理性,以及逼近精度与计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理Caputo线性分数阶系统的半无限最优控制问题,在保证约束满足的同时兼顾精度与计算效率,可通过区间细分在精度与复杂度之间灵活折中。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of optimal control of Caputo linear fractional-order systems of order $α\in (0, 1]$ with continuous-time state constraints and input constraints. The resulting problems are semi-infinite, since the state constraints are imposed over the sampling intervals and not merely at the sampling instants. To address this, we derive a sampled-data representation of the inter-sample trajectory and use Bernstein polynomials to construct polytopic approximations of the continuous-time state constraints. Explicit bounds are obtained for the conservatism introduced by this approximation. By subdividing each sampling interval and applying the Bernstein-based construction on every subinterval, the approximation is made arbitrarily tight. Numerical examples illustrate the tightness of the proposed enclosures, the computational tractability of the proposed method, and the trade-off between approximation accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>

### [109] [A Splitting Method for SDE Terminal-Law Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12513)
*Rushil Gupta,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究通过分裂部分路径生成路径树以提升终端分布采样效率的问题，以KS距离刻画经验分布极限误差，给出分裂策略并验证10-25%误差改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机微分方程（含扩散生成AI）中，目标是从终端分布精确采样。给定固定模拟预算，相比独立同分布地生成扩散路径，通过对部分路径适当分裂生成路径树可能提升效率，但会引入样本间依赖性，因此需要系统研究该依赖带来的影响。

Method: 以Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离为精度度量，推导模拟预算趋于无穷时经验分布的极限误差；基于对应渐近优化问题刻画一种分裂策略；实际实现分为初始估计阶段与最终推断阶段两个阶段。

Result: 理论上揭示了问题优雅的内在结构；在许多设置下，相比独立同分布采样，平均误差获得10-25%的改善；在探索性CIFAR-10研究中，方法将最大均值差异（MMD）降低8-13%。

Conclusion: 路径树分裂策略在固定预算下可有效提升终端分布采样精度，尽管存在注入的依赖性，其极限误差仍可被刻画并优化，实验验证了相较i.i.d.采样的稳定增益。

Abstract: In many settings involving stochastic differential equations, including in diffusion based generative AI, our aim is to accurately generate samples from a terminal distribution. Typically, this is done by generating i.i.d. samples of diffusion paths. Given a fixed simulation budget, a reasonable way to gain efficiency may be to instead generate a tree of paths through appropriately split partial paths. This suggests improved performance, but one worries about the injected dependence. In this paper, we study this issue comprehensively. With Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance as a measure of accuracy, we identify the limiting errors of the associated empirical distributions as the simulation budget increases to infinity. We characterize a splitting strategy motivated by a corresponding asymptotic optimization problem. The theoretical results bring out the elegant underlying structure in the problem. Practical implementation involves two phases, an initial estimation phase and a final inference phase. Overall, we observe a 10-25% improvement in mean error over i.i.d. samples in many settings. In an exploratory CIFAR-10 study, our method reduces the maximum mean discrepancy by 8-13%.

</details>


### [110] [Inference for Newton Methods with Accelerated Sketch-and-Project via Random Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12421)
*Xinchen Du,Elizaveta Rebrova,Michał Dereziński,Sen Na*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出在线sketched牛顿法，用GAS求解器加速牛顿方向求解；建立平均迭代的渐近正态性与极限协方差，并给出函数型中心极限定理，实现无需协方差估计的在线推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二阶方法（牛顿法）每步计算牛顿方向需精确求解线性系统，构成计算瓶颈；同时在线推断中通常需要显式估计渐近协方差，代价高且困难。作者希望在高精度二阶更新的前提下降低计算开销，并实现无需协方差估计的在线推断。

Method: 1）采用广义加速sketch-and-project求解器（GAS）近似每一步的牛顿方向，GAS通过Nesterov动量更新实现加速收敛，并支持灵活投影度量以进一步降低计算成本；2）在此框架下对平均sketched牛顿迭代建立渐近正态性并刻画极限协方差矩阵；3）进一步建立牛顿迭代的函数型中心极限定理（FCLT）；4）基于随机缩放构造枢轴化检验统计量，对平均迭代适当重缩放，使其极限分布不含未知参数。

Result: 所得协方差矩阵在特定加速参数选择下退化为未加速sketched牛顿法的协方差；一般情况下，其在sketch步数意义上更快收敛到极小极大最优协方差，且小于加速方法最后迭代的协方差。基于FCLT的枢轴化统计量极限分布与未知参数无关，可实现渐近有效的在线推断。数值实验表明所提推断过程性能优越。

Conclusion: 该工作将加速sketching求解器与在线牛顿法结合，在保持二阶方法统计效率的同时缓解计算瓶颈，并通过渐近正态性和FCLT为在线推断提供理论保证，所得推断无需显式估计协方差，具有渐近有效性。

Abstract: We study an online sketched Newton method that approximates the Newton direction at each step via a state-of-the-art sketching solver, called the generalized accelerated sketch-and-project solver (GAS), thereby mitigating the computational bottleneck of classical second-order methods. The GAS solver improves upon vanilla, unaccelerated sketch-and-project solvers by achieving accelerated convergence through Nesterov momentum updates, and accommodates a flexible projection metric whose proper choice further reduces computational cost. Building on this design, we establish asymptotic normality of the averaged sketched Newton iterates and characterize their limiting covariance matrix. The resulting covariance recovers that of the unaccelerated sketched Newton method under a specific choice of acceleration parameters, converges more rapidly (in the number of sketching steps) to the minimax-optimal covariance in general, and is smaller than that of the last iterate produced by the accelerated method. Finally, we strengthen these results by establishing a functional central limit theorem for the Newton iterates, which allows us to bypass explicit covariance estimation and develop an online inference procedure based on random scaling. Specifically, we construct a pivotal test statistic by appropriately rescaling the averaged iterates, so that its limiting distribution is free of any unknown parameters, enabling asymptotically valid online inference. Numerical experiments demonstrate superior performance of the proposed inference procedure.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Interaction Kernels from Collective Steady States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12004)
*Baoli Hao,Mauro Maggioni,Ming Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从集体行为的单快照观测中辨识相互作用粒子系统的方法，通过基于观测构型经验分布的正则化策略求解本质不适定的反问题，并在多种稳态与准稳态模型上实现稳定、准确的相互作用规律恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统辨识方法通常依赖粒子轨迹观测，但在许多实际场景中只能获得集体行为的单张快照，无法追踪轨迹。这一限制使辨识问题变为根本性的不适定反问题，需要新的求解思路。

Method: 提出一种学习流程，用于从集体行为的单快照观测中辨识相互作用粒子系统的规律。针对该不适定反问题，采用基于观测构型经验分布的正则化策略，这些构型来自不同且未被观测到的初始条件。在具有稳态和准稳态模式的多种代表性模型上进行测试，其中集体行为隐含编码了相互作用机制的信息。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够稳定且准确地恢复底层相互作用规律，忠实再现集体行为，并且在许多情况下甚至能重现导致该集体行为的动力学过程。

Conclusion: 单快照观测结合基于经验分布的正则化，可有效解决粒子系统相互作用辨识中的不适定反问题，为仅凭集体行为快照推断微观相互作用机制提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a learning procedure for system identification in interacting particle systems from single-snapshot observations of collective behaviors, unlike existing approaches that rely on observations of trajectories. This setting leads to a fundamentally ill-posed inverse problem, which we solve by using a regularization strategy based on the empirical distribution of observed configurations, drawn from different, unobserved initial conditions. We test our learning procedure on a variety of representative models with steady-state and quasi-stationary patterns, where collective behaviors encode implicit information about the interaction mechanisms, demonstrating that our approach enables stable and accurate recovery of the underlying interaction laws, leading to faithful reproduction of the collective behavior, and in many cases even of the dynamics leading up to it.

</details>

### [112] [Large-data modified wave operators for the defocusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation in one space dimension with subcritical long-range nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12518)
*Masaki Kawamoto,Haruya Mizutani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维散焦型非线性薛定谔方程（NLS）在亚临界长程区域（2/√7<σ<1）下终态问题解的长时间行为，证明了修正波算子的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有幂次非线性|u|^{2σ}u的一维散焦NLS在亚临界长程区域下的修正波算子存在性，特别是对无尺寸限制的加权L²空间中给定渐近轮廓构造唯一整体散射解。

Method: 在渐近轮廓附近对NLS线性化，将非线性项主部作为线性势并入线性部分，证明线性化方程的全局修正能量估计；利用线性化产生的非线性结构所导致的临界抵消，控制非线性相位修正的多项式增长。

Result: 对加权L²空间中规定的渐近轮廓（由自由解经非线性多项式相位修正得到），构造了NLS的唯一全局解，该解散射到此轮廓，从而证明了修正波算子的存在性。

Conclusion: 扩展了此前三次情形的工作，将方法推广至亚临界长程区域，通过新发展的修正能量估计与结构抵消机制，成功证明了修正波算子的存在性。

Abstract: We study long-time behavior of the solutions to the final state problem for the defocusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLS) in one space dimension with the power nonlinearity $|u|^{2σ}u$ in the subcritical long-range regime $\frac{2}{\sqrt{7}}<σ<1$. Given a prescribed asymptotic profile in a weighted $L^2$-space, without size restriction, obtained by modifying the free solution with a nonlinear polynomial phase correction, we construct a unique global solution of the NLS that scatters to this profile, thereby proving the existence of modified wave operators. The proof relies on two new ingredients. Extending our previous work for the cubic case, we incorporate the leading part of the nonlinear term into the linear part as a linear potential by linearizing the NLS around the asymptotic profile and prove a global modified energy estimate for the linearized equation. We also exploit a specific structure of the nonlinearity arising from the linearization, which gives rise to a crucial cancellation when estimating the nonlinear terms in the modified energy space and enables us to control the polynomial growth of the nonlinear phase correction in the subcritical case.

</details>


### [113] [Low Mach number limit of the compressible Euler--Vlasov--Fokker--Planck system in the whole space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12648)
*Fucai Li,Jinkai Ni,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上解决了可压缩等熵欧拉--弗拉索夫--福克--普朗克（Euler--VFP）系统的低马赫数极限问题，通过建立对马赫数 $\varepsilon$ 一致的整体先验估计，并构造修正声学变量，证明了可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统到不可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统在 $H^2$ 拓扑下以 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon)$ 速率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩与不可压缩流体--粒子相互作用模型各有重要进展，但如何通过低马赫数极限将二者联系起来仍是富有挑战性的公开问题。本文旨在针对可压缩等熵 Euler--VFP 系统在全空间 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上解决这一开放问题。

Method: 首先在全局 Maxwell 分布附近建立对马赫数 $\varepsilon$ 一致的整体强解先验估计。证明依赖精细的能量方法，结合流体--粒子相互作用诱导的松弛结构 $b^\varepsilon-u^\varepsilon$ 与可压缩欧拉部分的对称化声学结构。在良态初始数据假设下，引入修正声学变量 $q^\varepsilon-\varepsilon[P'(1)]^{-1}\pi$ 以刻画压力修正项，利用奇异声学项的精确抵消来封闭整体误差估计。

Result: 在良态初始数据假设下，得到可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统解与极限不可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统解之间 $H^2$ 框架下整体一致误差估计；证明低马赫数极限的收敛速率为 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon)$（时间连续 $H^2$ 拓扑）。阻尼项 $b^\varepsilon-u^\varepsilon$ 在恢复相对速度耗散、控制耦合流体--粒子动力学中起关键作用。

Conclusion: 成功建立了可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上低马赫数极限的严格理论，证明其以 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon)$ 速率收敛到不可压缩 Euler--VFP 系统，为连接可压缩与不可压缩流体--粒子模型提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Although there are many important contributions on compressible and incompressible fluid-particle interaction models respectively, how to connect the two-type fluid-particle models via the low Mach number limit remains a challenging open problem. In this paper, we resolve it for the compressible isentropic fluid-particle model (Euler--Vlasov--Fokker--Planck (Euler--VFP) system) in the whole space $\mathbb{R}^3$. First, we establish the global-in-time {\it a priori estimates} of strong solutions that are uniform with respect to the Mach number $\varepsilon$ near the global Maxwellian. The proof relies on a refined energy method that combines the relaxation structure $b^\varepsilon-u^\varepsilon$ induced by the fluid-particle interaction and the symmetrized acoustic structure of the compressible Euler part in the model. Under the assumption of well-prepared initial data, we derive a {\it global-in-time} uniform error estimate in the $H^2$ framework between the solution of the compressible Euler--VFP system and that of the limiting incompressible Euler--VFP system. A key point is to introduce the corrected acoustic variable $ q^\varepsilon-\varepsilon [P'(1)]^{-1}π$, which captures the pressure corrector in the low Mach number limit. This also allows us to exploit the exact cancellation of the singular acoustic terms and to close the {\it global-in-time} error estimate. The damping term $b^\varepsilon-u^\varepsilon$, which is absent in the pure Euler equations, plays an essential role in recovering the relative velocity dissipation and in controlling the coupled fluid-particle dynamics. As a consequence, we prove the low Mach number limit of the compressible Euler--VFP system with the convergence rate $\mathcal O(\varepsilon)$ in the time-continuous $H^2$ topology.

</details>


### [114] [Weak Solutions for the Unregularised Hibler Momentum Equation with Degenerate Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12925)
*Henrik Schneider*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究退耦未正则化Hibler海冰模型的动量方程，允许冰质量与冰强度非负且可为零，利用海洋拖曳提供矫顽性，将应力律与混合边界条件编码为凸耗散泛函，证明了时间离散问题的存在性、唯一性与稳定性，并通过Rothe逼近构造全局弱变分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hibler海冰模型中冰质量与冰强度可能为零（如开阔水域或薄冰区），导致传统正则化方法失效；需在未正则化、退耦情形下建立动量方程的适定性理论，并处理时间相关系数与弱解结构问题。

Method: 将应力律与混合边界条件表示为凸耗散泛函；对时间离散问题证明存在性、唯一性与稳定性并重构容许应力；采用Rothe逼近（时间离散+空间逼近）在质量单侧增长条件下构造全局弱变分解；对空间Lipschitz冰强度分析加权变形的局部测度结构与加权散度负部全局界。

Result: 在冰质量单侧增长条件下，得到时间相关系数的全局弱变分解；当冰强度与时间无关时弱解唯一；对空间Lipschitz冰强度，有限耗散蕴含加权变形的局部测度结构，并给出加权散度负部的全局界。

Conclusion: 该工作为未正则化Hibler海冰动量方程建立了完整的适定性框架，覆盖冰质量/强度消失的退化情形，并揭示了有限耗散下解的测度正则性，为海冰数值模拟提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the momentum equation of the decoupled unregularised Hibler sea-ice model with non-negative ice mass and ice strength, both of which may vanish. The ocean drag provides coercivity, while the stress law and mixed boundary conditions are encoded by a convex dissipation functional. We prove existence, uniqueness and stability for the time-discrete problem and reconstruct an admissible stress. A Rothe approximation yields global weak variational solutions for time-dependent coefficients under a one-sided growth condition on the mass. Weak solutions are unique when the ice strength is independent of time. For spatially Lipschitz ice strength, finite dissipation also yields a local measure structure of the weighted deformation and a global bound on the negative part of the weighted divergence.

</details>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [A free-surface-only closure model for linear waves on general deep-water flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12995)
*Emanuele Zuccoli,Edward James Brambley,Dwight Barkley*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一组二维自由面方程，用于研究线性深水表面波在一般三维稳态背景流上的传播，无需平坦自由面与无旋假设，模型显著简化且计算快数个量级，并在两个测试问题上与线性化欧拉方程高度吻合，首次发现足够陡的二维Gerstner波不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性深水表面波在一般三维稳态背景自由面流上的传播，避免对背景自由面平坦性或背景无旋性的限制，以克服传统方法的理论复杂性与计算成本。

Method: 在基流自由面上评估线性化欧拉方程，并提出一个闭合条件来处理自由面处的垂向导数；将结果推广到近期的纯旋转背景流结果，最终得到在二维自由面上求解的五个耦合一阶偏微分方程，涉及五个未知量：水平与垂向扰动速度、自由面扰动高度、以及扰动压力在表面沿深度的梯度。

Result: 以两个测试问题验证模型：具有平坦自由面的单向涡旋随深度变化的基流，以及行进Gerstner波解的扰动。计算线性化欧拉方程的特征值与特征函数并与模型对比，二者吻合极好；并首次发现足够陡的二维Gerstner波是不稳定的。

Conclusion: 该二维自由面模型在理论简化和计算效率上具有显著优势，且能准确刻画线性深水表面波在一般三维背景流上的传播；其预测与线性化欧拉方程高度一致，并揭示陡峭二维Gerstner波的失稳现象。

Abstract: We present a novel, spatially two-dimensional (2D) set of equations to study the propagation of linear deep-water surface waves over a general steady three-dimensional (3D) background free surface flow. No assumptions of a flat background free surface, nor of an irrotational background flow, are made. The resulting model is not only a significant theoretical simplification, but also results in orders of magnitude faster computations. The linearized Euler equations are evaluated on the free surface of the base flow, and a closure condition is proposed to account for the vertical derivatives at the free surface. This generalizes a recent result for purely rotating background flows (Zuccoli, Brambley & Barkley, 2025, arXiv:2405.12078). The final model consists of five coupled first order partial differential equations (PDEs) to be solved on the 2D free surface, involving five unknowns: the horizontal and vertical perturbation velocities; the free surface perturbation height; and unexpectedly the gradient of perturbation pressure with depth at the surface. Two test problems are used to validate the model: a uni-directional vortical depth-varying base flow with a flat free surface; and perturbations to a travelling Gerstner wave solution. Eigenvalues and eigenfunctions of the linearized Euler equations are computed and compared with those of the model. Results show remarkable agreement between the two. Our study finds for the first time, to the best of our knowledge, that sufficiently steep two-dimensional Gerstner waves are unstable.

</details>

### [116] [Abundance theorem for log surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12478)
*Makoto Enokizono,Nao Moriyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在对数曲面情形下，去掉 $\mathbb{Q}$-factoriality 与 log canonicity 假设，证明了 abundance 定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 abundance 定理通常需要 $\mathbb{Q}$-factoriality 或 log canonicity 等假设，这些条件限制了对数曲面理论的应用范围；本文意在放宽这些限制。

Method: 在一般对数曲面的框架下证明 abundance 定理，不再预设 $\mathbb{Q}$-factoriality 与 log canonicity。

Result: 在不假设 $\mathbb{Q}$-factoriality 与 log canonicity 的前提下，对数曲面的 abundance 定理成立。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了既有对数曲面 abundance 定理的适用范围，表明相关结论在更一般的奇异与边界条件下依然成立。

Abstract: We establish the abundance theorem for log surfaces without assuming $\mathbb{Q}$-factoriality or log canonicity.

</details>


### [117] [Crepant resolutions of fiber products and Kummer fibrations of rational elliptic surfaces with non-reduced fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12522)
*Dominik Burek,Sławomir Cynk*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究有理椭圆曲面纤维积 \(X=S_1\times_{\mathbb{P}^1}S_2\) 的局部模型与crepant消解，计算消解纤维的不可约分支数与Euler示性数，并在全局兼容假设下给出Hodge数公式；另讨论Kummer商及其在特征5、7约化下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有理椭圆曲面的纤维积及Kummer商是构造Calabi–Yau型或高维特殊簇的重要来源，但其局部奇点结构与crepant消解的存在性、投射性以及拓扑不变量尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其对非约化Kodaira纤维等情形仍需明确分类。

Method: 在特征零代数闭域上，对截面的有理椭圆曲面 \(S_1,S_2\) 的纤维积，逐对分析Kodaira纤维（含非约化纤维）所产生的局部方程；对容许局部模型构造crepant消解，记录投射性，并计算消解中心纤维的不可约分支数与Euler示性数。对Kummer商则通过多截面正规化纤维积的monodromy轨道描述分析其固定曲线。在显式全局兼容假设下，由局部数据给出光滑投射消解纤维积的Hodge数公式，并记录特征5、7约化后的相应推论。

Result: 给出了由Kodaira纤维对产生的局部方程与容许模型的crepant消解，标注每个构造是否投射，并计算消解中心纤维的不可约分支数和Euler示性数；仅在正常Gorenstein且受terminal factorial障碍覆盖的情形断言不存在，其余记为开放。全局兼容假设下由局部数据给出光滑投射消解纤维积的Hodge数公式；Kummer商的固定曲线通过monodromy轨道描述得到分析；并记录特征5、7约化后的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了有理椭圆曲面纤维积的局部crepant消解框架与不变量计算，明确了存在性/非存在性边界与开放情形，并在兼容条件下导出Hodge数公式，同时为Kummer商及正特征约化提供了可迁移的局部—全局方法。

Abstract: Let $S_1$ and $S_2$ be rational elliptic surfaces with section over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero, and let $X=S_1\times_{\mathbb{P}^1}S_2$. We determine the local equations produced by pairs of Kodaira fibers, including non-reduced fibers, and construct crepant resolutions for the admissible local models. For every construction we record whether it is projective and compute the number of irreducible components and the Euler characteristic of the resolved central fiber. Non-existence is asserted only in the normal Gorenstein cases covered by the terminal factorial obstruction; the remaining entries are stated as open for the methods of this paper. Under an explicit global compatibility hypothesis, the local data give formulas for the Hodge numbers of a smooth projective resolved fiber product. We also study Kummer quotients by fiberwise involutions. Their fixed curves are analyzed through the monodromy-orbit description of normalized fiber products of multisections. The final section records what the same constructions imply after reduction in characteristics $5$ and $7$, without interpreting characteristic-$p$ Euler and Picard data as complex Hodge numbers.

</details>


### [118] [Spectrum of singularities, analytic torsion and Calabi--Yau degenerations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12849)
*Dennis Eriksson,Gerard Freixas i Montplet*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过全纯解析挠率将奇点谱与退化Calabi–Yau流形几何联系起来，用消失圈的Hodge不变量表出解析挠率的渐近系数，提出高阶Durfee–Saito猜想，证明拟齐次奇点的谱测度在凸序下被Irwin–Hall分布支配，并用挠率公式构造BCOV不变量障碍，排除若干情形下的双有理光滑填充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立退化Calabi–Yau流形的奇点谱与几何之间的定量联系，用解析挠率作为桥梁，将奇点的Hodge理论信息转化为可计算的渐近系数和不变量，从而回答双有理光滑填充的存在性问题。

Method: 计算全纯微分形式解析挠率的首阶渐近系数，并将其表示为消失圈的Hodge理论不变量；对孤立超曲面奇点推导Hodge滤过维数的界；对孤立拟齐次奇点比较谱测度与Irwin–Hall分布的凸序；将挠率公式应用于BCOV不变量，得到双有理光滑填充的障碍，其局部贡献由Hertling谱方差和由单值性决定的Bernoulli和表示。

Result: 对孤立超曲面奇点提出新的高阶Durfee–Saito猜想，用Milnor数与Eulerian数界Hodge滤过维数；证明孤立拟齐次奇点的谱测度在凸序下被Irwin–Hall分布支配；给出可计算的双有理光滑填充障碍，并在总空间光滑、中心纤维仅含ADE或终端Brieskorn–Pham奇点、相对维数至少三的情形下排除双有理光滑填充，推广了Voisin的结果。

Conclusion: 解析挠率是联系奇点谱与Calabi–Yau退化几何的有效工具，由此得到的挠率公式既产生新的Hodge维数猜想，也给出BCOV不变量的可计算障碍，从而在相对维数至少三时排除一类退化的双有理光滑填充，扩展了Voisin的结论。

Abstract: We relate the spectrum of singularities to the geometry of degenerating Calabi--Yau manifolds through holomorphic analytic torsion. We express the leading asymptotic coefficients of the analytic torsion of holomorphic differential forms in terms of Hodge-theoretic invariants of vanishing cycles. For isolated hypersurface singularities, these formulas lead to the formulation of new higher Durfee--Saito conjectures bounding Hodge-filtration dimensions by the Milnor number and Eulerian numbers. We also prove that, for isolated quasi-homogeneous singularities, the spectral measure is dominated in convex order by the corresponding Irwin--Hall distribution. Applying the torsion formulas to the BCOV invariant yields a computable obstruction to birational smooth fillings. For isolated singularities, its local contribution is expressed in terms of Hertling's spectral variance and a Bernoulli sum determined by monodromy. As applications, we rule out birational smooth fillings for projective Calabi--Yau degenerations with smooth total space and singular central fiber having only ADE or terminal Brieskorn--Pham singularities, in relative dimension at least three, extending results of Voisin.

</details>


### [119] [Descent, Seminormalization, and Conductors of Sparse Profile Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12854)
*Yangcheng Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为稀疏因子分解剖面像建立下降理论，判定哪些归一化上的函数可下降到实际像；对同质掩码给出Fourier零分类及尖锐稳定逆定理，对混合非周期剖面给出Fourier线分类，并通过半群模型与单点-补剖面刻画像代数、导子与深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稀疏因子分解剖面像的下降问题：归一化空间上的函数何时能下降到真实像上；并厘清归一化、极化、点纤维、半归一化乃至源导子等不变量对实际像的刻画能力及局限性。

Method: 建立稀疏因子分解剖面像的下降理论；采用整纤维完备化方法，对同质掩码作Fourier零分类并证明尖锐稳定逆命题，对给定标记稳定范围内的混合非周期剖面作Fourier线分类；对同质剖面用有界整数关系确定几何纤维与半归一化，并用显式半群模型计算实际像代数、导子与深度；对单点-补剖面用精确单射流条件确定有限输出代数、导子及穿过第一非正规边界的射影下降，并在光滑边界范围与第一非正规层的全零点计算局部类型；此外证明饱和单位根特化定理。

Result: 得到同质掩码的Fourier零分类（含尖锐稳定逆定理）与混合非周期剖面的Fourier线分类；同质剖面的几何纤维与半归一化由有界整数关系决定，显式半群模型在若干族中给出实际像代数、导子与深度；单点-补剖面的有限输出代数、导子与射影下降由精确单射流条件决定，并在光滑边界范围及第一非正规层全零点算出局部类型；同时给出饱和单位根特化定理及一组分离性例子。

Conclusion: 归一化、自然极化、点纤维、半归一化甚至源导子均不足以决定实际像，需借助整纤维完备化、Fourier分类与单射流条件等更精细工具；上述分类与半群模型在相应稳定范围内给出实际像代数、导子、深度及局部类型的完整刻画。

Abstract: We develop a descent theory for sparse factorization-profile images, asking which functions on a normalization descend to the actual image. Whole-fiber completions yield a Fourier-zero classification for homogeneous masks, with a sharp stable converse, and a Fourier-line classification for mixed aperiodic profiles in the stated marked stable ranges. For homogeneous profiles, bounded integer relations determine geometric fibers and seminormalization, while explicit semigroup models compute actual image algebras, conductors, and depth in substantial families. For singleton--complement profiles, exact one-jet conditions determine the finite-output algebra, conductor, and projective descent through the first nonnormal boundary; the local type is computed in the smooth-boundary range and at the total-zero point of the first nonnormal layer. We also prove a saturated root-of-unity specialization theorem and give separating examples showing that normalization, natural polarizations, point fibers, seminormalization, and even the source conductor need not determine the actual image.

</details>


### [120] [Clifford Realizations of Hyperbolic Cubics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12957)
*Tim Netzer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了五元三次型的广义Lax猜想：其双曲锥是谱面体的，并给出了基于Clifford代数完全正映射的构造方法及若干推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Lax猜想断言双曲多项式的双曲锥具有谱面体实现，此前仅对至多四元的形式及任意元数的对称形式获证，五元三次型这一关键情形尚未解决。

Method: 通过Clifford代数上的完全正映射构造谱面体实现，借助若干极端三次型的四元数行列式表示，并利用Clifford可实现三次型集合的凸性推广到全部五元三次型；同时建立任意维数下Clifford可实现的充分判据。

Result: 证明五元三次型的双曲锥均为谱面体；新方法覆盖此前所有已知的三次型情形（至多四元及任意元数对称形式）；获得三次型范数球原点邻域内Clifford可实现的充分条件；并证明四元稀疏双曲四次型具有谱面体性。

Conclusion: 该工作将广义Lax猜想在三次型上推进到五元情形，确立了Clifford代数方法作为构造谱面体实现的有效框架，并拓展至稀疏双曲四次型。

Abstract: We prove the generalized Lax conjecture for cubics in five variables: their hyperbolicity cones are spectrahedral. Our approach constructs spectrahedral realizations from completely positive maps on Clifford algebras. The construction passes through quaternionic determinantal representations of certain extremal cubics. Convexity of the set of Clifford-realizable cubics then yields the result for all cubics in five variables. The approach also covers all previously known cubic cases of the generalized Lax conjecture: forms in at most four variables and symmetric forms in arbitrarily many variables. We further establish a sufficient criterion for Clifford realizability in arbitrary dimension and use it to obtain a neighborhood of the origin in the cubic norm ball consisting of Clifford-realizable cubics. Finally, we prove spectrahedrality for sparse hyperbolic quartics in four variables.

</details>


### [121] [Positivity properties of Schur classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13025)
*Matt Larson,Alan Stapledon*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将Fulton-Lazarsfeld与Ross-Toma关于丰富及nef向量丛Schur类的正性结果推广到纯代数框架：若射影丛环在适当锥下满足Kähler package，则其Schur类满足Hodge-Riemann关系的版本，并由此证明拟阵的Schur系数非负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fulton-Lazarsfeld和Ross-Toma已证明丰富与nef向量丛的Schur类具有显著的正性性质，但这些结果依赖于复代数几何的解析/几何工具。作者希望剥离几何假设，在纯代数（环论/组合）框架下重建该正性理论，从而拓展其适用范围并连通拟阵等组合对象。

Method: 引入"射影丛环"（p rojective bundle ring）这一抽象代数结构，它类似于光滑复射影簇上射影丛的上同调环；在给定适当锥（cone）的前提下，假设该环满足Kähler package（包括Poincaré对偶、硬Lefschetz、Hodge-Riemann双线性关系等），进而推导其关联Schur类所满足的Hodge-Riemann关系变体。

Result: 在射影丛环满足Kähler package的条件下，证明了Schur类满足Hodge-Riemann关系的推广版本；该结果即便对光滑复射影簇上丰富向量丛的射影化也是新的；作为应用，证明了拟阵（matroid）的Schur系数非负。

Conclusion: Schur类的正性性质可由抽象的Kähler package公理刻画，无需依赖复几何的具体构造；该代数化框架统一并强化了既有结果，并给出拟阵Schur系数非负性这一组合学推论。

Abstract: Results of Fulton-Lazarsfeld and Ross-Toma show that Schur classes of ample and nef vector bundles have remarkable positivity properties. We generalize these results to a purely algebraic setting. We show that if a projective bundle ring, a ring which resembles the cohomology ring of a projective bundle over a smooth complex projective variety, has the Kähler package with respect to a suitable cone, then the associated Schur classes satisfy a version of the Hodge-Riemann relations. This result is new even for the projectivization of an ample vector bundle over a smooth complex projective variety. We apply this result to prove that Schur coefficients of matroids are nonnegative.

</details>


### [122] [A Higher dimensional log Riemann--Hurwitz inequality and rigidity of covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13081)
*Donu Arapura,Chikako Mese,Deepam Patel*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了对数情形下满支撑反常层欧拉示性数正性，导出对数Riemann-Hurwitz不等式，并应用于半阿贝尔簇与曲线模空间的自同构刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在arXiv:2408.15788中已得到较弱的不等式；本文旨在给出严格版本，并将其应用于对数Riemann-Hurwitz型不等式以及模空间有限自映射的刚性（如曲线模空间上有限满自映射必为同构）。

Method: 设(Z̅,D)为带简单正规交叉除子的光滑射影簇，Ω^1_{Z̅}(log D) nef，Y⊂Z=Z̅\D为光滑闭子簇且欧拉示性数非零；对Y上满支撑反常层P，证明χ(Y,P)>0。将其用于有限直像的迹零部分，推导对数Riemann-Hurwitz不等式；并验证半阿贝尔簇子簇及M̅_{g,n}满足nef假设。

Result: 得到χ(Y,P)>0；若dim Y=n，任意次数d的光滑源有限满态射f:X→Y满足(-1)^nχ(X)≥d(-1)^nχ(Y)，差为显式非负相交数之和；当(-1)^nχ(Y)>0时，χ(X)=χ(Y)蕴含f为同构。进一步验证了半阿贝尔簇子簇与M̅_{g,n}的nef假设，从而带level结构曲线模空间的有限满自映射必为同构。

Conclusion: 满支撑反常层正性给出了对数Riemann-Hurwitz不等式的严格形式，并产生模空间的刚性结论：在适当正性条件下，有限满自映射只能是同构。

Abstract: Let $\overline{Z}$ be a smooth projective variety with an simple normal crossing divisor $D$ such that $Ω_{\overline{Z}}^1(\log D)$ is nef. We prove that if $Y\subset Z:=\overline Z\setminus D$ is a smooth closed subvariety with nonzero Euler characteristic, and $P$ is a perverse sheaf on $Y$ with full support, then $χ(Y,P)>0$. This is a strict version of an inequality obtained in arXiv:2408.15788. Applying this to the trace-zero part of a finite direct image yields a logarithmic Riemann--Hurwitz inequality: if $Y$ has dimension $n$, any finite surjective morphism $f\colon X\to Y$ of degree $d$ with $X$ smooth satisfies $(-1)^nχ(X)\ge d\,(-1)^nχ(Y)$, the difference being an explicit sum of nonnegative intersection numbers. When $(-1)^nχ(Y)>0$ this forces any such $f$ with $χ(X)=χ(Y)$ to be an isomorphism. We verify the nef hypothesis for subvarieties of semiabelian varieties, and for $\overline{\mathscr M}_{g,n}$---the moduli of curves, obtaining in particular that every finite surjective self-morphism of a moduli space of curves with level structure is an isomorphism.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Transform before linearizing: robust Newton methods for singular $p$-Laplace and $p$-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12241)
*Gonzalo G. De Diego,Fortino Garcia,Georg Stadler,Kewei Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 通过提升通量变量并对本构关系施加非线性变换，构造了一种求解p-Laplace及p-Stokes问题的新迭代法，可全局收敛、局部二次收敛，且迭代次数对p与网格几乎不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当1<p<2时，p-Laplace与p-Stokes方程数值求解困难：牛顿法仅在解附近快速收敛，迭代次数随网格加密增加且随p→1而恶化；Picard迭代虽稳健但仅线性收敛。作者不采用全局化或预处理牛顿法，而是直接改造牛顿法所作用的方程组结构。

Method: 先提升（lift）方程，引入通量|∇u|^{p-2}∇u作为辅助变量；再对本构关系做非线性变换；随后线性化，并通过静力凝聚（static condensation）代数地、逐点地在求积点上消去辅助变量。由于消去是逐点代数的，通量变量无需离散化，不产生inf-sup条件或不定系统，每次迭代代价与标准牛顿步相同；同时对提升变量施加逐点可行性界，保持扩散张量一致正定。

Result: 在有限维、p-Laplace情形下证明该迭代全局收敛且局部二次收敛；一维模型问题解释了滞后通量变量为何能消除p接近1时标准牛顿法的锯齿（zig-zag）行为；基于Firedrake的二维、三维p-Laplace及定常与时间依赖p-Stokes实验显示，迭代次数对p和网格加密基本不敏感，p接近1时迭代次数比标准牛顿法少至多一个数量级。

Conclusion: 关键并非提升本身（提升不改变线性化），而是提升前的非线性变换；结合滞后通量与逐点可行性界，可在保持标准牛顿单步代价的前提下，获得对p和网格均鲁棒的高效迭代方法。

Abstract: For $1<p<2$, the $p$-Laplace equation and its $p$-Stokes generalization are difficult to solve numerically. Newton's method converges rapidly only close to the solution, with iteration counts that grow under mesh refinement and deteriorate as $p\to1$. The more robust Picard iteration converges only linearly. Rather than globalizing or preconditioning Newton's method, we modify the system to which it is applied. We \emph{lift} the equation by introducing the flux $|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u$ as an auxiliary (or ''lifting'') variable, apply a nonlinear \emph{transformation} to the resulting constitutive relation, \emph{linearize}, and \emph{eliminate} the auxiliary variable by static condensation. Lifting alone leaves the linearization unchanged; it is the preceding transformation that yields the new method. The elimination is algebraic and pointwise at the quadrature points, so the flux variable is never discretized, no inf-sup condition or indefinite system arises, and the cost per iteration is that of a standard Newton step. Together with a pointwise feasibility bound on the lifting variable that keeps the diffusion tensor uniformly positive definite, this yields an iteration that we prove, in finite dimensions and for the $p$-Laplace equation, to converge globally and locally at a quadratic rate; a one-dimensional model problem explains why the lagged flux variable removes the zig-zag behavior of standard Newton for $p$ close to one. Firedrake-based experiments for $p$-Laplace problems in two and three dimensions and for stationary and time-dependent $p$-Stokes flows show iteration counts largely insensitive to $p$ and to mesh refinement, and up to an order of magnitude fewer iterations than standard Newton for $p$ close to one.

</details>


### [124] [Error bounds for the Sherman-Morrison formula and its modification with improved stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12266)
*Behnam Hashemi,Yuji Nakatsukasa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出改进的Sherman-Morrison方法MSM，通过Govaerts算法实现自带自校正，只需3次n×n线性系统求解（标准SM为2次，SMIR为2+k次），并给出SM与MSM的前后向误差界及可计算增长因子，实验显示MSM数值稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Sherman-Morrison（SM）公式数值不稳定，现有改进方法SMIR依赖迭代精化，其收敛速度可能很慢，所需求解次数2+k中k可能远大于3。因此需要一种兼具稳定性与效率的替代算法。

Method: 改编Govaerts针对一般加边线性系统的算法，构造改进的Sherman-Morrison（MSM）方法，利用其内建自校正机制提升稳定性；随后推导SM与MSM的后向和前向误差界，并提取可廉价后验计算的增长因子用于认证稳定性。

Result: MSM仅需3次n×n线性系统求解，优于SMIR的2+k次；所得SM后向误差界比[Hashemi & Nakatsukasa 2026]更强，覆盖所有舍入误差且对电容大小无条件限制；实验表明MSM始终产生后向稳定解，并因此前向稳定。

Conclusion: MSM在效率与数值稳定性上均表现良好，是可替代SM与SMIR的有效方法；但MSM稳定性的形式化证明（或反证）仍是未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: It is known that the Sherman--Morrison (SM) formula is not numerically stable. In recent work, we introduced SMIR, an algorithm that incorporates iterative refinement to enhance the SM backward error. In this paper we take a different route: adapting an algorithm of Govaerts, originally designed for general bordered linear systems, we develop a modified Sherman--Morrison (MSM) method whose built-in self-correction makes it surprisingly resilient. Whereas standard SM requires the solution of two $n\times n$ linear systems, MSM requires three; by contrast, SMIR requires $2+k$ solves, where $k$ is the number of IR steps and can be substantially larger than three, when IR converges slowly. We then derive backward and forward error bounds for both SM and MSM. The SM backward error bound established here is stronger than the one proved in [Hashemi \& Nakatsukasa 2026]: it accounts for every rounding error and holds with no conditions on the size of the capacitance. From these bounds we extract growth factors that are cheap to compute a posteriori and can be used to certify the backward and forward stability of SM and MSM on a given problem. In our experiments, MSM consistently produces backward stable solutions, and is therefore observed to be forward stable as well. A formal proof of the stability --- or instability --- of MSM remains an open problem.

</details>


### [125] [Sharp Error Estimates for a Fully Discrete Finite Element Method for Semilinear SPDEs with Multiplicative Noise and Nonsmooth Initial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12485)
*Jitendra Nath Naik,Lok Pati Tripathi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为乘性噪声与非光滑初值下的半线性抛物SPDE全离散逼近建立了尖锐强误差估计，空间用标准有限元、时间用线性隐式Euler格式，通过将扩散算子映射到负分数阶空间统一处理trace-class与时空白噪声，解耦噪声正则性参数β与初值正则性参数μ，得到尖锐正则性估计并将初值正则性损失隔离为可积时间奇异；在远离t=0处获得强收敛率O(h^{β−ε}+k^{½min{β−ε,1}})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性抛物SPDE的数值逼近在非光滑初值与乘性噪声同时存在时，误差分析面临噪声正则性与初值正则性耦合的困难，已有结果往往无法给出尖锐的强收敛率，也难以统一覆盖trace-class与时空白噪声两种情形。

Method: 采用标准有限元进行空间离散，时间方向采用线性隐式Euler格式；将扩散算子映射到负分数阶空间，以统一处理trace-class与时空白噪声；将噪声正则性参数β∈(0,2)与初值正则性参数μ∈(0,2]解耦，进行尖锐正则性估计分析。

Result: 获得了尖锐正则性估计，将初值正则性的精确损失隔离为可积的时间奇异项；在远离t=0处建立了尖锐强收敛率O(h^{β−ε}+k^{½min{β−ε,1}})，ε>0；并通过修正Langmuir分数阶表面覆盖模型与抛物Anderson模型的数值实验验证了理论收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的负分数阶空间框架与参数解耦分析有效解决了非光滑初值、乘性噪声下SPDE全离散逼近的尖锐强误差估计问题，数值实验证实理论结果。

Abstract: This article establishes sharp strong error estimates for the fully discrete approximation of semilinear parabolic stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) driven by multiplicative noise and subject to nonsmooth initial data. The spatial discretization is based on a standard finite element method, coupled with the linearly implicit Euler scheme in time. By mapping the diffusion operator into negative fractional spaces, our framework accommodates both trace-class and space-time white noise. For nonsmooth initial data, by decoupling the noise regularity parameter $β\in (0,2)$ from the initial data regularity parameter $μ\in (0,2]$, we derive sharp regularity estimates that isolate the exact loss of initial regularity into an integrable temporal singularity. Furthermore, we establish sharp strong convergence rates of $O(h^{β-\varepsilon} + k^{\frac{1}{2}\min\{β-\varepsilon, 1\}})$ for $\varepsilon>0$ away from $t = 0$. Finally, we consider physically relevant stochastic models, such as the modified Langmuir fractional surface coverage model and the parabolic Anderson model, in our numerical experiments to confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [126] [A parameterized block LU preconditioning for double saddle point problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12615)
*Hamed Aslani,Mehdi Najafi Kalyani,Davod Khojasteh Salkuyeh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种双参数预条件技术，用于求解大型稀疏非对称非奇异双鞍点问题，并验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型稀疏非对称非奇异双鞍点问题在科学计算与工程中广泛存在，需要高效且稳定的求解方法，现有预条件技术仍有改进空间。

Method: 基于平稳迭代法构造预条件子，并利用系数矩阵的双参数化块LU分解；推导迭代法的收敛性结果，分析预条件后矩阵的谱特征，并将其与GMRES方法结合使用。

Result: 给出了迭代法的收敛性结论及预条件矩阵的谱分布特征，数值实验表明所提预条件子性能令人满意。

Conclusion: 所提出的双参数预条件子与GMRES结合可有效求解双鞍点问题，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a biparametric preconditioning technique for large, sparse, non-symmetric, and non-singular double saddle point problems. The preconditioner is induced using a stationary iteration method. It is also based on a two-parameterized block LU factorization of the coefficient matrix. To begin with, some convergence results of the iteration method are derived. Moreover, the spectral features exhibited by the preconditioned matrix are given. Finally, the presented preconditioner is conjuncted with the GMRES method. Numerical results demonstrate the satisfactory performance of the preconditioner.

</details>


### [127] [An Optimal IPDG Scheme for the Biharmonic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12675)
*Bohua Zhang,Xia Ji,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对平面双调和方程提出一种最优内罚间断Galerkin(IPDG)格式，取分片多项式次数 k=3 或 4，通过仅强制顶点连续性并将函数及其法向导数的跳跃分别投影到 P^{k-3} 与 P^{k-2}，使罚参数趋于无穷时离散解落入最优非协调有限元空间 B_h^k，从根本上消除数值锁定，并获得最优误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 IPDG 方法中，大罚参数会迫使离散解进入过度约束的空间，导致精度严重退化，即数值锁定现象。本文旨在设计一种无锁定的最优 IPDG 格式，使大罚参数下仍保持最优收敛精度。

Method: 针对平面双调和方程，采用 k=3 或 4 的分片多项式构造 IPDG 格式；仅强制顶点连续性，并将函数跳跃与其法向导数跳跃分别投影到 P^{k-3} 和 P^{k-2} 空间。分析罚参数趋于无穷时离散解所趋向的约束子空间 V_{h,∞}^k，并证明其恰为最优非协调有限元空间 B_h^k。

Result: 证明了网格依赖能量范数下 O(h^{k-1}) 的最优误差估计以及 L^2 范数下 O(h^{k+1}) 的最优误差估计；在凸区域与 L 形区域上的数值实验表明，即使罚参数极大，该格式依然稳健且完全无锁定。

Conclusion: 所提 IPDG 格式通过约束子空间 V_{h,∞}^k 与最优非协调有限元空间 B_h^k 的内在联系，从根本上消除了数值锁定，是双调和方程的一种最优且无锁定的间断Galerkin方法。

Abstract: This paper presents an optimal interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin (IPDG) scheme for the planar biharmonic equation using piecewise polynomials of degree $k=3$ or $4$. In standard IPDG methods, large penalty parameters force the discrete solution into an overconstrained space, severely degrading accuracy---phenomenon known as numerical locking. To overcome this, our method enforces only vertex continuity and projects the jumps of the function and its normal derivative onto $\mathcal{P}^{k-3}$ and $\mathcal{P}^{k-2}$, respectively. Consequently, as the penalty parameters tend to infinity, the discrete solution is forced to lie in a constrained subspace $V_{h,\infty}^k$, which we identify as the optimal nonconforming finite element space $B_h^k$. This intrinsic connection fundamentally eliminates numerical locking. We prove optimal error estimates of $\mathcal{O}(h^{k-1})$ in a mesh-dependent energy norm and $\mathcal{O}(h^{k+1})$ in the $L^2$ norm. Numerical experiments on both convex and L-shaped domains confirm that the proposed scheme is robust and entirely locking-free, even for extremely large penalty parameters.

</details>


### [128] [Total Generalized Variation for Inverse Problems Involving Piecewise Linear Finite Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12778)
*Moritz Kappes,Manuel Haas,Thomas Beiert,Simone Pezzuto,Alexander Effland*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了二阶总广义变分(TGV)泛函的分片线性有限元离散的收敛性，并将其推广到时空情形。两种情形下离散极小元均以速率 $h^{1/4}$ 收敛到连续极小元，与TV有限元离散的最佳已知速率一致。数值实验验证了收敛速率并显示实际收敛更快；时空情形在electrocardiographic imaging反问题中改进了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶正则化（如二阶TGV）相比总变分等一阶方法能给出更好的结果，但其有限元离散的收敛性尚缺乏理论保证，尤其在时空问题中，需要建立与TV相当的收敛速率并拓展到时空反问题。

Method: 采用分片线性有限元对二阶总广义变分泛函进行离散化；系统引入时空函数空间，将该收敛性结果推广到时空情形；结合电生理成像反问题构建时空正则化模型。

Result: 证明了空间及时空两种情形下离散极小元以速率 $h^{1/4}$ 收敛到连续极小元，与TV有限元离散在该一般设定下的最佳已知速率一致；数值实验验证了理论速率并表明实际收敛更快；与相关空间图像重建算法重建效果相当；在电生理成像反问题中该泛函改进了现有最优结果。

Conclusion: 为二阶TGV的分片线性有限元离散提供了收敛性理论，并成功扩展到时空设定；在保持与TV相同收敛速率的同时，提高了电生理成像反问题的求解性能。

Abstract: Higher-order regularization has shown improved results over first-order methods such as total variation. In this paper, convergence of a piecewise linear finite element discretization is shown for the second-order total generalized variation functional. Additionally, this convergence result is extended to a spatiotemporal setting after the spatiotemporal function space is comprehensively introduced. In both cases, discrete minimizers converge to the continuous minimizer with rate $h^{1/4}$, matching the best known rate for finite element discretizations of total variation in the same general setting. Numerical experiments confirm the convergence rates and indicate faster convergence in practice. Comparisons with related spatial image reconstruction algorithms show comparable reconstruction results. The spatiotemporal case is discussed using the example of the inverse problem in electrocardiographic imaging, where the proposed functional improves the state of the art.

</details>


### [129] [A stabilized, nodal strain finite element method for small and large deformations: formulation, analysis, and impact on remeshing strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12893)
*Nestor Rossi,Ignacio Romero*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于节点应变有限元的变分一致稳定格式，用于小应变与有限应变力学问题的离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点应变方法在频繁重网格化和非弹性材料问题中表现良好，但其变分基础与稳定项的作用尚需系统阐明。

Method: 证明这些方法源于对偶变量采用不同网格的混合变分原理；将节点应变有限元写成带假定应变算子的纯原始格式，指出稳定项的必要性，并提出适用于有限应变的新一类通用稳定函数。

Result: 数值算例验证了相关论断，表明该格式可视为纯粒子方法，所有运动学与材料信息均存储于节点。

Conclusion: 该视角解释了节点应变方法在频繁重网格化和非弹性材料问题中的优势，并可最大限度减少内变量的扩散。

Abstract: We present in this article the discretization of small and finite strain mechanics using
  variationally consistent, stabilized, nodal strain finite element formulations. We prove that
  these methods derive from a mixed variational principle that uses different meshes for the primal
  and dual variables. We demonstrate that, for both mechanical problems, nodal strain finite
  elements can be written as pure primal formulations with assumed strain operators. The
  stabilization terms are shown to be necessary and a new, general class of stabilizing functions is
  proposed for the finite strain regime. The ensuing formulations can be interpreted as pure
  particle methods because all kinematic and material information is stored at the nodes. This point
  of view explains the favorable properties of nodal strain methods when employed in problems that
  require frequent remeshing and involve inelastic materials. In these cases, the diffusion of
  internal variables is kept to a minimum. The numerical examples presented validate the claims.

</details>


### [130] [A Unified Two-Grid Framework for Anderson Acceleration and Nonlinear GMRES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13091)
*James Adler,Yunhui He,Xiaozhe Hu,Satchel Lefebvre*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将 Anderson 加速（AA）与非线性广义最小残量法（NGMRES）重新表述为两网格方法，通过显式推导其在线性问题上的误差传播矩阵，统一了二者的算法框架，并据此提出一种扩大粗空间的新变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AA 与 NGMRES 是定点迭代中广泛使用的加速方法，且各自存在多种变体，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们之间的联系；同时标准表述掩盖了某些可改进的设计空间。

Method: 通过在线性问题中显式推导 AA 与 NGMRES 的误差传播矩阵，将二者及其若干现有变体重新表述为两网格方法：包含单步前/后平滑以及基于合适内积投影的粗网格校正；并利用该框架独立调整粗空间与平滑步等组件，提出将最近更新残量纳入粗空间的新变体。

Result: 新变体在 SPD Poisson 问题与非 SPD 对流扩散问题上通常优于 AA、NGMRES 及其现有变体。

Conclusion: 两网格重构不仅统一了 AA、NGMRES 及其变体，还揭示了标准表述中隐含的设计自由度，从而能够系统性地设计新算法。

Abstract: In this work, we recast two widely used acceleration methods for fixed-point iterations, Anderson acceleration (AA) and the nonlinear generalized minimal residual method (NGMRES), as two-grid methods. By explicitly deriving the error propagation matrices for AA and NGMRES on linear problems, we show that both methods, together with several of their existing variants, can be reformulated as two-grid methods with a single pre- or postsmoothing step and a coarse-grid correction given by a projection with respect to a suitable inner product. This reformulation not only unifies AA, NGMRES, and their variants within a single algorithmic framework, but also enables the design of new variants by independently adjusting the components of the two-grid method, such as the coarse space and the smoothing steps. In particular, we propose a new variant that enlarges the coarse space by incorporating the most recently updated residual, an improvement that is naturally revealed by the two-grid formulation but is hidden in the standard formulations of the AA and NGMRES algorithms. Numerical experiments on both an SPD Poisson problem and a family of non-SPD convection-diffusion problems show that this new variant often outperforms both AA and NGMRES, as well as their variants.

</details>
